📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • LFIA 与 qPCR 的一致性因标记物和样本类型而异,血液中 HIV 和 HCV 抗体检测的一致性较高,而肝脏和脾脏的一致性较低。在符合预定 qPCR 阳性标准的样本中,LFIA 阳性样本往往比 LFIA 阴性样本表现出更高的病毒载量,肝脏中的 HBV 达到统计学显着性。
  • 脑脊液分析证实结核性脑膜炎,胸部计算机断层扫描显示弥漫性粟粒结节,提示血行播散性结核病。血清学乙型肝炎表面抗原、e抗原和核心抗体呈阳性,表明病毒复制活跃。 [10.1186/s40249-026-01519-5]。
  • 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染与病毒和 宿主 蛋白的糖基化改变有关,这会影响病毒进入、免疫逃避和疾病进展,而 HBV 感染会重塑 宿主 糖基化。 [10.1007/s10482-026-02453-0]。
  • 研究发现,乙型肝炎病毒 家族史与 HBsAg 清除风险较低显着相关。大约 37% 的这种负相关是由基线 HBsAg 水平升高介导的。 [10.3748/wjg.127443]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • LFIA agreement with qPCR varied by marker and specimen type, with substantial agreement for HIV and HCV antibody detection in blood and lower agreement in liver and spleen. [10.1128/spectrum.01681-26].
  • Cerebrospinal fluid analysis confirmed tuberculous meningitis, and chest computed tomography revealed diffuse miliary nodules suggestive of hematogenous disseminated tuberculosis.
  • Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is associated with altered glycosylation of both viral and host proteins, which influences viral entry, immune evasion and disease progression, while HBV infection remodels host glycosylation.
  • EHF, FIB-4 achieved the highest area under the curve [AUC; 0.911, 95% confidence interval (CI): 0.844-0.969) followed by APRI (0.843, 95% CI: 0.755-0.923). [10.4274/dir.2026.264052].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 一项 II 期研究评估了 siRNA 疗法 HRS-5635 治疗慢性 HBV 感染的效果。治疗 结束后 24 周,最高剂量(400 mg)显示 19% 的患者出现 功能性治愈,HBsAg 水平低于 0.05 IU/mL。该研究已在 AASLD 年会上发表。
  • 该文章报告了美国肝移植受者中乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 率不理想的情况,正如一位传染病专家所强调的那样。这是报告的问题,而不是 暴发 或已确认的事件。 [news-f7a38f3fcdb3a134]。
  • 该报告提供了 AASLD 2026 会议关于 慢性乙型肝炎 和迟发性遗传性胆汁淤积症和 Alagille 综合征的最新信息。它包括研究摘要和专家评论,但没有报告已证实或疑似的 暴发 事件。 [news-fe306b1f0998e5fb]。
  • Precision BioSciences 公布了 ELIMINATE-B 研究的 1 期数据,显示使用 PBGENE-HBV 进行基因编辑可以消除 92% 具有可检测 pgRNA 的患者中的病毒 储存宿主 (共价闭合环状DNA),其中包括 0.4 mg/kg 剂量水平的所有 6 名可评估患者。这被认为是这种方法迄今为止最完整的人类证据。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Hepatitis B virus news developments.
  • A phase II study evaluated the siRNA therapy HRS-5635 for treating chronic HBV infection.
  • The article reports on suboptimal vaccination rates for Hepatitis B among liver transplant recipients in the United States, as highlighted by an expert in infectious disease.
  • The report provides updates from the AASLD 2026 conference on chronic hepatitis B and late-onset genetic cholestasis and Alagille syndrome.
  • Precision BioSciences presented Phase 1 data from the ELIMINATE-B study showing that gene editing using PBGENE-HBV can eliminate the viral reservoir (cccDNA) in 92% of patients with detectable pgRNA, including all six evaluable patients at the 0.4 mg/kg dose level.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of Viral Hepatitis  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/jvh.70258  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 33, 12, e70258
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

新型下一代乙型肝炎衣壳组装调节剂 ABI ‐4334 的安全性、药代动力学和 抗病毒药物 活性

The Safety, Pharmacokinetics, and Antiviral Activity of ABI ‐4334, A Novel Next‐Generation Hepatitis B Capsid Assembly Modulator
作者： Edward John Gane, Christiane Schwabe, Nelli Ghicavii, Cristina Ticot, Katie Zomorodi, Steven J. Knox, Kathryn M. Kitrinos, Ran Yan, Jie-Ming Liu, Grace Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要:需要针对 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染 (cHBV) 的新型、耐受性良好的短期疗法。ABI-4334 是一种针对 HBV 核心蛋白的下一代衣壳组装调节剂。在两项 1 期研究中检查了 ABI-4334 在健康志愿者和 cHBV 参与者中的安全性、耐受性和药代动力学,以及 抗病毒药物 对 cHBV 参与者的影响。研究101是一项针对健康志愿者的安慰剂对照研究,包括四名单次递增剂量队列研究和两项每日一次多次递增剂量队列研究随机6:2(ABI-4334:安慰剂),以及一项交叉食物效应队列研究随机3:3(进食:禁食)。研究 102 是一项随机、盲法、安慰剂对照研究,其中 cHBV 参与者按 8:2 随机分配(ABI-4334 [2 剂量 队列研究]:安慰剂)。总体而言,研究 101 和研究 102 分别纳入了 54 名健康志愿者和 20 名患有 cHBV 的参与者。研究 102 中的大多数参与者 (75%) 乙型肝炎 e 抗原 (HBeAg) 阴性,并且基线 HBV DNA 和前基因组 RNA (pgRNA) 水平普遍较低。ABI-4334 耐受性良好,没有出现 治疗 突发严重不良事件 (AE) 或 治疗 突发 AE 导致研究中止。两项研究中不同剂量的 ABI-4334 平均血浆半衰期范围相当。cHBV 参与者多剂量给药后,稳态平均血浆半衰期为 17.5 至 27.5 小时。在不同剂量的 HBeAg 阳性 (n = 3) 和阴性 (n = 13) 参与者中均观察到 HBV DNA 下降。在基线时可检测到 pgRNA 的参与者中观察到 pgRNA 减少。ABI-4334 耐受性良好,药代动力学和 抗病毒药物 结果支持 ABI-4334 作为 cHBV 单一疗法或新型联合疗法的组成部分的持续开发。试验注册:研究 101 (NCT05569941)。研究 102 (NCT06384131)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于目前的 治疗 存在局限性,因此需要一种新型、耐受性良好的短期 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染 (cHBV) 治疗方法。
设计与对象
进行了两项 1 期研究:研究 101 涉及健康志愿者,接受四名单次递增剂量 队列研究 和两次每日一次多次递增剂量 队列研究,而研究 102 涉及 cHBV 随机 8:2 受试者(ABI-4334 [2 剂量 队列研究]:安慰剂)。总体而言,研究 101 和研究 102 分别纳入了 54 名健康志愿者和 20 名患有 cHBV 的参与者。
核心方法
ABI-4334 的安全性、耐受性和药代动力学在两项 1 期研究中进行了检查。在研究101中,使用单次和多次递增剂量队列研究的安慰剂对照设计,在研究102中,实施随机、盲法、安慰剂对照设计。
主要结果
在不同剂量的 HBeAg 阳性 (n = 3) 和阴性 (n = 13) 参与者中均观察到 HBV DNA 下降。此外,基线时可检测到 pgRNA 的参与者的 pgRNA 有所减少。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,ABI-4334 具有良好的耐受性,并且在 cHBV 参与者中表现出 抗病毒药物 活性,支持其作为单一疗法或联合疗法的组成部分的开发。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为了解 ABI-4334 作为治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的新治疗选择的潜力提供了重要见解,这可以为未来的临床试验和 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
The studies were Phase 1 trials with limited sample sizes, which may restrict the generalizability of the findings. However, the evidence is strong given the consistent pharmacokinetic profiles and antiviral activity observed.

The Safety, Pharmacokinetics, and Antiviral Activity of ABI ‐4334, A Novel Next‐Generation Hepatitis B Capsid Assembly Modulator

Authors: Edward John Gane, Christiane Schwabe, Nelli Ghicavii, Cristina Ticot, Katie Zomorodi, Steven J. Knox, Kathryn M. Kitrinos, Ran Yan, Jie-Ming Liu, Grace Wang
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Novel, well‐tolerated, short‐term therapeutics for chronic hepatitis B virus infection (cHBV) are needed. ABI‐4334 is a next‐generation capsid assembly modulator that targets the HBV core protein. The safety, tolerability, and pharmacokinetics of ABI‐4334 in healthy volunteers and participants with cHBV and antiviral effects in participants with cHBV were examined in two Phase 1 studies. Study 101 was a placebo‐controlled study of healthy volunteers that included four single‐ascending dose cohorts and two once‐daily multiple‐ascending dose cohorts randomized 6:2 (ABI‐4334:placebo), and one crossover food‐effect cohort randomized 3:3 (fed:fasted). Study 102 was a randomized, blinded, placebo‐controlled study where participants with cHBV were randomized 8:2 (ABI‐4334 [2 dose cohorts]:placebo). Overall, 54 healthy volunteers and 20 participants with cHBV were included in Studies 101 and 102, respectively. Most participants (75%) in Study 102 were hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg) negative and generally had lower baseline HBV DNA and pregenomic RNA (pgRNA) levels. ABI‐4334 was well tolerated, with no treatment‐emergent serious adverse events (AEs) or treatment‐emergent AEs leading to study discontinuation. The mean ABI‐4334 plasma half‐life ranges were comparable across doses in both studies. Following multiple‐dose administration in participants with cHBV, the steady‐state mean plasma half‐life ranged from 17.5 to 27.5 h. HBV DNA declines were observed in both HBeAg‐positive ( n = 3) and ‐negative ( n = 13) participants across doses. Reductions in pgRNA were observed in participants with detectable pgRNA at baseline. ABI‐4334 was well tolerated, with pharmacokinetics and antiviral results supporting the continued development of ABI‐4334 as a monotherapy or component of novel combination regimens for cHBV. Trial Registration: Study 101 (NCT05569941). Study 102 (NCT06384131)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A novel, well‐tolerated, short‐term therapeutic for chronic hepatitis B virus infection (cHBV) is needed, as current treatments have limitations.
Design and population
Two Phase 1 studies were conducted: Study 101 involved healthy volunteers with four single‐ascending dose cohorts and two once‐daily multiple‐ascending dose cohorts, while Study 102 involved participants with cHBV randomized 8:2 (ABI‐4334 [2 dose cohorts]:placebo). Overall, 54 healthy volunteers and 20 participants with cHBV were included in Studies 101 and 102, respectively.
Core methods
The safety, tolerability, and pharmacokinetics of ABI‐4334 were examined in two Phase 1 studies. In Study 101, a placebo‐controlled design with single‐ and multiple‐ascending dose cohorts was used, and in Study 102, a randomized, blinded, placebo‐controlled design was implemented.
Main results
HBV DNA declines were observed in both HBeAg‐positive (n = 3) and ‐negative (n = 13) participants across doses. Additionally, reductions in pgRNA were noted in participants with detectable pgRNA at baseline.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that ABI‐4334 is well tolerated and demonstrate antiviral activity in participants with cHBV, supporting its development as a monotherapy or component of combination regimens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide important insights into the potential of ABI‐4334 as a new therapeutic option for managing chronic hepatitis B virus infection, which could inform future clinical trials and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The studies were Phase 1 trials with limited sample sizes, which may restrict the generalizability of the findings. However, the evidence is strong given the consistent pharmacokinetic profiles and antiviral activity observed.

妊娠晚期危重孕妇播散性结核病合并结核性脑膜炎和活动性乙型肝炎的多学科治疗:病例报告

Multidisciplinary management of disseminated tuberculosis with tuberculous meningitis and active hepatitis B in a critically ill pregnant woman in the third trimester: a case report
作者： Ju Zhao, Li-Ming Zhang, Yun Wang, Cheng-Jin Zhao, Guo-Dong Cheng, Guodong Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 妊娠期结核性脑膜炎(TBM)很少见,但危及生命,对母体和胎儿具有很大的风险。妊娠晚期播散性结核病累及中枢神经系统并并发活动性乙型肝炎感染的报道很少,特别是在医疗保健机会有限的偏远牧区人群中。本报告强调了这种三重合并症的诊断和治疗挑战,并强调了多学科团队 (MDT) 管理在这种复杂情况下的价值。病例介绍 一名 24 岁牧民在妊娠 30 周时出现头痛、呕吐、发烧和颈项强直。脑脊液分析证实了 TBM,胸部计算机断层扫描显示弥漫性粟粒结节,提示血行播散性结核病。血清学乙型肝炎表面抗原、e抗原和核心抗体呈阳性,表明病毒复制活跃。由于妊娠晚期出现危及生命的颅内高压[颅内压(ICP)>330 mmH 2 O],为了胎儿安全,停用渗透剂,并进行腰大池引流。组建了包括呼吸内科、产科、肝病学、药学和重症监护护理专业的 MDT。抗结核治疗加强为异烟肼、利福平、利奈唑胺和莫西沙星;由于胎儿视神经炎而停用乙胺丁醇,并加用替诺福韦治疗乙型肝炎。病情稳定后,患者在妊娠 35 周时被转移到更高级别的机构,并在 36 周时接受剖腹产手术,产下一名活产早产女婴(2320 克,1 分钟阿普加评分为 8)。母亲和新生儿均于 2025 年 12 月 1 日出院,无并发症。结论 该病例表明,早期识别、个体化抗结核治疗方案和协调的多学科合作对于播散性结核病合并 TBM 并并发乙型肝炎感染的妊娠患者取得良好结局至关重要。成功的管理依赖于综合重症监护和多学科方法,即使在资源有限的牧区环境中,这种方法也被证明是有效的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本 病例报告 解决了在危重孕妇中诊断和治疗伴有结核性脑膜炎和活动性乙型肝炎的播散性结核病的挑战,强调了在临床实践中认识到这种罕见且复杂的合并症的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究是一名24岁妊娠晚期孕妇的病例报告,她出现头痛、呕吐、发烧和颈项强直,被诊断为累及中枢神经系统的播散性结核病并并发活动性乙型肝炎感染。
核心方法
诊断过程包括脑脊液分析和胸部计算机断层扫描,以确认结核性脑膜炎和播散性结核病。血清学 测试用于识别活动性乙型肝炎感染,治疗 涉及强化抗结核治疗方案,并针对胎儿安全进行调整。
主要结果
脑脊液分析证实结核性脑膜炎,胸部计算机断层扫描显示弥漫性粟粒结节,提示血行播散性结核病。血清学乙型肝炎表面抗原、e抗原和核心抗体呈阳性,表明病毒复制活跃。
解释与创新
作者解释说,早期识别和个性化的 治疗 方案以及协调的多学科合作对于管理此类复杂病例至关重要。该病例增加了关于结核病、乙型肝炎和妊娠交叉的有限文献。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,多学科方法在管理孕妇罕见和复杂感染方面的重要性,特别是在资源有限的环境中,此类合并症可能未被充分认识。
局限与证据强度
该研究是单一的病例报告,这限制了其普遍性。然而,考虑到所提供的详细临床和诊断信息,所提出的具体病例的证据是强有力的。

Multidisciplinary management of disseminated tuberculosis with tuberculous meningitis and active hepatitis B in a critically ill pregnant woman in the third trimester: a case report

Authors: Ju Zhao, Li-Ming Zhang, Yun Wang, Cheng-Jin Zhao, Guo-Dong Cheng, Guodong Chen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Tuberculous meningitis (TBM) in pregnancy is rare but life-threatening, with substantial maternal and fetal risks. Third-trimester disseminated tuberculosis involving the central nervous system with concurrent active hepatitis B infection has scarcely been reported, particularly among remote pastoral populations with limited healthcare access. This report highlights the diagnostic and therapeutic challenges of this triple comorbidity and underscores the value of multidisciplinary team (MDT) management in such complex scenarios. Case presentation A 24-year-old pastoralist at 30 weeks of gestation presented with headache, vomiting, fever, and nuchal rigidity. Cerebrospinal fluidanalysis confirmed TBM, and chest computed tomography revealed diffuse miliary nodules suggestive of hematogenous disseminated tuberculosis. Serology was positive for hepatitis B surface antigen, e-antigen, and core antibody, indicating active viral replication. Given life-threatening intracranial hypertension [intracranial pressure (ICP) > 330 mmH 2 O] in late pregnancy, osmotic agents were withheld for fetal safety, and lumbar cisternal drainage was performed. An MDT comprising respiratory medicine, obstetrics, hepatology, pharmacy, and critical care nursing specialties was assembled. Anti-tuberculosis therapy was intensified to isoniazid, rifampicin, linezolid, and moxifloxacin; ethambutol was discontinued due to fetal optic neuritis, and tenofovir was added for hepatitis B management. Following stabilization, the patient was transferred to a higher-level facility at 35 weeks of gestation and underwent cesarean section at 36 weeks, delivering a live preterm female infant (2320 g, 1-min Apgar score of 8). Both mother and neonate were discharged without complications on December 1, 2025. Conclusions This case demonstrates that early recognition, individualized anti-tuberculosis regimens, and coordinated multidisciplinary collaboration are critical for achieving favorable outcomes in pregnant patients with disseminated tuberculosis complicated by TBM and concurrent hepatitis B infection. The successful management relied on integrated critical care and a multidisciplinary approach, which proved effective even in resource-limited pastoral settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This case report addresses the challenges of diagnosing and managing disseminated tuberculosis with tuberculous meningitis and active hepatitis B in a critically ill pregnant woman, highlighting the need for awareness of such rare and complex comorbidities in clinical practice.
Design and population
The study is a case report of a 24-year-old pregnant woman in the third trimester who presented with headache, vomiting, fever, and nuchal rigidity, and was diagnosed with disseminated tuberculosis involving the central nervous system and concurrent active hepatitis B infection.
Core methods
The diagnostic process included cerebrospinal fluid analysis and chest computed tomography to confirm tuberculous meningitis and disseminated tuberculosis. Serology tests were used to identify active hepatitis B infection, and the treatment involved an intensified anti-tuberculosis regimen with adjustments for fetal safety.
Main results
Cerebrospinal fluid analysis confirmed tuberculous meningitis, and chest computed tomography revealed diffuse miliary nodules suggestive of hematogenous disseminated tuberculosis. Serology was positive for hepatitis B surface antigen, e-antigen, and core antibody, indicating active viral replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that early recognition and individualized treatment regimens, along with coordinated multidisciplinary collaboration, are essential for managing such complex cases. This case adds to the limited literature on the intersection of tuberculosis, hepatitis B, and pregnancy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of multidisciplinary approaches in managing rare and complex infections in pregnant women, particularly in resource-limited settings where such comorbidities may be under-recognized.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a single case report, which limits its generalizability. However, the evidence is strong for the specific case presented, given the detailed clinical and diagnostic information provided.

高流行、脆弱环境中青少年与乙型肝炎相关的知识、态度、实践和 疫苗接种 差距:来自索马里摩加迪沙的一项横断面研究。

Knowledge, attitudes, practices, and vaccination gaps related to hepatitis B among adolescents in a hyperendemic, fragile setting: A cross-sectional study from Mogadishu, Somalia.
作者： Ahmed Hassan Said, Ahmed Saed Hussein, Ahmed Nor Osman, Mubarak Hassan Mohamud, Abdikafi Ahmed Omar, Abdirahman Mohamed Abdullahi, Nafisa Mohamed Abubakar, Yusra Omar Ibrahim, Ahmed Abdukadir Elmi
摘要中文翻译背景乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区的一个主要公共卫生问题,但索马里青少年对 HBV 的知识、态度和做法 (KAP) 的证据有限。本研究评估了摩加迪沙高中生的 HBV 相关 KAP 及相关因素。目的 评估索马里摩加迪沙高中生的 HBV 相关知识、态度、实践和 疫苗接种 状况,并确定相关因素。方法 采用结构化问卷对 392 名高中生进行横断面研究。描述性统计总结了 KAP 水平。使用卡方检验和逻辑回归来检查社会人口统计学变量与 KAP 结果之间的关联。结果尽管 98.0% 的学生听说过 HBV,但 63.0% 的参与者表现出对乙型肝炎有良好的了解,而 37.0% 的学生对此了解甚少。27.0% 的参与者对 HBV 持积极态度,73.0% 的参与者持消极态度。超过一半的受访者 (52.5%) 报告了良好的预防措施,而 47.5% 的受访者报告了不良的预防措施。HBV 疫苗接种 覆盖率为 39.3%。在多变量分析中,年级水平与 KAP 领域显着相关,尽管关联模式因知识、态度和实践而异。结论摩加迪沙的高中生在 HBV 知识、消极态度和 疫苗接种 吸收率方面表现出巨大差距。教育年级水平是 HBV 相关 KAP 的关键决定因素,强调需要采取有针对性的、以学校为基础的干预措施,以加强青少年 HBV 预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区的一个主要公共卫生问题,但索马里青少年对 HBV 的知识、态度和做法 (KAP) 的证据有限。本研究旨在通过评估摩加迪沙高中生中与 HBV 相关的 KAP 和相关因素来解决这一差距。
设计与对象
对索马里摩加迪沙的 392 名高中生进行了一项横断面研究,以评估 HBV 相关知识、态度、实践和 疫苗接种 状况。该研究的重点是处于高流行、脆弱环境中的青少年。
核心方法
描述性统计总结了 KAP 水平,并使用卡方检验和逻辑回归检查了社会人口统计学变量与 KAP 结果之间的关联。该研究使用结构化问卷来收集数据。
主要结果
尽管98.0%的学生听说过乙型肝炎病毒,但63.0%的学生表现出良好的知识,而37.0%的学生则知识较差。27.0% 的参与者对 HBV 持积极态度,73.0% 的参与者持消极态度。超过一半的受访者 (52.5%) 报告了良好的预防措施,而 47.5% 的受访者报告了不良的预防措施。HBV 疫苗接种 覆盖率为 39.3%。
解释与创新
尽管关联模式因知识、态度和实践而异,但年级水平与 KAP 领域显着相关。这凸显了需要采取有针对性的、以学校为基础的干预措施,以加强青少年乙型肝炎的预防。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了解决索马里青少年对乙型肝炎的知识差距和消极态度的重要性,这可以为制定更有效的学校预防策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。然而,由于使用了统计分析和高流行环境中高中生的代表性样本,证据是强有力的。

Knowledge, attitudes, practices, and vaccination gaps related to hepatitis B among adolescents in a hyperendemic, fragile setting: A cross-sectional study from Mogadishu, Somalia.

Authors: Ahmed Hassan Said, Ahmed Saed Hussein, Ahmed Nor Osman, Mubarak Hassan Mohamud, Abdikafi Ahmed Omar, Abdirahman Mohamed Abdullahi, Nafisa Mohamed Abubakar, Yusra Omar Ibrahim, Ahmed Abdukadir Elmi
Original abstract (English)
BackgroundHepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health problem in sub-Saharan Africa, yet evidence on adolescents' knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) toward HBV is limited in Somalia. This study assessed HBV-related KAP and associated factors among high school students in Mogadishu.ObjectivesTo assess HBV-related knowledge, attitudes, practices, and vaccination status among high school students in Mogadishu, Somalia, and identify associated factors.MethodsA cross-sectional study was conducted among 392 high school students using a structured questionnaire. Descriptive statistics summarized KAP levels. Associations between sociodemographic variables and KAP outcomes were examined using chi-square tests and logistic regression.ResultsAlthough 98.0% of students had heard of HBV, 63.0% of participants demonstrated good knowledge of hepatitis B, while 37.0% had poor knowledge. Positive attitudes toward HBV were reported by 27.0% of participants, whereas 73.0% had negative attitudes. Just over half of respondents (52.5%) reported good preventive practices, while 47.5% had poor practices. HBV vaccination coverage was 39.3%. In multivariable analysis, grade level was significantly associated with the KAP domains, although the pattern of association varied across knowledge, attitudes, and practices.ConclusionHigh school students in Mogadishu exhibited substantial gaps in HBV knowledge, negative attitudes, and low vaccination uptake. Educational grade level was a key determinant of HBV-related KAP, highlighting the need for targeted, school-based interventions to strengthen adolescent HBV prevention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health problem in sub-Saharan Africa, yet evidence on adolescents' knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) toward HBV is limited in Somalia. This study aimed to address this gap by assessing HBV-related KAP and associated factors among high school students in Mogadishu.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 392 high school students in Mogadishu, Somalia, to assess HBV-related knowledge, attitudes, practices, and vaccination status. The study focused on adolescents in a hyperendemic, fragile setting.
Core methods
Descriptive statistics summarized KAP levels, and associations between sociodemographic variables and KAP outcomes were examined using chi-square tests and logistic regression. The study used a structured questionnaire to collect data.
Main results
Although 98.0% of students had heard of HBV, 63.0% demonstrated good knowledge, while 37.0% had poor knowledge. Positive attitudes toward HBV were reported by 27.0% of participants, whereas 73.0% had negative attitudes. Just over half of respondents (52.5%) reported good preventive practices, while 47.5% had poor practices. HBV vaccination coverage was 39.3%.
Interpretation and novelty
Grade level was significantly associated with the KAP domains, although the pattern of association varied across knowledge, attitudes, and practices. This highlights the need for targeted, school-based interventions to strengthen adolescent HBV prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of addressing knowledge gaps and negative attitudes toward HBV among adolescents in Somalia, which could inform the development of more effective school-based prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. However, the evidence is strong due to the use of statistical analyses and a representative sample of high school students in a hyperendemic setting.

对“阿尔伯塔省 慢性乙型肝炎 和丁型肝炎检测的地理空间差异:基于人群的空间分析”的更正

Correction to ‘Geospatial Discrepancy of Chronic Hepatitis B and Hepatitis D Testing in Alberta: A Population‐Based Spatial Analysis’
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译Shaheen,“阿尔伯塔省 慢性乙型肝炎 和丁型肝炎检测的地理空间差异:基于人群的空间分析”,《病毒性肝炎杂志》33,第 1 期。9(2026):e70223,https://doi.org/10.1111/jvh.70223。为了响应更高分辨率的图的要求,图 2 和图 3 无意中互换了。图 2 错误地显示了 慢性乙型肝炎 患病率 的热点分析,而不是抗 HDV 测试,图 3 错误地显示了抗 HDV 测试的热点分析,而不是抗 HDV 阳性。下面提供了正确的图 2 和图 3。图 1 是正确的并且保持不变。对于这个错误,我们深表歉意。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决阿尔伯塔省 慢性乙型肝炎 和丁型肝炎检测的地理空间差异,强调需要了解诊断实践的区域差异及其对公共卫生的影响。
设计与对象
该研究是在艾伯塔省进行的一项基于人群的空间分析,重点关注 慢性乙型肝炎 和丁型肝炎检测在不同地理区域的分布情况。
核心方法
该研究涉及与 慢性乙型肝炎 和丁型肝炎检测相关的地理空间数据分析,由于图 2 和图 3 无意中互换,因此对数据进行了更正。
主要结果
主要结果表明,图2和图3标注错误,图2显示的是慢性乙型肝炎 患病率的热点分析,图3显示的是抗HDV检测而非抗HDV阳性的热点分析。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为对先前发表的数据的修正,强调了肝炎检测空间分析中准确数据表示的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于提高公共卫生领域地理空间数据的准确性具有重要意义,确保乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎检测的针对性干预措施获得可靠的信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据受到数字修正的限制,这可能会影响空间模式的解释,并且研究结果的普遍性仅限于艾伯塔省人口。

Correction to ‘Geospatial Discrepancy of Chronic Hepatitis B and Hepatitis D Testing in Alberta: A Population‐Based Spatial Analysis’

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
Shaheen, “ Geospatial Discrepancy of Chronic Hepatitis B and Hepatitis D Testing in Alberta: A Population-Based Spatial Analysis,” Journal of Viral Hepatitis 33, no. 9 (2026): e70223, https://doi.org/10.1111/jvh.70223. Figures 2 and 3 were inadvertently interchanged in response to a request for higher-resolution figures. Figure 2 incorrectly shows a hotspot analysis of chronic hepatitis B prevalence rather than anti-HDV testing, and Figure 3 incorrectly shows a hotspot analysis of anti-HDV testing rather than anti-HDV positivity. The correct Figures 2 and 3 are provided below. Figure 1 is correct and remains unchanged. We apologize for this error.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the geospatial discrepancy in testing for chronic hepatitis B and hepatitis D in Alberta, highlighting the need to understand regional variations in diagnostic practices and their implications for public health.
Design and population
The study is a population-based spatial analysis conducted in Alberta, focusing on the distribution of testing for chronic hepatitis B and hepatitis D across different geographic areas.
Core methods
The study involved the analysis of geospatial data related to testing for chronic hepatitis B and hepatitis D, with figures being corrected due to an inadvertent interchange of figures 2 and 3.
Main results
The main results indicated that figures 2 and 3 were incorrectly labeled, with figure 2 showing a hotspot analysis of chronic hepatitis B prevalence and figure 3 showing a hotspot analysis of anti-HDV testing rather than anti-HDV positivity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a correction to the previously published figures, emphasizing the importance of accurate data representation in spatial analyses of hepatitis testing.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for improving the accuracy of geospatial data in public health surveillance, ensuring reliable information for targeted interventions in hepatitis B and D testing.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by the correction of figures, which may affect the interpretation of spatial patterns, and the generalizability of the findings is constrained to the Alberta population.

分段右叶 IVIM-DWI 指标与 GPR 的融合,用于 慢性乙型肝炎 相关肝纤维化的无创评估:一项试点研究

Fusion of segmental right-lobe IVIM-DWI metrics and GPR for non-invasive assessment of chronic hepatitis B–related hepatic fibrosis: a pilot study
作者： Wan-Chun Zhang, Jia-Hui Tang, Se Peng, Tian-Zhu Liu, Jun Chen, Le-Sheng Huang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
分段内体素不相干运动扩散加权成像 (IVIM-DWI)/γ-谷氨酰转肽酶与血小板比率 (GPR) 融合模型用于 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 纤维化(对照与晚期)的无创评估,并与天冬氨酸转氨酶与血小板比率指数 (APRI) 和纤维化-4 (FIB-4) 进行比较。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究包括 139 名参与者:42 名健康对照 (HC)、46 名早期肝纤维化 (EHF) 和 51 名晚期肝纤维化 (AHF)。
核心方法
IVIM-DWI 参数(表观扩散系数、慢扩散成分、快扩散成分和灌注分数)是从右肝叶(段 V-VIII)的分段分析中提取的。GPR、APRI 和 FIB-4 是根据实验室值计算的。IVIM + GPR 融合模型是使用逻辑回归开发的。使用接收器操作特征分析和引导程序内部验证来评估模型性能。
主要结果
EHF, FIB-4 achieved the highest area under the curve [AUC; 0.911, 95% confidence interval (CI): 0.844-0.969) followed by APRI (0.843, 95% CI: 0.755-0.923). The IVIM + GPR fusion model (AUC = 0.745, 95% CI: 0.643-0.847) outperformed IVIM alone (AUC = 0.652) and GPR alone (AUC = 0.730) but did not outperform APRI or FIB-4. For AHF, the IVIM + GPR fusion model (AUC = 0.698) was comparable to APRI (0.573) and FIB-4 (0.637), with no significant differences among methods.
解释与创新
IVIM + GPR 融合模型可以在腹部 MRI 期间或当血清标志物被肝外因素混淆时提供补充的机会性信息,但它应该保持互补,等待在更大的 队列研究 中进行验证。
科研与公卫意义
融合模型最多应被视为腹部磁共振成像 (MRI) 期间的补充性机会辅助手段,而不是 FIB-4/APRI 的替代品。FIB-4 和 APRI 仍然是可用实验室面板的一线。
局限与证据强度
在缺乏活检证实的 S0 CHB 组的情况下,HC 与 EHF 区分 HC 和已确定的纤维化,而不是检测发生的纤维化。所提供的证据并未报告确诊乙肝病毒感染的参与者的确切人数。

Fusion of segmental right-lobe IVIM-DWI metrics and GPR for non-invasive assessment of chronic hepatitis B–related hepatic fibrosis: a pilot study

Authors: Wan-Chun Zhang, Jia-Hui Tang, Se Peng, Tian-Zhu Liu, Jun Chen, Le-Sheng Huang
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: A segmental intravoxel incoherent motion diffusion-weighted imaging (IVIM-DWI)/gamma-glutamyl transpeptidase-to-platelet ratio (GPR) fusion model was evaluated for the non-invasive assessment of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) fibrosis (controls vs. advanced) and compared with the aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index (APRI) and Fibrosis-4 (FIB-4). METHODS: This retrospective study included 139 participants: 42 healthy controls (HCs), 46 with early hepatic fibrosis (EHF), and 51 with advanced hepatic fibrosis (AHF). IVIM-DWI parameters (apparent diffusion coefficient, slow diffusion component, fast diffusion component, and perfusion fraction) were extracted from segmental analysis of the right hepatic lobe (segments V-VIII). The GPR, APRI, and FIB-4 were calculated from laboratory values. The IVIM + GPR fusion model was developed using logistic regression. Model performance was assessed using receiver operating characteristic analysis with bootstrap internal validation. RESULTS: For HCs vs. EHF, FIB-4 achieved the highest area under the curve [AUC; 0.911, 95% confidence interval (CI): 0.844-0.969) followed by APRI (0.843, 95% CI: 0.755-0.923). The IVIM + GPR fusion model (AUC = 0.745, 95% CI: 0.643-0.847) outperformed IVIM alone (AUC = 0.652) and GPR alone (AUC = 0.730) but did not outperform APRI or FIB-4. AHF, the IVIM + GPR fusion model (AUC = 0.698) was comparable to APRI (0.573) and FIB-4 (0.637), with no significant differences among methods. CONCLUSION: FIB-4/APRI outperformed the fusion model for controls vs. early fibrosis, whereas fusion added no value over GPR for early vs. In the absence of a biopsy-proven S0 CHB group, HCs vs. EHF discriminates between HCs and established fibrosis rather than detecting incident fibrosis. The model should be considered, at most, a supplementary, opportunistic adjunct during abdominal magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) rather than a replacement for FIB-4/APRI. CLINICAL SIGNIFICANCE: The GPR uses only gamma-glutamyl transpeptidase and platelets, making it practical in resource- limited settings. The fusion model may provide supplementary, opportunistic information during abdominal MRI or when serum markers are confounded by extrahepatic factors, but it should remain complementary pending validation in larger cohorts; FIB-4 and APRI remain first-line where laboratory panels are available.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A segmental intravoxel incoherent motion diffusion-weighted imaging (IVIM-DWI)/gamma-glutamyl transpeptidase-to-platelet ratio (GPR) fusion model was evaluated for the non-invasive assessment of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) fibrosis (controls vs. advanced) and compared with the aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index (APRI) and Fibrosis-4 (FIB-4).
Design and population
This retrospective study included 139 participants: 42 healthy controls (HCs), 46 with early hepatic fibrosis (EHF), and 51 with advanced hepatic fibrosis (AHF).
Core methods
IVIM-DWI parameters (apparent diffusion coefficient, slow diffusion component, fast diffusion component, and perfusion fraction) were extracted from segmental analysis of the right hepatic lobe (segments V-VIII). The GPR, APRI, and FIB-4 were calculated from laboratory values. The IVIM + GPR fusion model was developed using logistic regression. Model performance was assessed using receiver operating characteristic analysis with bootstrap internal validation.
Main results
EHF, FIB-4 achieved the highest area under the curve [AUC; 0.911, 95% confidence interval (CI): 0.844-0.969) followed by APRI (0.843, 95% CI: 0.755-0.923). The IVIM + GPR fusion model (AUC = 0.745, 95% CI: 0.643-0.847) outperformed IVIM alone (AUC = 0.652) and GPR alone (AUC = 0.730) but did not outperform APRI or FIB-4. For AHF, the IVIM + GPR fusion model (AUC = 0.698) was comparable to APRI (0.573) and FIB-4 (0.637), with no significant differences among methods.
Interpretation and novelty
The IVIM + GPR fusion model may provide supplementary, opportunistic information during abdominal MRI or when serum markers are confounded by extrahepatic factors, but it should remain complementary pending validation in larger cohorts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The fusion model should be considered, at most, a supplementary, opportunistic adjunct during abdominal magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) rather than a replacement for FIB-4/APRI. FIB-4 and APRI remain first-line where laboratory panels are available.
Limitations and evidence strength
In the absence of a biopsy-proven S0 CHB group, HCs vs. EHF discriminates between HCs and established fibrosis rather than detecting incident fibrosis. The supplied evidence does not report the exact number of participants with confirmed HBV infection.

Journal: Journal of viral hepatitis  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/jvh.70238  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 33, 12, e70238
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

澳大利亚 治疗 患者的抗病毒 治疗 资格和 治疗 的使用情况:REACH-B 研究。

Anti-Viral Treatment Eligibility and Treatment Uptake Among People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Australia: The REACH-B Study.
作者： Behzad Hajarizadeh, Renjy Nelson, Jacob George, Josh Hanson, Jane Davies, Marianne Martinello, Tracey Brown, Ian Wong, Tasnim Hasan, Lester Mascarenhas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的适应症因临床指南而异。本研究旨在评估大量澳大利亚 队列研究 慢性乙型肝炎患者的 治疗 资格和使用情况。
设计与对象
REACH-B 研究招募了通过全国医疗服务网络管理的慢性乙型肝炎患者。参与者被分为三组:(1)接受抗病毒药物治疗;(2) 尽管符合资格但未接受治疗;(3)未经治疗且不符合资格的。在 3648 名参与者中(56% 为男性;中位年龄 48 岁;49% 出生于东北亚或东南亚;27% 为澳大利亚原住民),14% 的人 HBeAg 呈阳性,7% 的人患有肝硬化。
核心方法
治疗 资格是根据澳大利亚药品福利计划 (PBS) 标准和国际临床指南确定的。
主要结果
总体而言,47% (n = 1666) 接受 抗病毒药物 治疗,45% (n = 1573) 未接受治疗且不符合资格,7% (n = 251) 尽管根据澳大利亚 PBS 标准符合资格但未接受治疗。
解释与创新
In adjusted analysis, the likelihood of remaining untreated despite eligibility was higher among women (aOR 1.59, 95% CI 1.34, 1.88) and younger participants (age 41-60 vs. > 60 years: aOR 1.51, 95% CI 1.01-2.28; ≤ 40 vs. 41-60 years: aOR 1.85, 95% CI 1.34-2.56). The proportion untreated despite eligibility increased to 13% when applying 2024 World Health Organisation guidelines and ranged 2%-44% based on other guidelines, highlighting the variability across the guidelines.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了各指南中 治疗 资格标准的可变性,并强调了随着指南的发展监测 治疗 模式的重要性。尽管符合资格,但女性和年轻人的 治疗 摄取率较低,部分反映了女性 ALT 升高的阈值较低,以及临床医生对非 CHB 相关 ALT 升高的判断。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于自我报告的数据以及临床医生确定非 CHB 相关 ALT 升高的潜在偏差。证据是中等的,因为它基于观察数据,不包括实验或纵向随访。

Anti-Viral Treatment Eligibility and Treatment Uptake Among People With Chronic Hepatitis B in Australia: The REACH-B Study.

Authors: Behzad Hajarizadeh, Renjy Nelson, Jacob George, Josh Hanson, Jane Davies, Marianne Martinello, Tracey Brown, Ian Wong, Tasnim Hasan, Lester Mascarenhas
Original abstract (English)
Treatment indication for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) varies across clinical guidelines. This study evaluated treatment eligibility and uptake in a large Australian cohort of people with CHB. The REACH-B study recruited people with CHB managed through a national network of healthcare services. Participants were categorised into three groups: (1) receiving antiviral therapy; (2) untreated despite eligibility; (3) untreated and ineligible. Treatment eligibility was determined using Australian Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme (PBS) criteria, and by international clinical guidelines. Among 3648 participants (56% male; median age 48 years; 49% born in North-East or South-East Asia; 27% First Nations Australians), 14% were HBeAg positive and 7% had cirrhosis. Overall, 47% (n = 1666) were on antiviral therapy, 45% (n = 1573) were untreated and ineligible and 7% (n = 251) were untreated despite eligibility based on Australian PBS criteria. In adjusted analysis, the likelihood of remaining untreated despite eligibility was higher among women (aOR 1.59, 95% CI 1.34, 1.88) and younger participants (age 41-60 vs. > 60 years: aOR 1.51, 95% CI 1.01-2.28; ≤ 40 vs. > 60 years: aOR 2.65, 95% CI 1.60, 4.40). The proportion untreated despite eligibility increased to 13% when applying 2024 World Health Organisation guidelines and ranged 2%-44% based on other guidelines, highlighting the variability across the guidelines. In conclusion, among REACH-B participants, most eligible individuals receive treatment. Lower treatment uptake among women and younger people despite eligibility partly reflects lower threshold for elevated ALT in women, and clinician determination of non-CHB related ALT elevation. As treatment guidelines evolve and eligibility criteria broaden, monitoring treatment patterns is important.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Treatment indication for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) varies across clinical guidelines. This study aimed to evaluate treatment eligibility and uptake in a large Australian cohort of people with CHB.
Design and population
The REACH-B study recruited people with CHB managed through a national network of healthcare services. Participants were categorised into three groups: (1) receiving antiviral therapy; (2) untreated despite eligibility; (3) untreated and ineligible. Among 3648 participants (56% male; median age 48 years; 49% born in North-East or South-East Asia; 27% First Nations Australians), 14% were HBeAg positive and 7% had cirrhosis.
Core methods
Treatment eligibility was determined using Australian Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme (PBS) criteria, and by international clinical guidelines.
Main results
Overall, 47% (n = 1666) were on antiviral therapy, 45% (n = 1573) were untreated and ineligible and 7% (n = 251) were untreated despite eligibility based on Australian PBS criteria.
Interpretation and novelty
In adjusted analysis, the likelihood of remaining untreated despite eligibility was higher among women (aOR 1.59, 95% CI 1.34, 1.88) and younger participants (age 41-60 vs. > 60 years: aOR 1.51, 95% CI 1.01-2.28; ≤ 40 vs. 41-60 years: aOR 1.85, 95% CI 1.34-2.56). The proportion untreated despite eligibility increased to 13% when applying 2024 World Health Organisation guidelines and ranged 2%-44% based on other guidelines, highlighting the variability across the guidelines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the variability in treatment eligibility criteria across guidelines and underscore the importance of monitoring treatment patterns as guidelines evolve. Lower treatment uptake among women and younger people despite eligibility partly reflects lower threshold for elevated ALT in women, and clinician determination of non-CHB related ALT elevation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and potential biases in clinician determination of non-CHB related ALT elevation. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data and does not include experimental or longitudinal follow-up.

通过机器学习实现针对慢性乙型肝炎相关肝纤维化的个性化预测

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning
作者： Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
摘要中文翻译尽管长期抗病毒药物治疗可以逆转慢性乙型肝炎患者的肝纤维化,但个体对治疗的反应差异显著,目前仍缺乏可靠的预测方法。传统的临床工具如肝硬度测量和天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶与血小板比值指数最初设计用于横断面分期,而非纵向预测,其捕捉纤维化逆转动态过程的能力有限。在本观点综述中,我们讨论了机器学习(ML)如何帮助解决这一未满足的需求。我们重点关注了机器学习在三个方面的早期但有希望的结果:利用机器学习进行图像的定量分析、通过多模态模型整合病理和临床数据,以及基于抗病毒药物治疗期间的连续测量动态预测治疗反应。我们认为,机器学习的最大价值并不在于取代现有评估工具,而在于补充它们,特别是在早期识别那些仅通过抗病毒药物治疗难以实现纤维化逆转的患者方面。然而,我们也提醒,目前大多数模型都是基于小规模、单中心的回顾性队列研究开发的,其临床应用需要前瞻性多中心验证、提高模型的可解释性以及遵循标准化的报告框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决目前缺乏可靠方法预测慢性乙型肝炎患者对治疗反应的问题,因为长期抗病毒治疗可以逆转肝纤维化,但并非所有患者都能实现逆转。
设计与对象
本观点综述讨论了机器学习在改善慢性乙型肝炎患者纤维化逆转预测方面的潜力,重点关注临床工具在捕捉动态纤维化过程方面的局限性。
核心方法
作者回顾了机器学习如何应用于分析影像数据、整合临床和病理数据,并通过在抗病毒治疗期间进行连续测量来预测患者对治疗的反应。
主要结果
机器学习在三个领域已显示出早期但有希望的结果:定量影像分析、临床和病理数据的多模态整合,以及基于连续测量的动态预测患者对治疗的反应。
解释与创新
作者解释认为,机器学习的最大价值在于补充现有工具,特别是在识别那些仅通过抗病毒药物治疗难以实现纤维化逆转的患者方面,而不是取代它们。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,机器学习可能有助于提高慢性乙型肝炎治疗的功能性治愈率,并优化个性化治疗策略,从而改善纤维化逆转的预测能力并指导临床决策。
局限与证据强度
目前的证据受到限制,因为大多数现有模型都是基于小规模、单一中心的回顾性数据,这可能影响其普适性,需要前瞻性多中心验证。

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning

Authors: Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Although long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in a considerable proportion of patients with chronic hepatitis B, treatment response differs markedly among individuals, and reliable methods for predicting who will benefit remain lacking. Conventional clinical tools such as liver stiffness measurement and the aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index were originally dewsigned for cross-sectional staging rather than longitudinal prediction, and their ability to capture the dynamic process of fibrosis reversal is limited. In this opinion review, we discuss how machine learning (ML) may help address this unmet need. We focus on three areas where ML has shown early but encouraging results: Quantitative analysis of images using ML, integration of pathological and clinical data through multimodal models, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements during antiviral therapy. In our view, the greatest value of ML lies not in replacing existing assessment tools but in complementing them, particularly through the early identification of patients who are unlikely to achieve fibrosis reversal with antiviral therapy alone. However, we also caution that most current models have been developed from small, single-center retrospective cohorts, and their clinical adoption will require prospective multi-center validation, improved model interpretability, and adherence to standardized reporting frameworks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of reliable methods for predicting individual treatment response in chronic hepatitis B patients, as long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in some individuals but not all.
Design and population
This opinion review discusses the potential of machine learning to improve prediction of fibrosis reversal in chronic hepatitis B, focusing on clinical tools that are limited in capturing dynamic fibrosis processes.
Core methods
The authors review how machine learning can be applied to analyze images, integrate clinical and pathological data, and predict treatment response through serial measurements during antiviral therapy.
Main results
Machine learning has shown early but encouraging results in three areas: quantitative image analysis, multimodal integration of clinical and pathological data, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that machine learning's greatest value lies in complementing existing tools, particularly for identifying patients unlikely to achieve fibrosis reversal with antiviral therapy alone, rather than replacing them.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that machine learning could enhance surveillance and personalized treatment strategies for chronic hepatitis B, improving the prediction of fibrosis reversal and guiding clinical decisions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the fact that most current models are developed from small, single-center retrospective cohorts, which may affect their generalizability and require prospective multi-center validation.

破坏 NTCP-EGFR 受体复合物作为选择性抑制乙型和丁型肝炎病毒进入的策略

Disruption of the NTCP–EGFR receptor complex as a strategy for selective inhibition of hepatitis B and D virus entry
作者： Atsuto Kusunoki, Kaho Shionoya, Frank Stappenbeck, Takeshi Morita, Hirofumi Ohashi, Noritaka Kagaya, Makoto Nagano, Junki Mifune, Ryo Morishita, Feng Wang
摘要中文翻译乙型和丁型肝炎病毒(HBV、HDV)通过受体复合物介导的协调过程进入肝细胞,该受体复合物由牛磺胆酸钠共转运多肽(NTCP)和宿主进入辅助因子(包括表皮生长因子受体(EGFR))组成。在这里,我们建立了一种体外测定法来评估 NTCP-EGFR 相互作用,并鉴定出 Oxy229,一种基于氧甾醇的化合物,可以破坏这种分子相互作用。Oxy229 选择性抑制 HBV 和 HDV 对 HepG2-NTCP 细胞和原代人肝细胞的感染。机制分析表明,Oxy229 损害了 HBV-NTCP 复合物从质膜到细胞内囊泡的重新定位。值得注意的是,Oxy229 不会损害 NTCP 和 EGFR 的生理功能,即胆汁酸转运蛋白和下游 EGFR 信号通路(包括 Ras-MAPK 和 PI3K-Akt 通路)的激活,表明对病毒进入的选择性抑制。化合物衍生物分析确定了 Oxy283,它获得了针对 NTCP-EGFR 相互作用和 NTCP 多聚化的双重抑制活性,从而增强了抗 HBV 效力。这些发现确立了 NTCP-受体复合物形成在 HBV/HDV 进入中的功能意义,并强调该机制作为 抗病毒药物 干预的潜在目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型和丁型肝炎病毒(HBV、HDV)通过受体复合物介导的协调过程进入肝细胞,该受体复合物由牛磺胆酸钠共转运多肽(NTCP)和宿主进入辅助因子(包括表皮生长因子受体(EGFR))组成。
设计与对象
在这里,我们建立了一种体外测定法来评估 NTCP-EGFR 相互作用,并鉴定出 Oxy229,一种基于氧甾醇的化合物,可以破坏这种分子相互作用。
核心方法
Oxy229 选择性抑制 HBV 和 HDV 对 HepG2-NTCP 细胞和原代人肝细胞的感染。
主要结果
机制分析表明,Oxy229 损害了 HBV-NTCP 复合物从质膜到细胞内囊泡的重新定位。
解释与创新
值得注意的是,Oxy229 不会损害 NTCP 和 EGFR 的生理功能,即胆汁酸转运蛋白和下游 EGFR 信号通路(包括 Ras-MAPK 和 PI3K-Akt 通路)的激活,表明对病毒进入的选择性抑制。
科研与公卫意义
化合物衍生物分析确定了 Oxy283,它获得了针对 NTCP-EGFR 相互作用和 NTCP 多聚化的双重抑制活性,从而增强了抗 HBV 效力。
局限与证据强度
这些发现确立了 NTCP-受体复合物形成在 HBV/HDV 进入中的功能意义,并强调该机制作为 抗病毒药物 干预的潜在目标。

Disruption of the NTCP–EGFR receptor complex as a strategy for selective inhibition of hepatitis B and D virus entry

Authors: Atsuto Kusunoki, Kaho Shionoya, Frank Stappenbeck, Takeshi Morita, Hirofumi Ohashi, Noritaka Kagaya, Makoto Nagano, Junki Mifune, Ryo Morishita, Feng Wang
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B and D virus (HBV, HDV) enter hepatocytes through a coordinated process mediated by a receptor complex consisting of sodium taurocholate cotransporting polypeptide (NTCP), and host entry cofactors, including epidermal growth factor receptor (EGFR). Here, we established an in vitro assay to evaluate the NTCP–EGFR interaction and identified Oxy229, an oxysterol-based compound that disrupted this molecular interaction. Oxy229 selectively inhibited HBV and HDV infection to HepG2-NTCP cells and primary human hepatocytes. Mechanistic analysis revealed that Oxy229 impaired the relocalization of HBV–NTCP complex from the plasma membrane to intracellular vesicles. Notably, Oxy229 did not compromise the physiological functions of NTCP and EGFR, i.e., bile acid transporter and activation of downstream EGFR signaling pathways including Ras–MAPK and PI3K–Akt pathways, indicating selective inhibition of viral entry. Compound derivative analysis identified Oxy283, which acquired dual inhibitory activity against both NTCP–EGFR interaction and NTCP multimerization, resulting in enhanced anti-HBV potency. These findings establish the functional significance of NTCP–receptor complex formation in the HBV/HDV entry and highlight this machinery as a potential target for antiviral intervention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B and D virus (HBV, HDV) enter hepatocytes through a coordinated process mediated by a receptor complex consisting of sodium taurocholate cotransporting polypeptide (NTCP), and host entry cofactors, including epidermal growth factor receptor (EGFR).
Design and population

Core methods

Main results

法医尸检标本中血源性感染的即时检测(POCT)评估。

Evaluation of point-of-care testing (POCT) for blood-borne infections in forensic autopsy specimens.
作者： Ga Eun Kim, Dong-Ye-On Lee, Eun-Byoul Jung, Sojung Lim, Hwan Young Lee, Soong Deok Lee, Moon-Young Kim
摘要中文翻译摘要 基于侧流免疫分析 (LFIA) 的病毒抗原和抗体检测即时检测 (POCT) 能够快速筛查血源性病毒感染,在法医尸检环境中可能特别有价值,因为在法医尸检环境中,不完整的生前病史和侵入性操作会增加职业暴露的风险。然而,其在尸检标本中的表现仍不清楚。我们评估了基于 LFIA 的 POCT 在检测尸检标本中人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 方面的适用性,重点关注死后分解和使用实体器官组织作为血液替代品。使用 LFIA 和定量聚合酶链反应 (qPCR) 对血液、肝脏和脾脏标本总共 309 例进行了分析。使用总体一致性百分比、正一致性百分比和负一致性百分比以及 Cohen 的 kappa 系数 (κ) 来评估一致性。根据标本类型和分解状态比较阳性率。LFIA 与 qPCR 的一致性因标记物和样本类型而异,血液中 HIV 和 HCV 抗体检测的一致性较高,而肝脏和脾脏的一致性较低。新鲜标本的 LFIA 阳性率通常高于腐烂标本。在符合预定 qPCR 阳性标准的标本中,LFIA 阳性标本往往比 LFIA 阴性标本表现出更高的病毒载量,肝脏中 HBV 达到统计学显着性,血液 HBV 和肝脏 HCV 具有相似但不显着的趋势。基于 LFIA 的 POCT 可以作为尸检中的初步筛查工具,特别是在有血液的情况下。然而,阴性结果并不能排除感染,尤其是病毒载量较低或分解较严重的情况,应通过分子检测来确认。重要性 进行尸检的法医人员面临着暴露于血源性病毒(包括人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型肝炎病毒 和丙型肝炎病毒)的重大职业风险。与临床环境不同,法医尸检经常涉及感染史未知的尸体,因此快速感染筛查对于人员安全至关重要。基于侧流免疫分析 (LFIA) 的病毒抗原和抗体检测即时检测 (POCT) 提供了一种快速、无需基础设施的筛查选项;然而,其在尸检标本中的性能尚未得到系统评估。这项研究表明,快速免疫层析 POCT 可以作为法医尸检环境中的初步筛查工具,特别是在有血液样本的情况下。然而,阴性结果并不能排除感染,特别是在病毒载量低或分解严重的情况下,仍然需要进行确认性分子检测。这些发现为法医专业人员适当选择标本和解释 LFIA 结果提供了实用指导,最终支持更安全的尸检实践。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估基于 LFIA 的即时检测 (POCT) 在法医尸检标本中检测 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 的适用性,解决在生前病史不完整和职业暴露风险显着的情况下快速筛查的需求。
设计与对象
该研究使用 LFIA 和定量聚合酶链反应 (qPCR) 分析了 309 例血液、肝脏和脾脏样本,以评估基于 LFIA 的 POCT 在死后环境中检测 HBV 的性能。
核心方法
使用整体、阳性和阴性百分比一致性以及科恩卡帕系数 (κ) 评估 LFIA 和 qPCR 之间的一致性,同时根据样本类型和分解状态比较阳性率。
主要结果
LFIA 与 qPCR 的一致性因标记物和样本类型而异,血液中 HIV 和 HCV 抗体检测的一致性较高,而肝脏和脾脏的一致性较低。在符合预定 qPCR 阳性标准的样本中,LFIA 阳性样本往往比 LFIA 阴性样本表现出更高的病毒载量,肝脏中的 HBV 达到统计学显着性。
解释与创新
该研究表明,基于 LFIA 的 POCT 可以作为法医尸检环境中的初步筛查工具,特别是在可获得血液样本的情况下,但强调阴性结果并不能排除感染,特别是在病毒载量较低或分解较严重的情况下。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为法医专业人员适当选择标本和解释 LFIA 结果提供了实用指导,支持更安全的尸检实践,并为尸检环境中快速筛查工具的使用提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注死后分解和使用实体器官组织作为血液的替代品,并且由于数据仅抽象且缺乏详细的统计分析,证据强度中等。

Evaluation of point-of-care testing (POCT) for blood-borne infections in forensic autopsy specimens.

Authors: Ga Eun Kim, Dong-Ye-On Lee, Eun-Byoul Jung, Sojung Lim, Hwan Young Lee, Soong Deok Lee, Moon-Young Kim
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Lateral flow immunoassay (LFIA)-based point-of-care testing (POCT) for viral antigen and antibody detection enables rapid screening for blood-borne viral infections and may be particularly valuable in forensic autopsy settings, where incomplete antemortem medical histories and invasive procedures increase the risk of occupational exposure. However, its performance in postmortem specimens remains unclear. We evaluated the applicability of LFIA-based POCT for detecting human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and hepatitis B virus (HBV) in autopsy specimens, focusing on postmortem decomposition and the use of solid organ tissues as alternatives to blood. A total of 309 cases were analyzed using LFIA and quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) across blood, liver, and spleen specimens. Agreement was assessed using overall, positive, and negative percent agreement and Cohen’s kappa coefficient ( κ ). Positivity rates were compared by specimen type and decomposition status. LFIA agreement with qPCR varied by marker and specimen type, with substantial agreement for HIV and HCV antibody detection in blood and lower agreement in liver and spleen. Fresh specimens generally demonstrated higher LFIA positivity rates than decomposed specimens. Among specimens meeting predefined qPCR positivity criteria, LFIA-positive specimens tended to exhibit higher viral loads than LFIA-negative specimens, reaching statistical significance for HBV in liver, with similar but non-significant trends for blood HBV and liver HCV. LFIA-based POCT may serve as a preliminary screening tool in postmortem settings, particularly when blood is available. Nevertheless, negative results do not exclude infection—especially with low viral loads or advanced decomposition—and should be confirmed by molecular testing. IMPORTANCE Forensic personnel performing autopsies face significant occupational risks from exposure to blood-borne viruses, including human immunodeficiency virus, hepatitis B virus, and hepatitis C virus. Unlike clinical settings, forensic autopsies frequently involve bodies with unknown infection histories, making rapid infection screening essential for personnel safety. Lateral flow immunoassay (LFIA)-based point-of-care testing (POCT) for viral antigen and antibody detection offers a rapid, infrastructure-free screening option; however, its performance in postmortem specimens has not been systematically evaluated. This study suggests that rapid immunochromatographic POCT may serve as a preliminary screening tool in forensic autopsy settings, particularly when blood specimens are available. However, a negative result does not exclude infection, especially in cases with low viral load or advanced decomposition, and confirmatory molecular testing remains necessary. These findings provide practical guidance for forensic professionals on appropriate specimen selection and interpretation of LFIA results, ultimately supporting safer autopsy practices.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the applicability of LFIA-based point-of-care testing (POCT) for detecting hepatitis B virus (HBV) in forensic autopsy specimens, addressing the need for rapid screening in settings where incomplete antemortem medical histories and occupational exposure risks are significant.
Design and population
The study analyzed 309 cases using LFIA and quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) across blood, liver, and spleen specimens to assess the performance of LFIA-based POCT for HBV detection in postmortem settings.
Core methods
Agreement between LFIA and qPCR was assessed using overall, positive, and negative percent agreement and Cohen’s kappa coefficient (κ), while positivity rates were compared by specimen type and decomposition status.
Main results
LFIA agreement with qPCR varied by marker and specimen type, with substantial agreement for HIV and HCV antibody detection in blood and lower agreement in liver and spleen. Among specimens meeting predefined qPCR positivity criteria, LFIA-positive specimens tended to exhibit higher viral loads than LFIA-negative specimens, reaching statistical significance for HBV in liver.
Interpretation and novelty
The study suggests that LFIA-based POCT may serve as a preliminary screening tool in forensic autopsy settings, particularly when blood specimens are available, but emphasizes that negative results do not exclude infection, especially with low viral loads or advanced decomposition.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide practical guidance for forensic professionals on appropriate specimen selection and interpretation of LFIA results, supporting safer autopsy practices and informing the use of rapid screening tools in postmortem settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on postmortem decomposition and the use of solid organ tissues as alternatives to blood, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the data and the lack of detailed statistical analysis.

根据年龄组和胎龄评估接受三剂乙型肝炎 疫苗 儿童的抗 HBs 水平:一项回顾性单中心横断面研究

Evaluation of Anti-HBs Levels in Children Who Received Three Doses of Hepatitis B Vaccine According to Age Groups and Gestational Age: A Retrospective Single-Center Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Zeynep Gizem Ergün Özdel, Fatma Dilşad Aksoy, Esra Çiftci, Sinem Irez Cetin, İmran Sağlık, Güven Özkaya, Salih Çağrı Çakır, Solmaz Çelebi, Mustafa Kemal HACIMUSTAFAOĞLU, Çiftçi E
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估接受三剂乙型肝炎 疫苗 的儿童的抗 HBs 水平如何随年龄和胎龄变化,因为大型儿科 疫苗 中这些因素的数据有限。
设计与对象
一项回顾性、单中心、横断面研究涉及 6,510 名 9 个月至 18 岁的儿童,他们在 2021 年至 2023 年间完成了三剂乙型肝炎 疫苗 剂量,并至少进行了一次定量抗 HBs 测量。
核心方法
该研究使用了 Kruskal-Wallis、卡方、ANCOVA 和逻辑回归等统计方法来比较不同年龄组和胎龄类别的抗 HBs 水平和血清阴性。
主要结果
总体血清阴性率为 30.6% (1,989/6,510),中位抗 HBs 水平为 34.30 mIU/mL (IQR 6.72–143.23)。抗 HBs 水平随着年龄的增长而显着下降,而血清阴性水平则随着年龄组的增加而增加。
解释与创新
作者解释说,在接受三剂乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 后,抗 HBs 水平随着年龄的增长而显着下降,并且未发现孕龄是血清阴性的独立预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性的血清学随访和个体化加强策略可能是必要的,特别是对于高危人群,以保持足够的乙型肝炎免疫力。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和单中心设置而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,由于样本量大和统计调整,证据是强有力的。

Evaluation of Anti-HBs Levels in Children Who Received Three Doses of Hepatitis B Vaccine According to Age Groups and Gestational Age: A Retrospective Single-Center Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Zeynep Gizem Ergün Özdel, Fatma Dilşad Aksoy, Esra Çiftci, Sinem Irez Cetin, İmran Sağlık, Güven Özkaya, Salih Çağrı Çakır, Solmaz Çelebi, Mustafa Kemal HACIMUSTAFAOĞLU, Çiftçi E
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Anti-HBs levels decline after infant hepatitis B vaccination, especially during school age and adolescence, but data evaluating both age and gestational age-related effects in a large pediatric cohort remain limited. Methods In this retrospective, single-center, cross-sectional study, we included 6,510 children aged 9 months–18 years who had completed three hepatitis B vaccine doses and had at least one quantitative anti-HBs measurement between 2021 and 2023. Seronegativity was defined as anti-HBs < 10 mIU/mL. Children were grouped by age (9 months–5 years, 6–11 years, 12–18 years) and gestational age (early preterm < 32 weeks, late preterm 32–36+6 weeks, term ≥ 37 weeks). Comparisons used Kruskal–Wallis, chi-square, ANCOVA, and logistic regression. Results Overall seronegativity was 30.6% (1,989/6,510), and the median anti-HBs level was 34.30 mIU/mL (IQR 6.72–143.23). Anti-HBs levels decreased significantly with age (medians 99.17, 23.94, and 12.55 mIU/mL for the 9 months–5 years, 6–11 years, and 12–18 years groups; p < 0.001), while seronegativity increased (14.4%, 33.9%, and 45.5%; p < 0.001). Age group was the strongest independent predictor of seronegativity (6–11 years: OR 3.218, 95% CI 2.521–4.108; 12–18 years: OR 5.348, 95% CI 4.183–6.839). Among 4,242 children with gestational age data, gestational age was not independently associated with seronegativity after adjustment (p = 0.109), and seronegativity rates were comparable across groups (34.5% early preterm, 25.8% late preterm, 36.9% term; p = 0.168). In children born at term, mean anti-HBs was 525.05 ± 401.31 mIU/mL at ≤ 3 years versus 74.99 ± 143.60 mIU/mL at ≥ 4 years. Conclusion Anti-HBs levels decline significantly with age after three-dose hepatitis B vaccination, whereas gestational age is not an independent predictor of seronegativity. Targeted serological follow-up and individualized booster strategies, particularly for high-risk groups, may be warranted.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate how anti-HBs levels change with age and gestational age in children who received three doses of hepatitis B vaccine, as data on these factors in a large pediatric cohort are limited.
Design and population
A retrospective, single-center, cross-sectional study was conducted involving 6,510 children aged 9 months to 18 years who had completed three hepatitis B vaccine doses and had at least one quantitative anti-HBs measurement between 2021 and 2023.
Core methods
The study used statistical methods including Kruskal–Wallis, chi-square, ANCOVA, and logistic regression to compare anti-HBs levels and seronegativity across age groups and gestational age categories.
Main results
Overall seronegativity was 30.6% (1,989/6,510), and the median anti-HBs level was 34.30 mIU/mL (IQR 6.72–143.23). Anti-HBs levels decreased significantly with age, while seronegativity increased across age groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that anti-HBs levels decline significantly with age after three-dose hepatitis B vaccination, and gestational age was not found to be an independent predictor of seronegativity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted serological follow-up and individualized booster strategies may be necessary, particularly for high-risk groups, to maintain adequate immunity against hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and single-center setting, which may affect generalizability. However, the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and statistical adjustments.

科特迪瓦献血者医疗随访中心 (CMSDS) 对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性献血者中的 δ 型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 进行 患病率 跟踪

Prevalence of hepatitis delta virus (HDV) among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) in Cte dIvoire
作者： Renaud Dechi Jacques, Jeanne Armand Ake Aya, Kabore Saydou, Philippe N'Din Jean-Louis, Moussa Salimata, Oura Doris, Dembele Bamory, Siransy Liliane, Mamadou Sekongo Yassongui, Doris Oura
摘要中文翻译慢性丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染会加剧 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝病。在科特迪瓦,未对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性献血者进行系统性筛查,大多数区域估计依赖于抗 HDV 血清学,这表明暴露但不活跃复制。本研究旨在确定 HBsAg 阳性献血者中 HDV RNA 复制阳性情况。科特迪瓦国家输血中心 (CNTSCI) 的献血者医疗随访中心 (CMSDS) 使用巢式逆转录聚合酶链式反应 (RT-PCR)。2021 年 3 月进行了一项基于非概率便利样本的横断面研究。通过针对两个基因组区域(核苷酸 888-1122 和 313-485)的巢式 RT-PCR 检测了 HDV RNA。51 名捐献者中其中,75% 为男性 (n = 38),平均年龄为 37 ± 9 岁。43 名捐献者 (84%) 可获得 HBV DNA 结果。其中,可检测到 HBV 病毒血症的 HDV RNA 阳性率为 88.9% (32/36),病毒载量无法检测到的患者为 100% (7/7),总体阳性率为 90.7% (39/43)。该数字仅适用于具有可用病毒载量数据的捐献者,而不是 51 名捐献者的全部 队列研究。在接受富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 且 HBV 病毒血症受到抑制的捐献者中也检测到 HDV RNA。考虑到已经处于后续护理中的捐献者的方便样本,研究结果是假设生成而不是人群水平估计。这些结果支持更大规模的代表性研究和个体随访期间的系统性 HDV 筛查关键词:乙型肝炎病毒/丁型肝炎病毒(HBV/HDV)合并感染,巢式逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR),分子流行病学,方便样本,输血医学,西非。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用巢式逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 确定在科特迪瓦国家输血中心 (CNTSCI) 的献血者医疗随访中心 (CMSDS) 随访的 HBsAg 阳性献血者中 HDV RNA 复制阳性情况。
设计与对象
2021 年 3 月进行了一项基于非概率便利样本的横断面研究。在 51 名捐赠者中,75% 为男性 (n = 38),平均年龄为 37 ± 9 岁。
核心方法
通过针对两个基因组区域(核苷酸 888-1122 和 313-485)的巢式 RT-PCR 检测 HDV RNA。43 名捐献者 (84%) 可获得 HBV DNA 结果。
主要结果
在 43 名有 HBV DNA 结果的捐献者中,可检测到 HBV 病毒血症的 HDV RNA 阳性率为 88.9% (32/36),而病毒载量未检测到的捐献者则为 100% (7/7),总体阳性率为 90.7% (39/43)。在接受富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 且 HBV 病毒血症受到抑制的捐赠者中也检测到了 HDV RNA。
解释与创新
这些结果支持在慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者的随访过程中进行更大规模的代表性研究和系统性丁型肝炎病毒筛查。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了对慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染者进行系统性丁型肝炎病毒筛查的必要性,特别是在没有常规检测丁型肝炎病毒的地区。
局限与证据强度
考虑到已经接受后续护理的捐赠者的方便样本,研究结果是假设生成而不是人口水平的估计。

Prevalence of hepatitis delta virus (HDV) among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)-positive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) in Cte dIvoire

Authors: Renaud Dechi Jacques, Jeanne Armand Ake Aya, Kabore Saydou, Philippe N'Din Jean-Louis, Moussa Salimata, Oura Doris, Dembele Bamory, Siransy Liliane, Mamadou Sekongo Yassongui, Doris Oura
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis delta virus (HDV) infection exacerbates hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related liver disease.In Côte d'Ivoire, HDV is systematic not systematically screened among hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg)positive blood donors, and most regional estimates rely on anti-HDV serology, which indicates exposure but not active replication.This study aimed to determine HDV RNA replication positivity among HBsAgpositive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) of the National Blood Transfusion Centre of Côte d'Ivoire (CNTSCI), using nested reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR).A cross-sectional study based on a non-probability convenience sample was conducted in March 2021.HDV RNA was detected by nested RT-PCR targeting two genomic regions (nucleotides 888-1122 and 313-485).Of 51 donors included, 75% were male (n = 38) and and mean age was 37 ± 9 years.HBV DNA results were available for 43 donors (84%).Among them, HDV RNA positivity was 88.9% (32/36) in those with detectable HBV viraemia and 100% (7/7) in those with undetectable viral load, giving an overall positivity 90.7% (39/43).This figure applies only to donors with available viral load data not the entire cohort of 51 donors.HDV RNA was also detected in donors receiving tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) with suppressed HBV viraemia.Given the convenience sample of donors already in follow-up care, findings are hypothesis-generating rather than population-level estimates.These results support larger representative studies and systematic HDV screening during follow-up of individuals with chronic HBV infection.Key words: Hepatitis B virus/hepatitis delta virus (HBV/HDV) co-infection, nested reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR), molecular epidemiology, convenience sample, transfusion medicine, West Africa.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to determine HDV RNA replication positivity among HBsAg-positive blood donors followed at the Blood Donor Medical Follow-up Centre (CMSDS) of the National Blood Transfusion Centre of Côte d'Ivoire (CNTSCI), using nested reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR).
Design and population
A cross-sectional study based on a non-probability convenience sample was conducted in March 2021. Of 51 donors included, 75% were male (n = 38) and the mean age was 37 ± 9 years.
Core methods
HDV RNA was detected by nested RT-PCR targeting two genomic regions (nucleotides 888-1122 and 313-485). HBV DNA results were available for 43 donors (84%).
Main results
Among the 43 donors with HBV DNA results, HDV RNA positivity was 88.9% (32/36) in those with detectable HBV viraemia and 100% (7/7) in those with undetectable viral load, giving an overall positivity of 90.7% (39/43). HDV RNA was also detected in donors receiving tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) with suppressed HBV viraemia.
Interpretation and novelty
These results support larger representative studies and systematic HDV screening during follow-up of individuals with chronic HBV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for systematic HDV screening among individuals with chronic HBV infection, particularly in regions where HDV is not routinely tested.
Limitations and evidence strength
Given the convenience sample of donors already in follow-up care, findings are hypothesis-generating rather than population-level estimates.

半定量超声评分揭示慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)在接受替诺福韦艾拉酚胺治疗期间肝脏结构恢复和免疫调节

Semi-quantitative ultrasound scoring reveals hepatic structural recovery and immune modulation during tenofovir alafenamide therapy in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Wen-Juan Li, Li-Hui Yang, Juan He, Wenjuan Li, Lihui Yang
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在利用半定量超声(US)评分系统评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺二甲基磺酸盐(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中的治疗反应,并探讨超声影像变化与免疫功能恢复之间的相关性。方法 入选了100例接受TAF单药治疗(25 mg/天)至少24个月的慢性乙型肝炎患者进行回顾性分析。在基线、12个月和24个月时计算了包含肝脏回声、包膜轮廓、边缘形态、血管清晰度、门静脉直径(PVD)、脾脏厚度(ST)和脾静脉血流速度(SVV)的七参数半定量超声评分。同时测量了血清乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)脱氧核糖核酸(DNA)、丙氨酸氨基转移酶(ALT)、天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶(AST)以及外周T细胞亚群,包括分化簇3阳性(CD3+)、CD4+、CD8+ T细胞、CD4+/CD8+比值和自然杀伤(NK)细胞活性。分析了超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的相关性。结果 在24个月时,所有患者均达到HBV DNA <20 IU/mL,ALT和AST的正常化率分别为92%和95%。综合超声评分从基线的7.86 ± 1.73显著下降至24个月的3.37 ± 0.95(P < 0.001),其中包膜平滑度和实质均匀性的改善最为显著。CD4+ T细胞比例和CD4+/CD8+比值增加,而CD8+ T细胞比例下降;NK细胞活性从42.7 ± 7.3%上升至52.4 ± 6.2%(P < 0.05)。超声评分与ALT(r = 0.56)和AST(r = 0.47)呈正相关,与CD4+/CD8+比值(r = −0.46)和NK细胞活性(r = −0.38)呈负相关(所有P < 0.01)。结论 这些发现表明,TAF不仅抑制病毒复制并使肝酶正常化,还促进可测量的肝脏结构改善,这体现在半定量超声评分的下降。超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的强相关性支持其作为非侵入性影像生物标志物用于监测治疗疗效和肝脏修复的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用半定量超声(US)评分系统评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺二甲基磺酸盐(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中的治疗反应,并探讨超声影像变化与免疫功能恢复之间的相关性。
设计与对象
回顾性纳入 100 名接受 TAF 单药治疗(25 毫克/天)至少 24 个月的慢性乙型肝炎患者。
核心方法
在基线、12 个月和 24 个月时计算七参数半定量 US 评分,包括肝脏回声性、包膜轮廓、边缘形状、血管清晰度、门静脉直径 (PVD)、脾厚度 (ST) 和脾静脉速度 (SVV)。同时,测量血清 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA)、丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT)、天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST)、外周 T 细胞亚群,包括分化簇 3 阳性 (CD3 + )、CD4 + 、CD8 + T 细胞、CD4 + / CD8 + 比率和自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞活性。
主要结果
在24个月时,所有患者均达到HBV DNA <20 IU/mL,ALT和AST的正常化率分别为92%和95%。综合超声评分从基线的7.86 ± 1.73显著下降至24个月的3.37 ± 0.95(P < 0.001),其中包膜平滑度和实质均匀性的改善最为显著。
解释与创新
分析了美国评分与生化/免疫参数之间的相关性。US评分与ALT(r = 0.56)和AST(r = 0.47)呈正相关,与CD4 + /CD8 + 比值(r = -0.46)和NK细胞活性(r = -0.38)呈负相关(均P < 0.01)。
科研与公卫意义
超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的强相关性支持其作为非侵入性影像生物标志物用于监测治疗疗效和肝脏修复的潜力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。

Semi-quantitative ultrasound scoring reveals hepatic structural recovery and immune modulation during tenofovir alafenamide therapy in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Wen-Juan Li, Li-Hui Yang, Juan He, Wenjuan Li, Lihui Yang
Original abstract (English)
Objective This study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic response to tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) using a semi-quantitative ultrasound (US) scoring system, and to investigate the correlation between sonographic changes and immune function restoration. Methods A total of 100 CHB patients who received TAF monotherapy (25 mg/day) for at least 24 months were retrospectively enrolled. A seven-parameter semi-quantitative US score, encompassing liver echogenicity, capsular contour, margin shape, vessel clarity, portal vein diameter (PVD), spleen thickness (ST), and splenic vein velocity (SVV), was calculated at baseline, 12 months, and 24 months. Concurrently, serum hepatitis B virus (HBV) deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA), alanine aminotransferase (ALT), aspartate aminotransferase (AST), peripheral T-cell subsets, including cluster of differentiation 3-positive (CD3 + ), CD4 + , CD8 + T cells, CD4+/CD8+ ratio, and natural killer (NK) cell activity were measured. Correlations between US scores and biochemical/immune parameters were analyzed. Results At 24 months, all patients achieved HBV DNA <20 IU/mL, with ALT and AST normalization rates of 92% and 95%, respectively. The comprehensive US score decreased significantly from 7.86 ± 1.73 at baseline to 3.37 ± 0.95 at 24 months ( P < 0.001), with the most pronounced improvements observed in capsular smoothness and parenchymal homogeneity. The CD4 + T-cell proportion and CD4 + /CD8 + ratio increased, whereas the CD8 + proportion decreased; NK cell activity rose from 42.7 ± 7.3% to 52.4 ± 6.2% ( P < 0.05). The US score was positively correlated with ALT (r = 0.56) and AST (r = 0.47), and negatively correlated with the CD4 + /CD8 + ratio (r = −0.46) and NK cell activity (r = −0.38) (all P < 0.01). Conclusion These findings indicate that TAF not only suppresses viral replication and normalises liver enzymes but also promotes measurable structural liver improvement, as reflected by declining semi-quantitative US scores. The strong correlations of US scores with biochemical and immune parameters support its utility as a non-invasive imaging biomarker for monitoring treatment efficacy and hepatic repair.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic response to tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) using a semi-quantitative ultrasound (US) scoring system, and to investigate the correlation between sonographic changes and immune function restoration.
Design and population
A total of 100 CHB patients who received TAF monotherapy (25 mg/day) for at least 24 months were retrospectively enrolled.
Core methods
A seven-parameter semi-quantitative US score, encompassing liver echogenicity, capsular contour, margin shape, vessel clarity, portal vein diameter (PVD), spleen thickness (ST), and splenic vein velocity (SVV), was calculated at baseline, 12 months, and 24 months. Concurrently, serum hepatitis B virus (HBV) deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA), alanine aminotransferase (ALT), aspartate aminotransferase (AST), peripheral T-cell subsets, including cluster of differentiation 3-positive (CD3 + ), CD4 + , CD8 + T cells, CD4+/CD8+ ratio, and natural killer (NK) cell activity were measured.
Main results
At 24 months, all patients achieved HBV DNA <20 IU/mL, with ALT and AST normalization rates of 92% and 95%, respectively. The comprehensive US score decreased significantly from 7.86 ± 1.73 at baseline to 3.37 ± 0.95 at 24 months (P < 0.001), with the most pronounced improvements observed in capsular smoothness and parenchymal homogeneity.
Interpretation and novelty
Correlations between US scores and biochemical/immune parameters were analyzed. The US score was positively correlated with ALT (r = 0.56) and AST (r = 0.47), and negatively correlated with the CD4 + /CD8 + ratio (r = −0.46) and NK cell activity (r = −0.38) (all P < 0.01).
Scientific and public-health significance
The strong correlations of US scores with biochemical and immune parameters support its utility as a non-invasive imaging biomarker for monitoring treatment efficacy and hepatic repair.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations.

中东 乙型肝炎病毒 基因型 D 的选择、逃逸和推理限制:对 824 个精选公共序列的分析

Selection, escape and the limits of inference in Middle Eastern hepatitis B virus genotype D: an analysis of 824 curated public sequences
作者： Abduladheem AL-Attabi, Adeeb Abdulally Abdulhussein Alazbjee, Noor Abbas Gaseb, Ghadah Ali Al-Oudah, Mustafa Tareq Shanshool
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 乙型肝炎病毒 基因型 D (HBV/D) 在整个中东地区占主导地位,但在对其公共序列档案提出问题之前很少先测试该档案是否能够回答这些问题。方法 从 GenBank 检索来自 15 个中东国家的 500-4,000 nt HBV 记录,根据 98-基因组 A-H 参考组分配基因型,并根据收集日期、表面基因覆盖度、模糊性和国内身份过滤记录。将策划的序列投影到基因型 D 参考 (LC519816.1) 上,从而在表面和反转录酶框架上提供参考锚定矩阵。在 GTR+Γ 下推断最大似然树,通过根到尖回归测试时间信号,在离散迁移模型下重建祖先国家,并在五个国家平衡子样本上重新估计。结果 在检索到的 4,398 条记录中,来自 10 个国家的 824 条序列通过了管理(伊拉克,94 条)。恢复了规范抗性替换(rtL180M、rtM204V/I、rtV173L)和表面逃逸替换(sP120S/T、sS143L、sD144A/E/G、sG145R/A),其中 rtL180M 和 rtM204V/I 最常见(每个 >2%),补偿和逃逸替换如下2%,如治疗 D 基因型感染的临床系列。十二个密码子通过两种方法表现出多样化选择,只有一个在决定簇内,没有一个在经典逃逸位置;抗原环选择与蛋白质的其余部分没有差异(Mann-Whitney p = 0.50;排列 p = 0.61)。四个计划分析失败:从根到尖回归得出 R2 = 0.002,因此无法确定数据集的日期;在平衡二次抽样下,明显的伊朗源信号(+200 净出口)降至+1.2,其中叙利亚(+27.0)取代了伊朗;中位成对距离为 0.0128 个取代/位点,其中一个组件中 97.0% 的序列为 0.015;亚基因型分配仅留下 7.2% 的序列可在 0.005 的裕度下解析。结论 实质上,尽管有免疫 监测,HBsAg 抗原环并未表现出过度的多样化选择,并且监测的逃逸位置不携带位点水平特征,这与逃逸作为罕见的单独选择事件而不是选择性机制一致。从方法上讲,该档案支持突变和选择 传播,但不支持约会、来源归因、传播 聚类或表面基因数据的亚基因型分配;约束是序列长度和抽样设计,而不是样本大小。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 乙型肝炎病毒 D 基因型的公共序列档案,以确定它是否可以支持有关中东选择和逃逸机制的推论。背景强调了 HBV/D 在该地区流行,但其序列档案尚未经过严格测试其回答特定问题的能力。
设计与对象
该研究分析了来自包括伊拉克在内的十个中东国家的 824 个精心策划的公共序列,以探索多样化选择和逃避替代的模式。这些序列源自 GenBank,并根据收集日期、表面基因覆盖度、模糊性和国内身份进行过滤。
核心方法
将序列投影到基因型 D 参考上,并在 GTR+Γ 下推断最大似然树。通过从根到尖的回归测试时间信号,并使用离散迁移模型重建祖先国家,并在五个国家平衡子样本上重新估计。
主要结果
在 4,398 个检索记录中,来自 10 个国家的 824 个序列通过了管理。确定了规范耐药替代(rtL180M、rtM204V/I)和表面逃逸替代(sP120S/T、sS143L、sD144A/E/G、sG145R/A),其中 rtL180M 和 rtM204V/I 最常见(>2%)。十二个密码子显示出多样化的选择,但没有一个位于经典的逃逸位置内。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,HBsAg 抗原环没有表现出过度的多样化选择,并且逃逸位置缺乏位点水平的特征,这表明逃逸源于罕见的单独选择事件,而不是一致的选择机制。这一发现是新颖的,因为它挑战了 HBV/D 中免疫驱动选择的假设。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于理解 HBV/D 中免疫驱动的 进化,并强调使用表面基因数据进行来源归因或 传播 聚类的局限性。这对于改进 监测 和 D 基因型乙型肝炎的诊断策略具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
数据集缺乏时间信号并且无法确定日期,并且由于序列长度和采样设计,亚基因型分配受到限制。尽管存在这些局限性,但证据并不充分,因为该研究提供了对策划序列及其进化模式的详细分析。

Selection, escape and the limits of inference in Middle Eastern hepatitis B virus genotype D: an analysis of 824 curated public sequences

Authors: Abduladheem AL-Attabi, Adeeb Abdulally Abdulhussein Alazbjee, Noor Abbas Gaseb, Ghadah Ali Al-Oudah, Mustafa Tareq Shanshool
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Hepatitis B virus genotype D (HBV/D) predominates across the Middle East, yet questions asked of its public sequence archive are rarely preceded by a test of whether that archive can answer them. Methods HBV records of 500–4,000 nt from fifteen Middle Eastern countries were retrieved from GenBank, genotype assigned against a 98-genome A–H reference panel, and records filtered on collection date, surface-gene coverage, ambiguity and within-country identity. Curated sequences were projected onto a genotype D reference (LC519816.1), giving a reference-anchored matrix across the surface and reverse-transcriptase frames. A maximum-likelihood tree was inferred under GTR+Γ, temporal signal tested by root-to-tip regression, and ancestral country states reconstructed under a discrete migration model and re-estimated on five country-balanced subsamples. Results Of 4,398 records retrieved, 824 sequences from ten countries passed curation (Iraq, 94). Canonical resistance substitutions (rtL180M, rtM204V/I, rtV173L) and surface escape substitutions (sP120S/T, sS143L, sD144A/E/G, sG145R/A) were recovered, with rtL180M and rtM204V/I most frequent (each >2%) the compensatory and escape substitutions each below 2%, as in clinical series of treated genotype D infection. Twelve codons showed diversifying selection by two methods, only one inside a determinant and none at classical escape positions; antigenic-loop selection did not differ from the rest of the protein (Mann-Whitney p = 0.50; permutation p = 0.61). Four planned analyses failed: root-to-tip regression gave R2 = 0.002, so the dataset cannot be dated; the apparent Iranian source signal (+200 net exports) fell to +1.2 under balanced subsampling, where Syria (+27.0) displaced Iran; median pairwise distance was 0.0128 substitutions/site, with 97.0% of sequences in one component at 0.015; and subgenotype assignment left only 7.2% of sequences resolvable at a 0.005 margin. Conclusions Substantively, the HBsAg antigenic loop shows no excess of diversifying selection despite immune surveillance, and monitored escape positions carry no site-level signature, consistent with escape arising as rare individually selected events rather than a selective regime. Methodologically, the archive supports mutational and selection epidemiology but not dating, source attribution, transmission clustering or subgenotype assignment from surface-gene data; the constraint is sequence length and sampling design, not sample size.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the public sequence archive of hepatitis B virus genotype D to determine if it could support inferences about selection and escape mechanisms in the Middle East. The background highlighted that HBV/D is prevalent in the region, but its sequence archive has not been rigorously tested for its ability to answer specific questions.
Design and population
The study analyzed 824 curated public sequences from ten Middle Eastern countries, including Iraq, to explore patterns of diversifying selection and escape substitutions. These sequences were sourced from GenBank and filtered based on collection date, surface-gene coverage, ambiguity, and within-country identity.
Core methods
The sequences were projected onto a genotype D reference, and a maximum-likelihood tree was inferred under GTR+Γ. Temporal signal was tested via root-to-tip regression, and ancestral country states were reconstructed using a discrete migration model, re-estimated on five country-balanced subsamples.
Main results
Of 4,398 retrieved records, 824 sequences from ten countries passed curation. Canonical resistance substitutions (rtL180M, rtM204V/I) and surface escape substitutions (sP120S/T, sS143L, sD144A/E/G, sG145R/A) were identified, with rtL180M and rtM204V/I being the most frequent (>2%). Twelve codons showed diversifying selection, but none were within classical escape positions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the HBsAg antigenic loop does not show an excess of diversifying selection, and escape positions lack site-level signatures, suggesting escape arises from rare individually selected events rather than a consistent selective regime. This finding is novel as it challenges assumptions about immune-driven selection in HBV/D.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the understanding of immune-driven evolution in HBV/D and highlight the limitations of using surface-gene data for source attribution or transmission clustering. This has implications for improving surveillance and diagnostic strategies for genotype D hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The dataset lacks temporal signal and cannot be dated, and subgenotype assignment is limited due to sequence length and sampling design. Despite these limitations, the evidence is moderate, as the study provides a detailed analysis of curated sequences and their evolutionary patterns.
