也门孕妇乙型肝炎表面抗原和乙型肝炎核心抗体患病率

Prevalence of hepatitis B surface antigen and hepatitis B core antibodies among pregnant women in Yemen
作者： Anwar Abdulla Bin-Ghouth, Abdulla Salem Bin Ghouth
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎感染是全世界的公共卫生问题。它与高母婴 传播 率有关,但尚未发表关于也门南部和东部省份孕妇感染的研究。目的:评估也门初级卫生保健中心孕妇的乙型肝炎感染 患病率。方法:2023 年 12 月至 2024 年 2 月期间,我们对在也门亚丁省、穆卡拉省和马里布省初级卫生保健中心接受产前保健服务的 921 名也门孕妇进行了访谈和测试。我们使用 SPSS 25 版分析数据,并使用卡方检验分类变量之间的关联。以P=0.05为统计学显着性。结果:921 名孕妇中有 45 名 (5%) 对 HBsAg 呈反应性 (95% CI;3.6–6.5%),66 名 (7.2%) 对 HBsAg (95% CI;5.6%-9%) 呈反应性。马里卜省的 HBsAg (10.4%) 和抗-HBc (12.7%) 患病率 最高。女性的省份类型和社会地位与 HBsAg 和抗 HBc 反应性显着相关。妊娠早期与 HBsAg 相关。结论:也门孕妇乙型肝炎中度传播增加了母婴传播的风险以及感染儿童慢性肝炎的高风险。它表明需要采取预防性干预措施,包括对孕妇进行常规筛查和引入出生剂量乙型肝炎 疫苗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎感染是全世界的公共卫生问题。它与高母婴 传播 感染率有关,但尚未发表关于也门南部和东部省份孕妇感染情况的研究。
设计与对象
2023年12月至2024年2月期间,我们对在也门亚丁省、穆卡拉省和马里布省初级卫生保健中心接受产前保健服务的921名也门孕妇进行了访谈和测试。
核心方法
我们使用 SPSS 25 版分析数据,并使用卡方检验分类变量之间的关联。以P=0.05为统计学显着性。
主要结果
921 名孕妇中有 45 名 (5%) 对 HBsAg 呈反应性 (95% CI;3.6–6.5%),66 名 (7.2%) 对 HBsAg (95% CI;5.6%-9%) 呈反应性。马里卜省的 HBsAg (10.4%) 和抗-HBc (12.7%) 患病率 最高。女性的省份类型和社会地位与 HBsAg 和抗 HBc 反应性显着相关。妊娠早期与 HBsAg 相关。
解释与创新
孕妇中乙型肝炎病毒的中度流行增加了母婴传播的风险,并且感染儿童中慢性乙型肝炎的高风险率。
科研与公卫意义
它表明需要采取预防性干预措施,包括对孕妇进行常规筛查和引入出生剂量乙型肝炎 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Prevalence of hepatitis B surface antigen and hepatitis B core antibodies among pregnant women in Yemen

Authors: Anwar Abdulla Bin-Ghouth, Abdulla Salem Bin Ghouth
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B infection is a public health concern worldwide. It is associated with high rates of mother-tochild transmission, yet there are no published studies on the infection among pregnant women in the southern and eastern governorates of Yemen. Aim: To assess the prevalence of hepatitis B infection among pregnant women attending primary health care centres in Yemen. Methods: Between December 2023 and February 2024, we interviewed and tested 921 pregnant Yemeni women attending primary health care centres for antenatal care services in Aden, Mukalla and Marib governorates of Yemen. We analysed the data using SPSS version 25 and used chi-square to test the association between the categorical variables. P = 0.05 was taken as statistical significance. Results: Forty-five of the 921 pregnant women (5%) were reactive for HBsAg (95% CI; 3.6–6.5%) and 66 (7.2%) for (95% CI; 5.6%-9%). Marib Governorate had the highest prevalence of HBsAg (10.4%) and anti-HBc (12.7%). Type of governorate and social status of the women were significantly associated with HBsAg and anti-HBc reactivity. First trimester was associated with HBsAg. Conclusion: The moderate prevalence of hepatitis B among pregnant women in Yemen increases the risk of motherto-child transmission and a high-risk of chronic hepatitis in infected children. It indicates the need for preventive interventions, including routine screening of pregnant women and introduction of birth dose hepatitis B vaccine.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B infection is a public health concern worldwide. It is associated with high rates of mother-to-child transmission, yet there are no published studies on the infection among pregnant women in the southern and eastern governorates of Yemen.
Design and population
Between December 2023 and February 2024, we interviewed and tested 921 pregnant Yemeni women attending primary health care centres for antenatal care services in Aden, Mukalla and Marib governorates of Yemen.
Core methods
We analysed the data using SPSS version 25 and used chi-square to test the association between the categorical variables. P = 0.05 was taken as statistical significance.
Main results
Forty-five of the 921 pregnant women (5%) were reactive for HBsAg (95% CI; 3.6–6.5%) and 66 (7.2%) for (95% CI; 5.6%-9%). Marib Governorate had the highest prevalence of HBsAg (10.4%) and anti-HBc (12.7%). Type of governorate and social status of the women were significantly associated with HBsAg and anti-HBc reactivity. First trimester was associated with HBsAg.
Interpretation and novelty
The moderate prevalence of hepatitis B among pregnant women in Yemen increases the risk of mother-to-child transmission and a high-risk of chronic hepatitis in infected children.
Scientific and public-health significance
It indicates the need for preventive interventions, including routine screening of pregnant women and introduction of birth dose hepatitis B vaccine.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

泰国北部乙型肝炎、丙型肝炎和单纯疱疹病毒的社区检测

Community-Based Detection of Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and Herpes Simplex Viruses in Northern Thailand
作者： Sureewan Bumrungthai, Winantaya Sodajun, Thininun Kaewkoon, Sasithon Kruttin, Siriyakorn Pawang, Sureewan Duangjit, Nuchsupha Sunthamala
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
血源性病毒感染仍然是全世界重要的公共卫生问题。该研究旨在提供泰国北部 HBV、HCV 和 HSV 的最新社区流行病学数据。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究调查了泰国北部社区人群的 HBsAg、抗 HCV 抗体和 HSV-1/HSV-2 DNA。对 779 名参与者的血清样本进行了 HBsAg 和抗 HCV 检测,对 639 名参与者的全血样本进行了 HSV-1 和 HSV-2 DNA 检测。
核心方法
Serum samples from 779 participants were tested for HBsAg and anti-HCV using rapid immunochromatographic assays, while whole-blood samples from 639 participants were tested for HSV-1 and HSV-2 DNA using real-time PCR. In the regression analysis, higher body weight was associated with lower odds of HBsAg detection (AOR = 0.955, 95% CI: 0.929–0.982; p = 0.001).
主要结果
分别有 3.0% (23/779) 和 1.8% (14/779) 的参与者检测到 HBsAg 和抗 HCV。在全血样本中分别检测到 0.5% (3/639) 的 HSV-1 和 HSV-2 DNA。
解释与创新
由于 HSV DNA 很少被检测到且序列确认有限,因此应谨慎解释 HSV 结果。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 监测 和泰国北部 HBV 和 HCV 预防策略提供参考。他们还强调需要进行涉及更多人群、定量病毒载量测量和纵向采样的进一步研究。
局限与证据强度
由于 HSV DNA 检测频率低且缺乏序列确认,证据有限。由于横断面设计和标准测定的使用,该研究对其研究结果提供了适度的信心。

Community-Based Detection of Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and Herpes Simplex Viruses in Northern Thailand

Authors: Sureewan Bumrungthai, Winantaya Sodajun, Thininun Kaewkoon, Sasithon Kruttin, Siriyakorn Pawang, Sureewan Duangjit, Nuchsupha Sunthamala
Original abstract (English)
Blood-borne viral infections remain important public health concerns worldwide. Hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) are well-established transfusion-transmissible pathogens, whereas the epidemiological significance of detecting herpes simplex virus (HSV) DNA in peripheral blood remains unclear. This cross-sectional study investigated HBsAg, anti-HCV antibodies, and HSV-1/HSV-2 DNA in a community-based population in northern Thailand. Serum samples from 779 participants were tested for HBsAg and anti-HCV using rapid immunochromatographic assays, while whole-blood samples from 639 participants were tested for HSV-1 and HSV-2 DNA using real-time PCR. HBsAg and anti-HCV were detected in 3.0% (23/779) and 1.8% (14/779) of participants, respectively. HSV-1 and HSV-2 DNA were each detected in 0.5% (3/639) of whole-blood samples. In the regression analysis, higher body weight was associated with lower odds of HBsAg detection (AOR = 0.955, 95% CI: 0.929–0.982; p = 0.001). These findings provide updated community-based epidemiological data on HBV, HCV, and HSV in northern Thailand. Because HSV DNA was detected infrequently and sequence confirmation was limited, the HSV findings should be interpreted with caution. Further studies involving larger populations, quantitative viral-load measurements, and longitudinal sampling are needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Blood-borne viral infections remain important public health concerns worldwide. The study aimed to provide updated community-based epidemiological data on HBV, HCV, and HSV in northern Thailand.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study investigated HBsAg, anti-HCV antibodies, and HSV-1/HSV-2 DNA in a community-based population in northern Thailand. Serum samples from 779 participants were tested for HBsAg and anti-HCV, while whole-blood samples from 639 participants were tested for HSV-1 and HSV-2 DNA.
Core methods
Serum samples from 779 participants were tested for HBsAg and anti-HCV using rapid immunochromatographic assays, while whole-blood samples from 639 participants were tested for HSV-1 and HSV-2 DNA using real-time PCR. In the regression analysis, higher body weight was associated with lower odds of HBsAg detection (AOR = 0.955, 95% CI: 0.929–0.982; p = 0.001).
Main results
HBsAg and anti-HCV were detected in 3.0% (23/779) and 1.8% (14/779) of participants, respectively. HSV-1 and HSV-2 DNA were each detected in 0.5% (3/639) of whole-blood samples.
Interpretation and novelty
Because HSV DNA was detected infrequently and sequence confirmation was limited, the HSV findings should be interpreted with caution.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and prevention strategies for HBV and HCV in northern Thailand. They also highlight the need for further studies involving larger populations, quantitative viral-load measurements, and longitudinal sampling.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the infrequency of HSV DNA detection and the lack of sequence confirmation. The study provides moderate confidence in its findings due to the cross-sectional design and the use of standard assays.

血清血小板衍生生长因子-BB 作为 慢性乙型肝炎 患者显着肝纤维化的无创生物标志物:一项横断面研究

Serum platelet-derived growth factor-BB as a noninvasive biomarker of significant liver fibrosis in patients with chronic hepatitis B: a cross-sectional study
作者： Abdelaty Ibrahim, Hassan Elbatea, Hanan Soliman, Shimaa Mahmoud El Sharawy, Ibrahim Fathi Amer, Shimaa Elsharawy
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 是进行性肝纤维化 (LF)、肝硬化和肝细胞癌的主要原因。尽管无创纤维化评估至关重要,但目前的工具具有实用和诊断局限性。血小板源性生长因子-BB (PDGF-BB)——一种肝星状细胞活化的促纤维化介质——可能是一种生物学相关的纤维化生物标志物。本研究检查了血清 PDGF-BB 检测慢性乙型肝炎患者显着纤维化的能力,慢性乙型肝炎患者的纤维化定义为通过振动控制瞬时弹性成像(VCTE;FibroScan)肝脏硬度 > 7 kPa。方法 这项横断面探索性诊断准确性研究包括 90 名在坦塔大学医院和 Kafr El-Sheikh 大学医院就诊的 90 名患有 治疗 的慢性乙型肝炎成人。所有参与者均接受临床评估;常规实验室检测,包括 HBsAg、HBeAg 和血清 HBV DNA;腹部超声检查;ELISA测定血清PDGF-BB;和 FibroScan 检查。VCTE 定义的显着纤维化 (F2–F4) 被设置为肝脏硬度 > 7 kPa。使用非随机方便抽样方法来招募参与者,并根据从该 队列研究 得出的 ROC 曲线建立最佳 PDGF-BB 截止值。结果 90例患者中,F0-F1期纤维化69例(76.7%),F2-F4期纤维化21例(23.3%)。21.1% 的患者 HBeAg 呈阳性,中位 HBV DNA 为 4.47 (3.68–5.31) log10 IU/mL F2-F4 患者血清 PDGF-BB 中位值显着高于 F0-F1 纤维化患者:2500.8 (2028.25-2721.8) vs. 777.13分别为 (479.21-1743.15) pg/mL (p < 0.001)。血清 PDGF-BB 以 ≥ 1885.9 pg/mL 的探索性内部衍生临界值区分 VCTE 定义的显着纤维化,AUC 为 0.905 (95% CI 0.816–0.994),敏感性为 85.7%,特异性为 84.1%,阳性预测值为 62.1%,阴性预测值为 95.1%(在23.3% 患病率 of this 队列研究),总体准确度为 84.4%(Youden 指数 0.698;p < 0.001)。PDGF-BB的AUC在数值上高于APRI(0.717)和GPR(0.715),但在多重比较的Bonferroni校正后这些差异并未达到统计学显着性。它与 FIB-4 (0.780) 没有显着差异,并且没有优于血小板计数 (0.980) 或 INR (0.931)。结论 血清 PDGF-BB 对 治疗 初治 CHB 患者中 VCTE 定义的显着纤维化表现出良好的辨别力。尽管与 患病率 相关,但其阴性预测值为 95%,表明作为分类或辅助生物标志物的潜在实用性。然而,这些发现是初步的,在临床实施或替代弹性成像之前需要外部验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 是进行性肝纤维化 (LF)、肝硬化和肝细胞癌的主要原因。本研究旨在评估血清血小板衍生生长因子-BB (PDGF-BB) 作为检测慢性乙型肝炎患者显着肝纤维化的无创生物标志物。
设计与对象
这项横断面探索性诊断准确性研究包括 90 名在坦塔大学医院和 Kafr El-Sheikh 大学医院就诊的患有慢性乙型肝炎的 90 名 治疗 成人。使用非随机方便抽样方法来招募参与者。
核心方法
所有参与者均接受临床评估;常规实验室检测,包括 HBsAg、HBeAg 和血清 HBV DNA;腹部超声检查;ELISA测定血清PDGF-BB;和 FibroScan 检查。最佳 PDGF-BB 截止值是根据 队列研究 得出的 ROC 曲线确定的。
主要结果
90 名患者中,69 名(76.7%)患有 F0-F1 纤维化,21 名(23.3%)患有 F2-F4 纤维化。F2-F4 纤维化患者的中位血清 PDGF-BB 显着高于 F0-F1 纤维化患者:分别为 2500.8 (2028.25-2721.8) 和 777.13 (479.21-1743.15) pg/mL (p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
血清 PDGF-BB 对 治疗 初治 CHB 患者中 VCTE 定义的显着纤维化表现出良好的辨别力。尽管与 患病率 相关,但其阴性预测值为 95%,表明作为分类或辅助生物标志物的潜在实用性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,血清 PDGF-BB 可以作为一种有用的分类或辅助工具,用于识别慢性乙型肝炎患者的显着纤维化,从而可能减少侵入性手术的需要。
局限与证据强度
这些发现是初步的,在临床实施或替代弹性成像之前需要外部验证。该研究使用了非随机方便抽样方法,这可能会限制普遍性。

Serum platelet-derived growth factor-BB as a noninvasive biomarker of significant liver fibrosis in patients with chronic hepatitis B: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Abdelaty Ibrahim, Hassan Elbatea, Hanan Soliman, Shimaa Mahmoud El Sharawy, Ibrahim Fathi Amer, Shimaa Elsharawy
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) is a major cause of progressive liver fibrosis (LF), cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Although noninvasive fibrosis assessment is essential, current tools have practical and diagnostic limitations. Platelet-derived growth factor-BB (PDGF-BB)—a profibrogenic mediator of hepatic stellate cell activation—may be a biologically relevant fibrosis biomarker. This study examined the ability of serum PDGF-BB to detect significant fibrosis in CHB patients which is defined as liver stiffness > 7 kPa by vibration-controlled transient elastography (VCTE; FibroScan). Methods This cross-sectional exploratory diagnostic accuracy study included 90 treatment-naive adults with CHB attending Tanta University Hospitals and Kafr El-Sheikh University Hospitals. All participants underwent clinical evaluation; routine laboratory testing, including HBsAg, HBeAg, and serum HBV DNA; abdominal ultrasonography; serum PDGF-BB measurement by ELISA; and FibroScan examination. VCTE-defined significant fibrosis (F2–F4) was set as liver stiffness > 7 kPa. A non-randomized convenience sampling approach was used to enroll participants, and the optimal PDGF-BB cutoff was established based on the ROC curve derived from this cohort. Results Among 90 patients, 69 (76.7%) had F0-F1 fibrosis and 21 (23.3%) had F2-F4 fibrosis. HBeAg was positive in 21.1% of patients and median HBV DNA was 4.47 (3.68–5.31) log10 IU/mL Median serum PDGF-BB was significantly increased in patients with F2-F4 than in those with F0-F1 fibrosis: 2500.8 (2028.25-2721.8) vs. 777.13 (479.21-1743.15) pg/mL, respectively ( p < 0.001). Serum PDGF-BB discriminated VCTE-defined significant fibrosis at an exploratory, internally derived cutoff of ≥ 1885.9 pg/mL, with an AUC of 0.905 (95% CI 0.816–0.994), sensitivity of 85.7%, specificity of 84.1%, positive predictive value of 62.1%, negative predictive value of 95.1% (at the 23.3% prevalence of this cohort), and overall accuracy of 84.4% (Youden index 0.698; p < 0.001). PDGF-BB had a numerically higher AUC than APRI (0.717) and GPR (0.715), but these differences did not reach statistical significance after Bonferroni correction for multiple comparisons. It did not differ significantly from FIB-4 (0.780) and did not outperform platelet count (0.980) or INR (0.931). Conclusions Serum PDGF-BB demonstrated good discrimination for VCTE-defined significant fibrosis in treatment-naive patients with CHB. Its negative predictive value of 95%, though prevalence-dependent, suggests potential utility as a triage or adjunctive biomarker. However, these findings are preliminary and require external validation before clinical implementation or substitution for elastography.

Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) is a major cause of progressive liver fibrosis (LF), cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma. This study aimed to evaluate serum platelet-derived growth factor-BB (PDGF-BB) as a noninvasive biomarker for detecting significant liver fibrosis in CHB patients.
Design and population
This cross-sectional exploratory diagnostic accuracy study included 90 treatment-naive adults with CHB attending Tanta University Hospitals and Kafr El-Sheikh University Hospitals. A non-randomized convenience sampling approach was used to enroll participants.
Core methods
All participants underwent clinical evaluation; routine laboratory testing, including HBsAg, HBeAg, and serum HBV DNA; abdominal ultrasonography; serum PDGF-BB measurement by ELISA; and FibroScan examination. The optimal PDGF-BB cutoff was established based on the ROC curve derived from this cohort.
Main results
Among 90 patients, 69 (76.7%) had F0-F1 fibrosis and 21 (23.3%) had F2-F4 fibrosis. Median serum PDGF-BB was significantly increased in patients with F2-F4 than in those with F0-F1 fibrosis: 2500.8 (2028.25-2721.8) vs. 777.13 (479.21-1743.15) pg/mL, respectively (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
Serum PDGF-BB demonstrated good discrimination for VCTE-defined significant fibrosis in treatment-naive patients with CHB. Its negative predictive value of 95%, though prevalence-dependent, suggests potential utility as a triage or adjunctive biomarker.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that serum PDGF-BB could serve as a useful triage or adjunctive tool for identifying significant fibrosis in CHB patients, potentially reducing the need for invasive procedures.
Limitations and evidence strength
These findings are preliminary and require external validation before clinical implementation or substitution for elastography. The study used a non-randomized convenience sampling approach, which may limit generalizability.

与 慢性乙型肝炎 中聚乙二醇化干扰素 治疗 反应相关的肠道微生物特征:对肠-肝轴的见解

Gut microbial characteristics associated with pegylated interferon treatment response in chronic hepatitis B: insights into the gut–liver axis
作者： Cai-Xia Zheng, Ye-Hong Lin, Yu-Rong Zhang, Hong-Jie Ou, Zhan Yang, Jian-Yong Zeng, Xiu-Lan Xue, Shaohang Cai, Zhang Yurong
摘要中文翻译背景 聚乙二醇化干扰素 (Peg-IFN) 治疗 的反应在 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者中差异很大。肠道微生物群在这种异质性中的作用仍不清楚。本研究旨在表征与 Peg-IFN 反应相关的肠道微生物特征,并探讨它们与肠-肝轴的潜在相关性。方法 共有 64 例完成 24 周 Peg-IFN 治疗的慢性乙型肝炎患者纳入本研究。使用 16S rRNA 基因测序对第 24 周收集的粪便样本进行分析。评估了与 治疗 反应相关的微生物特征和临床因素,然后开发了综合分类模型。结果 64 例患者中,38 例为有反应者,26 例为无反应者。应答者表现出独特的肠道微生物特征,其特征是微生物丰富度增加(p = 0.014)、群落组成改变(p = 0.045)和不同的微生物相互作用模式。Dorea 丰度在应答者中丰富,并且与 治疗 应答独立相关。多变量逻辑回归确定较低的基线 HBsAg 水平(OR = 0.562,p = 0.048)、较高的血小板计数(OR = 1.020,p = 0.007)和增加的 Dorea 丰度(OR = 3.876,p = 0.008)作为与缓解相关的独立因素。受限三次样条分析支持 Dorea 丰度与响应概率之间的显着关联。整合这些因素的病毒学-宿主-微生物 (VHM) 三联体模型在 队列研究 的开发中证明了有希望的明显区分 (ROC-AUC = 0.790) 和校准。结论 不同的肠道微生物生态模式与 CHB 中的 Peg-IFN 治疗 反应相关,其中 Dorea 代表了一种典型的微生物特征。这项研究为理解干扰素反应的异质性提供了一个多维框架,并强调了肠-肝-免疫轴的潜在相关性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在表征 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 中与 Peg-IFN 反应相关的肠道微生物特征,解决 CHB 患者中 治疗 反应的异质性。
设计与对象
共有 64 名 CHB 患者完成了 24 周的基于 Peg-IFN 的治疗,纳入了这项研究。
核心方法
使用 16S rRNA 基因测序分析第 24 周收集的粪便样本,并通过多变量逻辑回归确定与反应相关的因素。
主要结果
在 64 名患者中,38 名被归类为有反应者,26 名被归类为无反应者。反应者表现出独特的肠道微生物特征,其特征是微生物丰富度增加和群落组成改变。Dorea 丰度在应答者中丰富,并且与 治疗 应答独立相关。
解释与创新
该研究为理解干扰素反应的异质性提供了一个多维框架,并强调了肠-肝-免疫轴的潜在相关性,其中 Dorea 代表了一种典型的微生物特征。
科研与公卫意义
通过将微生物和临床因素整合到预测模型中,这些发现可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 提供 CHB 的信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究提供了一个有前途的框架,但也有局限性,包括样本量小以及需要在独立的 队列研究 中进行验证。

Gut microbial characteristics associated with pegylated interferon treatment response in chronic hepatitis B: insights into the gut–liver axis

Authors: Cai-Xia Zheng, Ye-Hong Lin, Yu-Rong Zhang, Hong-Jie Ou, Zhan Yang, Jian-Yong Zeng, Xiu-Lan Xue, Shaohang Cai, Zhang Yurong
Original abstract (English)
Background Pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) treatment response varies considerably among patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The role of the gut microbiota in this heterogeneity remains unclear. This study aimed to characterize gut microbial features associated with Peg-IFN response and explore their potential relevance to the gut–liver axis. Methods A total of 64 patients with CHB who completed 24 weeks of Peg-IFN-based therapy were enrolled in this study. Fecal samples collected at week 24 were analyzed using 16S rRNA gene sequencing. Microbial characteristics and clinical factors associated with treatment response were evaluated, followed by development of an integrated classification model. Results Among the 64 patients, 38 were classified as responders and 26 as non-responders. Responders exhibited distinct gut microbial profiles, characterized by increased microbial richness ( p = 0.014), altered community composition ( p = 0.045), and different microbial interaction patterns. Dorea abundance was enriched in responders and independently associated with treatment response. Multivariable logistic regression identified lower baseline HBsAg levels (OR = 0.562, p = 0.048), higher platelet counts (OR = 1.020, p = 0.007), and increased Dorea abundance (OR = 3.876, p = 0.008) as independent factors associated with response. Restricted cubic spline analysis supported a significant association between Dorea abundance and response probability. The Virological–Host–Microbial (VHM) triad model integrating these factors demonstrated promising apparent discrimination (ROC-AUC = 0.790) and calibration in the development cohort. Conclusion Distinct gut microbial ecological patterns are associated with Peg-IFN treatment response in CHB, with Dorea representing a characteristic microbial feature. This study provides a multidimensional framework for understanding heterogeneity in interferon responsiveness and highlights the potential relevance of the gut–liver–immune axis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize gut microbial features associated with Peg-IFN response in chronic hepatitis B (CHB), addressing the heterogeneity in treatment response among patients with CHB.
Design and population
A total of 64 patients with CHB who completed 24 weeks of Peg-IFN-based therapy were enrolled in this study.
Core methods
Fecal samples collected at week 24 were analyzed using 16S rRNA gene sequencing, and multivariable logistic regression identified factors associated with response.
Main results
Among the 64 patients, 38 were classified as responders and 26 as non-responders. Responders exhibited distinct gut microbial profiles, characterized by increased microbial richness and altered community composition. Dorea abundance was enriched in responders and independently associated with treatment response.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides a multidimensional framework for understanding heterogeneity in interferon responsiveness and highlights the potential relevance of the gut–liver–immune axis, with Dorea representing a characteristic microbial feature.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of CHB by integrating microbial and clinical factors into a predictive model.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study provides a promising framework but has limitations, including a small sample size and the need for validation in independent cohorts.

慢性乙型肝炎 中 PD-1 阻断和 PEG-IFNα 治疗期间不同的血浆蛋白质组轨迹与 HBsAg 反应相关

Distinct plasma proteomic trajectories during PD-1 blockade and PEG-IFNα therapy are associated with HBsAg response in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Hong-Min Wang, Ya-Ping Li, Xiao-Ying Li, Xiao-Ning Zhang, Qing Wang, Shan-Quan Liu, Sheng-Xian Yang, Xue-Shan Qiao, Shuang-Suo Dang, Fu-Sheng Wang
摘要中文翻译背景 PD-1 阻断和聚乙二醇化干扰素-α (PEG-IFNα) 是 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的有前途的免疫调节策略,但它们的全身免疫效应和反应相关的蛋白质组学模式仍不清楚。方法 我们使用 Olink Target 96 Inflammation panel 对 25 名接受 PD-1 阻断 (n=10) 或 PEG-IFNα (n=15) 的核苷(酸)类似物抑制的 CHB 患者进行了纵向血浆蛋白质组分析。在基线、第 12 周和第 24 周收集血浆样本。使用线性混合效应模型分析纵向蛋白质变化,并使用有反应者与无反应者比较和 Spearman 等级相关性评估与第 24 周 HBsAg 下降的关联。有反应者的定义是第 24 周时 HBsAg 下降≥1 log 10 IU/mL。结果 77 种蛋白质通过了质量控制标准。与 PD-1 阻断相比,PEG-IFNα 诱导了更广泛的纵向蛋白质组重塑,大多数变化发生在前 12 周内。治疗 按时间分析确定了治疗之间不同的轨迹,包括 IL-18R1、TNF、CX3CL1、CXCL9 和 CXCL10 的差异变化。响应相关分析表明 治疗 特异性模式:IL-10 是 PD-1 队列研究 中的主要探索信号,而 IL-12B、CDCP1 和 FGF-21 在 PEG-IFNα 队列研究 中更为突出。这些与反应相关的关联在错误发现率校正后并不仍然显着,因此被认为是探索性的。结论 PD-1 阻断和 PEG-IFNα 在 CHB 中诱导不同的纵向循环炎症蛋白质组程序。有利的 HBsAg 下降伴随着 治疗 特异性而非普遍的蛋白质组学特征,支持纵向蛋白质组学对于 HBV 功能治疗策略的机制和生物标志物研究的潜在价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在明确 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 中 PD-1 阻断和 PEG-IFNα 的全身免疫效应和反应相关的蛋白质组模式,满足更好地了解这些免疫调节策略的需要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 25 名核苷类似物抑制的 CHB 患者,其中 10 名接受 PD-1 阻断,15 名接受 PEG-IFNα,并在基线、第 12 周和第 24 周收集血浆样本。
核心方法
使用 Olink Target 96 Inflammation panel 进行血浆蛋白质组分析,并使用线性混合效应模型进行纵向分析以及应答者与无应答者的比较。
主要结果
77 种蛋白质通过了质量控制标准,显示出 PD-1 阻断和 PEG-IFNα 治疗 之间明显的纵向蛋白质组学重塑,其中大多数变化发生在前 12 周内。
解释与创新
该研究确定了 PD-1 阻断和 PEG-IFNα 之间不同的蛋白质组轨迹,以及响应相关分析中的 治疗 特异性模式,表明机械和生物标志物研究的潜在价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用纵向蛋白质组学来了解 CHB 治疗 策略中的机制并识别反应生物标志物。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到响应相关分析的探索性和小样本量的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。

Distinct plasma proteomic trajectories during PD-1 blockade and PEG-IFNα therapy are associated with HBsAg response in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Hong-Min Wang, Ya-Ping Li, Xiao-Ying Li, Xiao-Ning Zhang, Qing Wang, Shan-Quan Liu, Sheng-Xian Yang, Xue-Shan Qiao, Shuang-Suo Dang, Fu-Sheng Wang
Original abstract (English)
Background PD-1 blockade and pegylated interferon-α (PEG-IFNα) are promising immunomodulatory strategies for chronic hepatitis B (CHB), but their systemic immune effects and response-associated proteomic patterns remain poorly defined. Methods We performed longitudinal plasma proteomic profiling using the Olink Target 96 Inflammation panel in 25 nucleos(t)ide analogue-suppressed patients with CHB receiving PD-1 blockade (n=10) or PEG-IFNα (n=15). Plasma samples were collected at baseline, week 12, and week 24. Longitudinal protein changes were analyzed using linear mixed-effects models, and associations with week-24 HBsAg decline were evaluated using responder–non-responder comparisons and Spearman’s rank correlation. Responders were defined by an HBsAg decline of ≥1 log 10 IU/mL at week 24. Results Seventy-seven proteins passed quality-control criteria. PEG-IFNα induced substantially broader longitudinal proteomic remodeling than PD-1 blockade, with most changes occurring within the first 12 weeks. Treatment-by-time analyses identified distinct trajectories between therapies, including differential changes in IL-18R1, TNF, CX3CL1, CXCL9, and CXCL10. Response-associated analyses suggested treatment-specific patterns: IL-10 was the leading exploratory signal in the PD-1 cohort, whereas IL-12B, CDCP1, and FGF-21 were more prominent in the PEG-IFNα cohort. These response-related associations did not remain significant after false discovery rate correction and were therefore considered exploratory. Conclusions PD-1 blockade and PEG-IFNα induce distinct longitudinal circulating inflammatory proteomic programs in CHB. Favorable HBsAg decline was accompanied by treatment-specific rather than universal proteomic signatures, supporting the potential value of longitudinal proteomics for mechanistic and biomarker studies of HBV functional-cure strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to define the systemic immune effects and response-associated proteomic patterns of PD-1 blockade and PEG-IFNα in chronic hepatitis B (CHB), addressing the need for better understanding of these immunomodulatory strategies.
Design and population
The study involved 25 nucleos(t)ide analogue-suppressed patients with CHB, 10 receiving PD-1 blockade and 15 receiving PEG-IFNα, with plasma samples collected at baseline, week 12, and week 24.
Core methods
Plasma proteomic profiling was conducted using the Olink Target 96 Inflammation panel, with longitudinal analysis using linear mixed-effects models and responder–non-responder comparisons.
Main results
Seventy-seven proteins passed quality-control criteria, showing distinct longitudinal proteomic remodeling between PD-1 blockade and PEG-IFNα treatments, with most changes occurring within the first 12 weeks.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified distinct proteomic trajectories between PD-1 blockade and PEG-IFNα, with treatment-specific patterns in response-associated analyses, suggesting potential value for mechanistic and biomarker studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of longitudinal proteomics for understanding the mechanisms and identifying biomarkers of response in CHB treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the exploratory nature of the response-associated analyses and the small sample size, which may affect the generalizability of the findings.

患病率 乌干达 Kawempe 国家转诊医院住院分娩妇女的保护性乙型肝炎表面抗体水平及相关因素:一项横断面研究

Prevalence of protective hepatitis B surface antibody levels and associated factors among women admitted for delivery at Kawempe National Referral Hospital, Uganda: A cross-sectional study
作者： EMILLY KEMBABAZI, MOSES OCAN, HOOD IBANDA
摘要中文翻译撒哈拉以南非洲地区的母婴 传播 承受着 传播 (HBV) 感染的负担,但人们对在乌干达分娩的妇女是否受到保护知之甚少。我们评估了坎帕拉 Kawempe 国家转诊医院收治分娩的妇女的保护性乙型肝炎表面抗体(抗 HBs)水平的 患病率,描述了血清学特征并确定了相关因素。在这项横断面研究中,2024 年通过系统随机抽样选出了 418 名女性,并就社会人口特征、乙型肝炎检测、疫苗接种 和暴露情况进行了访谈。通过电化学发光免疫分析法对抗-HBs进行定量(保护性:≥10 mIU/mL);其他 HBV 标志物通过快速检测进行筛查。患病率 比率是用修正的泊松回归估计的。总体而言,264 名女性 (63.2%) 报告了 疫苗接种:203 名女性在当前怀孕期间报告了 疫苗接种,61 名女性仅在怀孕之前报告了 疫苗接种。在 322 名女性中发现了保护水平(77.0%;95% 置信区间 [CI] 72.8–80.8):50.6% 的从未接种过疫苗的女性,82.0% 的女性仅在当前怀孕前接种过疫苗,95.6% 的女性在怀孕期间接种过疫苗。调整后,疫苗接种 仅在当前怀孕之前(调整后的 患病率 比率 1.54;95% CI 1.24–1.89)和怀孕期间(1.86;95% CI 1.57–2.20)与保护相关;没有社会人口或行为因素。82 名女性(19.6%)没有免疫或感染的血清学证据;65 人从未接种过疫苗。在 300 名报告先前检测呈阴性的女性中,有 50 名 (16.7%) 从未接种过疫苗,而在已知完成情况的 250 名已接种疫苗的女性中,有 68 名 (27.2%) 没有完成三剂疫苗,主要是因为她们没有被告知返回或忘记。快速检测在 21 名女性 (5.0%) 中检测到了乙型肝炎表面抗原,但不一致的标记模式表明存在一些误报。五分之一的女性在分娩时容易感染乙肝病毒,这主要是因为 疫苗接种 未进行阴性检测。将每个阴性产前测试与 疫苗接种 联系起来可以在很大程度上缩小这一差距。

问题与背景
撒哈拉以南非洲地区的母婴 传播 承受着 传播 (HBV) 感染的负担,但人们对在乌干达分娩的妇女是否受到保护知之甚少。

Prevalence of protective hepatitis B surface antibody levels and associated factors among women admitted for delivery at Kawempe National Referral Hospital, Uganda: A cross-sectional study

Authors: EMILLY KEMBABAZI, MOSES OCAN, HOOD IBANDA
Original abstract (English)
Mother-to-child transmission sustains the burden of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in sub-Saharan Africa, yet little is known about whether women giving birth in Uganda are protected. We estimated the prevalence of protective hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) levels, described serological profiles and identified associated factors among women admitted for delivery at Kawempe National Referral Hospital, Kampala. In this cross-sectional study, 418 women were selected by systematic random sampling in 2024 and interviewed about socio-demographic characteristics, hepatitis B testing, vaccination and exposures. Anti-HBs was quantified by electrochemiluminescence immunoassay (protective: ≥10 mIU/mL); other HBV markers were screened with a rapid test. Prevalence ratios were estimated with modified Poisson regression. Overall, 264 women (63.2%) reported vaccination: 203 during the current pregnancy and 61 before it only. Protective levels were found in 322 women (77.0%; 95% confidence interval [CI] 72.8–80.8): 50.6% of never-vaccinated women, 82.0% of those vaccinated before the current pregnancy only and 95.6% of those vaccinated during it. After adjustment, vaccination before the current pregnancy only (adjusted prevalence ratio 1.54; 95% CI 1.24–1.89) and during it (1.86; 95% CI 1.57–2.20) were associated with protection; no socio-demographic or behavioural factor was. Eighty-two women (19.6%) had no serological evidence of immunity or infection; 65 had never been vaccinated. Of 300 women who reported a previous negative test, 50 (16.7%) had never been vaccinated, and 68 of 250 vaccinated women with known completion status (27.2%) had not completed three doses, mostly because they were not told to return or forgot. The rapid test detected hepatitis B surface antigen in 21 women (5.0%), but discordant marker patterns suggest some false positives. One in five women reached delivery susceptible to HBV, largely because a negative test was not followed by vaccination. Linking every negative antenatal test to vaccination could close much of this gap.

Main results

Interpretation and novelty
After adjustment, vaccination before the current pregnancy only (adjusted prevalence ratio 1.54; 95% CI 1.24–1.89) and during it (1.86; 95% CI 1.57–2.20) were associated with protection; no socio-demographic or behavioural factor was.

继发于 乙型肝炎病毒 重新激活的纵向广泛横贯性脊髓炎,并发急性感觉运动性多发性神经病:病例报告

Longitudinally extensive transverse myelitis with concurrent acute sensorimotor polyneuropathy secondary to hepatitis B virus reactivation: a case report
作者： Saad Shams Bin Shaheen, Wai Kin Su, Jacob George, Natasha Gerbis, Simone Irene Strasser, Salim Maher, Cameron Gofton
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活是免疫抑制治疗公认的并发症。乙型肝炎病毒再激活引起的神经系统并发症很少见,特别是同时累及中枢和周围神经系统。我们报告了一例弥漫性大 B 细胞淋巴瘤接受含利妥昔单抗化疗后 HBV 再激活后出现纵向广泛横贯性脊髓炎 (LETM) 并并发急性感觉运动多发性神经病的病例。一名 58 岁男性,有边缘区淋巴瘤转化为弥漫性大 B 细胞淋巴瘤 (DLBCL) 的病史,接受了基于利妥昔单抗的化疗,并因既往 HBV 暴露而使用恩替卡韦作为 抗病毒药物 预防药物。停止化疗1个月后,恩替卡韦停药。六个月后,他出现病毒学证实的乙型肝炎病毒再激活,随后出现急性进行性下肢无力、感觉障碍、尿潴留和神经性疼痛。神经系统检查显示双侧下肢无力、反射消失、胸部感觉平面振动和本体感觉受损。磁共振成像显示从 C4 延伸至 T10 的非增强纵向广泛 T2 高信号脊髓病变,而神经传导研究显示感觉运动性多发性神经病,伴有轴突异常和提示脱髓鞘的特征。脑脊液显示轻度蛋白质升高并伴有细胞增多,而广泛的感染性、自身免疫性和副肿瘤性研究并未揭示这一点。治疗 静脉注射免疫球蛋白 (IVIG) 联合继续 抗病毒药物 恩替卡韦治疗后,神经功能逐渐恢复,脊髓病变完全放射学消退,功能逐渐改善,尽管轻度远端感觉缺陷仍然存在。HBV 再激活很少会同时出现 LETM 和急性感觉运动性多发性神经病。对于接受含利妥昔单抗化疗后出现急性脊髓神经病的患者,特别是提前停止 抗病毒药物 预防后,在鉴别 诊断 时应考虑 HBV 再激活。早期识别以及联合 抗病毒药物 和免疫调节治疗可能与良好的神经学结果相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活是免疫抑制治疗公认的并发症,而 HBV 再激活引起的神经系统并发症很少见,特别是同时累及中枢和周围神经系统。
设计与对象
我们报告了一例弥漫性大 B 细胞淋巴瘤接受含利妥昔单抗化疗后 HBV 再激活后出现纵向广泛横贯性脊髓炎 (LETM) 并并发急性感觉运动多发性神经病的病例。
核心方法
该患者接受了基于利妥昔单抗的化疗,由于既往乙型肝炎病毒暴露,恩替卡韦作为 抗病毒药物 预防药物。化疗停止一个月后,恩替卡韦停药,六个月后,他出现病毒学证实的乙型肝炎病毒再激活。
主要结果
神经系统检查显示双侧下肢无力、反射消失、胸部感觉平面振动和本体感觉受损。磁共振成像和神经传导研究表明感觉运动性多发性神经病伴有轴突异常和提示脱髓鞘的特征。
解释与创新
HBV 再激活很少会同时出现 LETM 和急性感觉运动性多发性神经病。对于接受含利妥昔单抗化疗后出现急性脊髓神经病的患者,特别是提前停止 抗病毒药物 预防后,在鉴别 诊断 时应考虑 HBV 再激活。
科研与公卫意义
早期识别以及联合 抗病毒药物 和免疫调节治疗可能与良好的神经学结果相关。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体的设计限制或偏差。

Longitudinally extensive transverse myelitis with concurrent acute sensorimotor polyneuropathy secondary to hepatitis B virus reactivation: a case report

Authors: Saad Shams Bin Shaheen, Wai Kin Su, Jacob George, Natasha Gerbis, Simone Irene Strasser, Salim Maher, Cameron Gofton
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a recognised complication of immunosuppressive therapy. Neurological complications from HBV reactivation are rare, particularly concurrent involvement of the central and peripheral nervous systems. We report a case of longitudinally extensive transverse myelitis (LETM) with concurrent acute sensorimotor polyneuropathy following HBV reactivation after rituximab containing chemotherapy for diffuse large B-cell lymphoma. A 58 year old man with a history of marginal zone lymphoma transformed to diffuse large B-cell lymphoma (DLBCL) received rituximab based chemotherapy, with entecavir prescribed as antiviral prophylaxis because of previous HBV exposure. One month following cessation of chemotherapy, entecavir was discontinued. Six months later, he developed virologically confirmed HBV reactivation followed by acute progressive lower limb weakness, sensory disturbance, urinary retention and neuropathic pain. Neurological examination demonstrated bilateral lower limb weakness, areflexia, and a thoracic sensory level with impaired vibration and proprioception. Magnetic resonance imaging demonstrated a non-enhancing longitudinally extensive T2-hyperintense spinal cord lesion extending from C4 to T10, while nerve conduction studies demonstrated a sensorimotor polyneuropathy with axonal abnormalities and features suggestive of demyelination. Cerebrospinal fluid demonstrated mild protein elevation with pleocytosis, while extensive infectious, autoimmune and paraneoplastic investigations were unrevealing. Treatment with intravenous immunoglobulin (IVIG), alongside continued antiviral therapy with entecavir, was followed by progressive neurological recovery, with complete radiological resolution of the spinal cord lesion and gradual functional improvement, although mild distal sensory deficits persisted. HBV reactivation may rarely present with concurrent LETM and acute sensorimotor polyneuropathy. In patients presenting with acute myeloneuropathy following rituximab containing chemotherapy, particularly after premature discontinuation of antiviral prophylaxis, HBV reactivation should be considered in the differential diagnosis. Early recognition and combined antiviral and immunomodulatory therapy may be associated with favourable neurological outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a recognised complication of immunosuppressive therapy, and neurological complications from HBV reactivation are rare, particularly concurrent involvement of the central and peripheral nervous systems.
Design and population
We report a case of longitudinally extensive transverse myelitis (LETM) with concurrent acute sensorimotor polyneuropathy following HBV reactivation after rituximab containing chemotherapy for diffuse large B-cell lymphoma.
Core methods
The patient received rituximab based chemotherapy, with entecavir prescribed as antiviral prophylaxis because of previous HBV exposure. Entecavir was discontinued one month following cessation of chemotherapy, and six months later, he developed virologically confirmed HBV reactivation.
Main results
Neurological examination demonstrated bilateral lower limb weakness, areflexia, and a thoracic sensory level with impaired vibration and proprioception. Interpretation and novelty

In patients presenting with acute myeloneuropathy following rituximab containing chemotherapy, particularly after premature discontinuation of antiviral prophylaxis, HBV reactivation should be considered in the differential diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations or bias.

糖皮质激素治疗 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝衰竭的疗效和安全性

Efficacy and safety of glucocorticoid therapy for hepatitis B virus-related liver failure
作者： Jie Sun, Shi-Hong Yu, Xiao-Man Yang, Shi-Yu Fang, Feng-Jun Liu, Qi-Rong Zhu, 杨晓曼
摘要中文翻译目的 评估短期糖皮质激素治疗早期或前期 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝衰竭患者的临床疗效和安全性。方法 回顾性分析2015年1月至2025年12月期间在我中心治疗的143例早期或前期HBV相关肝衰竭患者。其中43例接受甲泼尼龙常规治疗(糖皮质激素组),100例仅接受常规治疗(常规组)。我们分析了两组在最初 4 周内总胆红素 (TBil)、凝血酶原时间活动 (PTA) 和白蛋白 (ALB) 的趋势,以及 4 周临床改善、12 周 死亡率、并发症和 HBV-DNA 变化。结果糖皮质激素组TBil下降较快,PTA改善较早(均P<0.05)。糖皮质激素组的 4 周临床改善率也显着较高(81.4% vs. 54.0%,P = 0.002),12 周 死亡率 较低(2.3% vs. 糖皮质激素治疗与 4 周改善独立相关(OR = 4.21),并且死亡风险显着降低(OR = 0.074)。总体并发症较少糖皮质激素组(37.2% vs. 基线或第 4 周时 HBV-DNA 水平未观察到显着组间差异(分别为 P = 0.389 和 P = 0.584)。结论 对于早期或前期 HBV 相关性肝衰竭患者,在传统治疗中加入短期甲泼尼龙可加速肝功能恢复,改善短期结局,并减少 死亡率,且无统计学显着增加并发症;4周观察期内未观察到HBV-DNA显着增加。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估短期糖皮质激素治疗对早期或前期 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝衰竭患者的临床疗效和安全性。
设计与对象
对2015年1月至2025年12月期间在该中心治疗的143例早期或前期HBV相关肝衰竭患者进行回顾性分析。其中43例接受甲泼尼龙常规治疗,100例仅接受常规治疗。
核心方法
该研究分析了两组在最初 4 周内总胆红素 (TBil)、凝血酶原时间活动 (PTA) 和白蛋白 (ALB) 的趋势,以及 4 周临床改善、12 周 死亡率、并发症和 HBV-DNA 变化。
主要结果
糖皮质激素组表现出更快的 TBil 下降和更早的 PTA 改善(均 P < 0.05)。该组的 4 周临床改善率也显着较高(81.4% vs. 54.0%,P = 0.002),12 周 死亡率 较低(2.3% vs.)。糖皮质激素组的总体并发症较少(37.2% vs.
解释与创新
作者解释说,在传统疗法中添加短期甲基泼尼松龙可以加速肝功能恢复并改善短期结果,并且不会出现统计学上显着增加的并发症。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,糖皮质激素治疗可能是治疗早期或前期 HBV 相关肝衰竭的重要辅助手段,有可能改善临床结果并减少 死亡率。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到回顾性设计和潜在偏倚的限制,但由于观察到糖皮质激素治疗和改善结果之间存在明确的统计关联,因此证据充足。

Efficacy and safety of glucocorticoid therapy for hepatitis B virus-related liver failure

Authors: Jie Sun, Shi-Hong Yu, Xiao-Man Yang, Shi-Yu Fang, Feng-Jun Liu, Qi-Rong Zhu, 杨晓曼
Original abstract (English)
Objective To assess the clinical efficacy and safety of short-term glucocorticoid therapy in patients with early- or pre-stage hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related liver failure. Methods We retrospectively analyzed 143 patients with early- or pre-stage HBV-related liver failure treated at our center between January 2015 and December 2025. Of these, 43 received methylprednisolone conventional therapy (glucocorticoid group) and 100 received conventional therapy alone (conventional group). We analyzed the trends in total bilirubin (TBil), prothrombin time activity (PTA), and albumin (ALB) between the two groups during the initial 4 weeks, as well as 4-week clinical improvement, 12-week mortality, complications, and HBV-DNA changes. Results The glucocorticoid group showed faster TBil decline and earlier PTA improvement (both P < 0.05). Glucocorticoid group also had significantly higher 4-week clinical improvement (81.4% vs. 54.0%, P = 0.002) and lower 12-week mortality (2.3% vs. Glucocorticoid therapy was independently associated with 4-week improvement (OR = 4.21) and with a significantly lower risk of death (OR = 0.074). Overall complications were fewer in the glucocorticoid group (37.2% vs. No significant between-group differences were observed in HBV-DNA levels at baseline or week 4 ( P = 0.389 and P = 0.584, respectively). Conclusion For patients with early or pre-stage HBV-related liver failure, adding short-term methylprednisolone to traditional therapy accelerates the recovery of liver function, improves short-term outcomes, and reduces mortality without a statistically significant increase in complications;no significant increase in HBV-DNA was observed during the 4-week observation period.

The group also had significantly higher 4-week clinical improvement (81.4% vs. 54.0%, P = 0.002) and lower 12-week mortality (2.3% vs. Overall complications were fewer in the glucocorticoid group (37.2% vs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that adding short-term methylprednisolone to traditional therapy accelerates liver function recovery and improves short-term outcomes without a statistically significant increase in complications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that glucocorticoid therapy may be a valuable adjunct in managing early or pre-stage HBV-related liver failure, potentially improving clinical outcomes and reducing mortality.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for bias, but the evidence is strong due to the clear statistical associations observed between glucocorticoid therapy and improved outcomes.

乙型肝炎病毒 感染的时间分辨蛋白质组学分析

Time-Resolved Proteomic Analysis of Hepatitis B Virus Infection
作者： Mohammed Akbor Akbor, Medhi Boutasbih, Alexander Koenig, Eunji Jo, Iolanda Vendrell, Svenja S. Hester, Senko Tsukuda, Monica M. Olcina, Marc Peter Windisch, Jane A. McKeating
摘要中文翻译摘要 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染是慢性肝病和肝细胞癌的主要原因,但其对早期感染期间 宿主 蛋白质组动态的影响仍不清楚。在这里,我们进行了时间分辨无标记定量(LFQ)蛋白质组学来表征人 HepG2 肝癌细胞中 宿主 对 HBV 感染的反应。在感染前 3 周的 5 个时间点,我们定量了 5365 种独特的蛋白质,包括两种 HBV 蛋白质(C/preC 衍生的和 S),揭示了肝细胞蛋白质组中微妙但协调的变化。早期感染的特点是细胞骨架组织、细胞外基质成分和粘斑途径的选择性改变,以及补体相关蛋白的富集。值得注意的是,我们发现补体成分 C5 丰度出现短暂、特异性增加,而 C3 没有相应变化,表明感染期间补体途径成员的差异调节。这些发现定义了 HBV-宿主 相互作用的时间解析蛋白质组学景观,并揭示了先前被低估的补体动态,与选择性和受限的 宿主 反应一致。总之,我们的结果为早期病毒感染和 宿主 适应的机制研究提供了框架。

乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染是慢性肝病和肝细胞癌的主要原因,但其对早期感染期间 宿主 蛋白质组动态的影响仍不清楚。
设计与对象
在这里,我们进行了时间分辨无标记定量(LFQ)蛋白质组学来表征人 HepG2 肝癌细胞中 宿主 对 HBV 感染的反应。
核心方法

主要结果
早期感染的特点是细胞骨架组织、细胞外基质成分和粘斑途径的选择性改变,以及补体相关蛋白的富集。

值得注意的是,我们发现补体成分 C5 丰度出现短暂、特异性增加,而 C3 没有相应变化,表明感染期间补体途径成员的差异调节。

Time-Resolved Proteomic Analysis of Hepatitis B Virus Infection

Authors: Mohammed Akbor Akbor, Medhi Boutasbih, Alexander Koenig, Eunji Jo, Iolanda Vendrell, Svenja S. Hester, Senko Tsukuda, Monica M. Olcina, Marc Peter Windisch, Jane A. McKeating
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a major cause of chronic liver disease and hepatocellular carcinoma, yet its impact on host proteome dynamics during early infection remains poorly defined. Here, we performed time-resolved label-free quantitative (LFQ) proteomics to characterize the host response to HBV infection in human HepG2 hepatoma cells. Across five time points spanning the first 3 weeks of infection, we quantified 5365 unique proteins, including two HBV proteins (C/preC-derived and S), revealing subtle yet coordinated changes in the hepatocellular proteome. Early infection was characterized by selective alterations in cytoskeletal organization, extracellular matrix components, and focal adhesion pathways, alongside enrichment of complement-associated proteins. Notably, we identified a transient, specific increase in the abundance of complement component C5 without a corresponding change in C3, indicating differential regulation of complement pathway members during infection. These findings define a temporally resolved proteomic landscape of HBV–host interactions and uncover previously underappreciated complement dynamics consistent with a selective and restrained host response. Together, our results provide a framework for mechanistic studies of early viral infection and host adaptation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a major cause of chronic liver disease and hepatocellular carcinoma, yet its impact on host proteome dynamics during early infection remains poorly defined.
Design and population

Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌中 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴超出程序性细胞死亡蛋白 1

CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis beyond programmed cell death protein 1 in hepatitis B virus-associated hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Hai Nguyen Ngoc Dang, Hau Phuc Tran, Hung Minh Vu, Trung Tran Nguyen, Bich Ngoc Hoang Thi, Thanh Van Ho, Adegbesan Abiodun Christopher, Hung Tran Xuan, Thang Viet Luong
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝细胞癌在慢性炎症、耐受性肝脏微环境中发生。在这种情况下,T 细胞功能障碍限制了程序性死亡 1 (PD-1) 阻断的功效。Liu 等人发表在《世界胃肠肿瘤学杂志》上的新证据表明,CD161 可能代表耗尽的 CD8+ T 细胞内的另一个抑制轴,该轴未被 PD-1/程序性死亡配体-1 通路完全捕获。在他们的研究中,CD161+CD8+T细胞在肿瘤组织中富集,即使PD-1+区室在PD-1抑制后收缩,CD161表达仍然持续,这表明CD161介导的抑制可能在治疗压力下保持活跃。从机制上讲,CD161-凝集素样转录物 1 相互作用的持续参与可能会损害 T 细胞效应器功能,并有助于 HBV 感染肝脏中的适应性免疫抵抗。这些发现强调了 CD161 蛋白质水平评估和 PD-1 平行评估对于准确表征免疫抑制的重要性。关键知识差距包括肿瘤微环境中凝集素样转录物 1 的细胞来源、持续的 HBV 病毒学活性对 CD161+ T 细胞亚群的影响,以及临床有意义的进展终点的识别。在这篇社论中,我们将这些发现置于机制和转化背景中,强调了 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴作为 PD-1 阻断之外的免疫抵抗的补充途径的潜在作用。总的来说,当前的证据支持谨慎的、假设驱动的转化策略,以评估精心挑选的 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者中 CD161 和 PD-1 轴的联合调节。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 CD161 在 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 T 细胞功能障碍中的作用,解决当前免疫疗法(例如 PD-1 阻断)的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究旨在调查 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者肿瘤组织中的 CD161 凝集素样转录物 1 轴。
核心方法
该研究涉及分析 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者的肿瘤组织样本,重点关注 CD161 表达及其与 PD-1 的相互作用。
主要结果
CD161+CD8+T细胞在肿瘤组织中富集,即使在PD-1抑制后CD161表达仍然持续,表明持续的免疫抵抗。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CD161 介导的抑制可能在治疗压力下保持活跃,凸显了在 HBV 相关肝细胞癌中评估 CD161 与 PD-1 的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 HBV 相关肝细胞癌患者的 CD161 和 PD-1 轴的联合调节策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
主要局限性包括需要进一步研究凝集素样转录物 1 的细胞来源以及持续的 HBV 病毒学活性对 CD161+ T 细胞亚群的影响。

CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis beyond programmed cell death protein 1 in hepatitis B virus-associated hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Hai Nguyen Ngoc Dang, Hau Phuc Tran, Hung Minh Vu, Trung Tran Nguyen, Bich Ngoc Hoang Thi, Thanh Van Ho, Adegbesan Abiodun Christopher, Hung Tran Xuan, Thang Viet Luong
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV)-associated hepatocellular carcinoma develops in a chronically inflamed, tolerogenic liver microenvironment. In this setting, T-cell dysfunction limits the efficacy of programmed death-1 (PD-1) blockade. Emerging evidence from Liu et al published in the World Journal of Gastrointestinal Oncology suggests that CD161 may represent an additional inhibitory axis within exhausted CD8+ T cells that is not fully captured by the PD-1/ programmed death ligand-1 pathway. In their study, CD161+CD8+ T cells were enriched in tumor tissue, and CD161 expression persisted even as PD-1+ compartments contracted after PD-1 inhibition, suggesting that CD161-mediated restraint may remain active under therapeutic pressure. Mechanistically, sustained engagement of the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 interaction may impair T-cell effector function and contribute to adaptive immune resistance in the HBV-infected liver. These findings highlight the importance of protein-level assessment of CD161 and parallel evaluation with PD-1 to accurately characterize immune inhibition. Key knowledge gaps include the cellular source of lectin-like transcript 1 within the tumor microenvironment, the influence of ongoing HBV virological activity on CD161+ T-cell subsets, and the identification of clinically meaningful progression endpoints. In this editorial, we place these findings in mechanistic and translational context, highlighting the potential role of the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis as a complementary pathway of immune resistance beyond PD-1 blockade. Collectively, the current evidence supports a cautious, hypothesis-driven translational strategy to evaluate combined modulation of the CD161 and PD-1 axes in carefully selected patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the role of CD161 in T-cell dysfunction in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma, addressing the limitations of current immunotherapies such as PD-1 blockade.
Design and population
The study was conducted to investigate the CD161-lectin-like transcript 1 axis in tumor tissue from patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Core methods
The study involved analyzing tumor tissue samples from patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma, focusing on CD161 expression and its interaction with PD-1.
Main results
CD161+CD8+ T cells were enriched in tumor tissue, and CD161 expression persisted even after PD-1 inhibition, suggesting ongoing immune resistance.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CD161-mediated restraint may remain active under therapeutic pressure, highlighting the importance of evaluating CD161 alongside PD-1 in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform the development of combined modulation strategies targeting both CD161 and PD-1 axes in patients with HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
Key limitations include the need for further investigation into the cellular source of lectin-like transcript 1 and the influence of ongoing HBV virological activity on CD161+ T-cell subsets.

复合淋巴结靶向纳米佐剂 NA@DCM 整合 TLR9 和线粒体代谢重编程以增强乙型肝炎 疫苗接种

A composite lymph node-targeting nanoadjuvant NA@DCM integrates TLR9 and mitochondrial metabolic reprogramming for enhanced hepatitis B vaccination
作者： Yi-Ling Zhang, Yu-Han Xie, Wen-Hao Han, Xin-Mou Wu, Yu-Fang Chen, Lei-Lei Wang, 裴丽娜 PEI Li-na, Jing-Wen Fu, Tian-Ying Zhang, Yu‐Chun Lin
摘要中文翻译:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题。尽管乙型肝炎亚基疫苗安全有效,但改进的佐剂可能会进一步增强其免疫原性。我们开发了 NA@DCM,一种铝基复合纳米佐剂,其中包含共价缀合 DSPE-PEG-CpG 1018 (DC) 缀合物的 DSPE-PEG 2000 纳米胶束与纳米铝 (NA) 进行界面组装。我们评估了小鼠模型中引流淋巴结 (dLN) 的生物分布、免疫原性和生物安全性,并通过转录组学、蛋白质印迹和药理学抑制分析检查了 B 细胞激活机制。与游离 CpG 相比,NA@DCM 在 6 小时增强了 dLN 中 CpG 相关的积累,并在 48 小时内具有持续保留和成分共定位。与其单独成分及其物理混合物相比,NA@DCM 增加了抗原特异性 IgG 滴度、IgG2c/IgG1 比率、IFN-γ + /TNF-α + / IL-2 + /IL-4 + CD4 + 和 CD8 + T 细胞以及生发中心反应,并与总 IgG 中的 AS04 样参考制剂相匹配。这些特征与 Th1 偏向性抗体谱一致。从机制上讲,NA@DCM 增强了 Toll 样受体 9 (TLR9​​)–NF-κB 信号传导和线粒体功能,并增加了超氧化物相关荧光、ATP 产生和呼吸链复合物活性。Oligomycin A 对 ATP 合酶的抑制减弱了人 Namalwa 和原代小鼠 B 细胞中的生物能反应和 CD69/CD86 上调,表明线粒体 ATP 合成是 NA@DCM 诱导的 B 细胞激活所必需的。NA@DCM 在体外表现出良好的血液相容性,在体内无明显的急性毒性;重复免疫诱导早期细胞因子反应和局部浸润,但未检测到肝脏或肾脏异常。总的来说,NA@DCM 整合了淋巴结靶向递送、TLR9 相关信号传导和线粒体生物能调节,支持作为乙型肝炎 疫苗 佐剂的进一步临床前开发。

A composite lymph node-targeting nanoadjuvant NA@DCM integrates TLR9 and mitochondrial metabolic reprogramming for enhanced hepatitis B vaccination

Authors: Yi-Ling Zhang, Yu-Han Xie, Wen-Hao Han, Xin-Mou Wu, Yu-Fang Chen, Lei-Lei Wang, 裴丽娜 PEI Li-na, Jing-Wen Fu, Tian-Ying Zhang, Yu‐Chun Lin
Original abstract (English)
: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global public health concern. Although hepatitis B subunit vaccines are safe and effective, improved adjuvants may further enhance their immunogenicity. We developed NA@DCM, an aluminum-based composite nanoadjuvant in which DSPE-PEG 2000 nanomicelles incorporating a covalently conjugated DSPE-PEG–CpG 1018 (DC) conjugate are interfacially assembled with nano-aluminum (NA). We evaluated draining lymph node (dLN) biodistribution, immunogenicity, and biosafety in murine models, and examined B-cell activation mechanisms by transcriptomic, Western blot, and pharmacological inhibition analyses. NA@DCM enhanced CpG-associated accumulation in dLNs at 6 h versus free CpG, with sustained retention and component colocalization over 48 h. Compared with its individual components and their physical mixture, NA@DCM increased antigen-specific IgG titers, the IgG2c/IgG1 ratio, IFN-γ + /TNF-α + / IL-2 + /IL-4 + CD4 + and CD8 + T cells, and germinal center responses, and matched an AS04-like reference formulation in total IgG. These features are consistent with a Th1-biased antibody profile. Mechanistically, NA@DCM enhanced Toll-like receptor 9 (TLR9)–NF-κB signaling and mitochondrial function, with increased superoxide-associated fluorescence, ATP production, and respiratory chain complex activities . Oligomycin A inhibition of ATP synthase attenuated bioenergetic responses and CD69/CD86 upregulation in human Namalwa and primary murine B cells, indicating mitochondrial ATP synthesis is required for NA@DCM-induced B-cell activation. NA@DCM showed favorable hemocompatibility in vitro and no overt acute toxicity in vivo ; repeated immunization induced early cytokine responses and local infiltration without detectable hepatic or renal abnormalities. Collectively, NA@DCM integrates lymph node-targeted delivery, TLR9-associated signaling, and mitochondrial bioenergetic modulation, supporting further preclinical development as a hepatitis B vaccine adjuvant.

聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 添加到核苷(酸)类似物可减少肝细胞癌和晚期纤维化的失代偿 慢性乙型肝炎

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B
作者： Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
摘要中文翻译背景目前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。然而,其在改善 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者预后方面的价值以及该联合策略的合适候选者仍有待进一步探索。目的 评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。方法 这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了诊断为 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化的患者。参与者被分为两组:(1)接受联合治疗(Peg-IFNα-2b 附加到正在进行的 NA 治疗);(2) 接受 NA 单一疗法的患者。采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。结果 该分析包括 302 对配对,中位随访时间为 220 周。与 NA 单一疗法相比,联合治疗与发生 HCC(风险比 = 0.22;95% CI:0.11-0.48)和失代偿性肝硬化(风险比 = 0.39;95% CI:0.20-0.77)的风险显着降低相关。这些益处在经验证的 HCC 预测模型分类为中高风险的患者以及持续生化异常持续 > 12 个月的患者中尤其明显。结论 Peg-IFNα-2b 与 NA 的附加治疗与患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者的 HCC 减少和失代偿显着相关,支持其在该人群中的使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。当前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了诊断患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化的患者。参与者被分为两组:(1)接受联合治疗(Peg-IFNα-2b 附加到正在进行的 NA 治疗);(2) 接受 NA 单一疗法的患者。
核心方法
采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。
主要结果
该分析包括 302 对配对,中位随访时间为 220 周。与 NA 单一疗法相比,联合治疗与发生 HCC(风险比 = 0.22;95% CI:0.11-0.48)和失代偿性肝硬化(风险比 = 0.39;95% CI:0.20-0.77)的风险显着降低相关。
解释与创新
这些益处在经验证的 HCC 预测模型分类为中高风险的患者以及持续生化异常持续 > 12 个月的患者中尤其明显。Peg-IFNα-2b 与 NA 的附加治疗与患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者的 HCC 减少和代偿失调显着相关,支持其在该人群中的使用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Peg-IFNα-2b 联合治疗可能为减少高危 HBV 患者的 HCC 和失代偿带来重要益处。这可以为未来 治疗 治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 晚期纤维化和肝硬化的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性队列研究研究,可能会引入偏倚和混杂因素。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于倾向评分匹配和分层生存分析。

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure. However, its value in improving prognosis and the suitable candidates for this combination strategy in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis remain to be further explored. AIM To evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. METHODS This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy. A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores. RESULTS The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy. These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. CONCLUSION Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy.
Core methods
A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores.
Main results
The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy.
Interpretation and novelty
These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that combination therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b may offer important benefits for reducing HCC and decompensation in high-risk HBV patients. This could inform future treatment strategies for managing advanced fibrosis and cirrhosis in chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective cohort study, which may introduce bias and confounding factors. The evidence is moderate as it relies on propensity score matching and stratified survival analysis.

罗马尼亚南部人群样本中的 患病率 和 乙型肝炎病毒 感染的流行病学特征:四年回顾性分析(2021-2024 年)

PREVALENCE AND EPIDEMIOLOGICAL PROFILE OF HEPATITIS B VIRUS INFECTION IN A POPULATION SAMPLE FROM SOUTHERN ROMANIA: A FOUR-YEAR RETROSPECTIVE ANALYSIS (2021–2024)
作者： Mariana Luminiţa Olaru
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战。该研究旨在评估罗马尼亚南部人群样本中乙型肝炎病毒感染的流行病学概况。
设计与对象
本回顾性研究使用快速免疫层析方法评估了 2021 年至 2024 年间在亚历山大(泰勒曼县)测试的 5501 名患者样本中的 HBsAg 血清流行率。
核心方法
该研究使用快速免疫色谱法检测 2021 年至 2024 年间来自亚历山大的 5501 名患者的人群样本中的 HBsAg。
主要结果
总体血清流行率为4.45 %(245例),男性显著高于女性(5.62 % vs. 3.99 %,p = 0.0078;OR = 1.44)。年龄分布显示随着年龄增长呈逐步上升趋势,在50–64岁年龄组达到5.68%,而在18岁以下年龄组未记录阳性病例。
解释与创新
研究结果证实了 1995 年启动的国家 HBV 疫苗接种 计划的有效性,因为 18 岁以下年龄组中没有记录到阳性病例。
科研与公卫意义
The results highlight the necessity of continuing population screening campaigns for HBV, as the prevalence increased slightly from 3.95 % in 2022 to 5.04 % in 2024.
局限与证据强度
该研究是基于单一人群样本的回顾性分析,这可能会限制普遍性。由于该研究的观察性质,证据是中等的。

PREVALENCE AND EPIDEMIOLOGICAL PROFILE OF HEPATITIS B VIRUS INFECTION IN A POPULATION SAMPLE FROM SOUTHERN ROMANIA: A FOUR-YEAR RETROSPECTIVE ANALYSIS (2021–2024)

Authors: Mariana Luminiţa Olaru
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health challenge. The present retrospective study evaluated the HBsAg seroprevalence in a sample of 5501 patients tested in Alexandria (Teleorman County) between 2021 and 2024 using rapid immunochromatographic methods. The overall seropositivity rate was 4.45 % (245 cases), with a significantly higher prevalence in males compared to females (5.62 % vs. 3.99 %, p = 0.0078; OR = 1.44). The age distribution showed a progressive increase reaching 5.68% in the 50–64 age group, whereas no positive cases were recorded in the under-18 age group, confirming the effectiveness of thenational HBV vaccination program launched in 1995. Temporal dynamics revealed a slight increase in annual prevalence from 3.95 % in 2022 to 5.04 % in 2024, highlighting the necessity of continuing population screening campaigns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health challenge. The study aimed to evaluate the epidemiological profile of HBV infection in a population sample from Southern Romania.
Design and population
The present retrospective study evaluated the HBsAg seroprevalence in a sample of 5501 patients tested in Alexandria (Teleorman County) between 2021 and 2024 using rapid immunochromatographic methods.
Core methods
The study used rapid immunochromatographic methods to test for HBsAg in a population sample of 5501 patients from Alexandria between 2021 and 2024.
Main results
The overall seropositivity rate was 4.45 % (245 cases), with a significantly higher prevalence in males compared to females (5.62 % vs. 3.99 %, p = 0.0078; OR = 1.44). The age distribution showed a progressive increase reaching 5.68% in the 50–64 age group, whereas no positive cases were recorded in the under-18 age group.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings confirm the effectiveness of the national HBV vaccination program launched in 1995, as no positive cases were recorded in the under-18 age group.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the necessity of continuing population screening campaigns for HBV, as the prevalence increased slightly from 3.95 % in 2022 to 5.04 % in 2024.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective analysis based on a single population sample, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

慢性乙型肝炎 各阶段的生物标志物概况比较

Comparative Biomarker Profiles Across Stages of Chronic Hepatitis B
作者： Devi Rahmadhona, Bambang Purwanto, Aryati
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB)、肝硬化 (LC) 和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 是 HBV 感染的进展阶段,代表着全球健康问题。在许多地区,先进的诊断工具并不容易获得;因此,需要易于使用且具有成本效益的检测方法来区分这些疾病阶段。这项研究比较了 HBV 相关肝病阶段的炎症、生化和病毒学生物标志物概况,包括中性粒细胞与淋巴细胞比率 (NLR)、血小板计数、天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST)、丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT)、AST 与血小板比率指数 (APRI)、纤维化 4 评分 (FIB-4) 和 HBV DNA 水平。一项回顾性横断面研究纳入了 2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间在西努沙登加拉省总医院入组的 213 名慢性 HBV 感染患者,分为 CHB (n=166)、LC (n=27) 和 HCC (n=20)。使用 Kruskal-Wallis 和事后检验进行分析。与 CHB (p=0.0001) 和 LC (p<0.01) 组相比,HCC 组的 NLR 显着较高。血小板计数显示从 CHB 到 LC 逐渐下降 (p<0.0001),但随后从 LC 到 HCC 逐渐增加 (p<0.0001),在 CHB 和 HCC 组之间没有观察到显着差异。天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST) 水平在所有疾病阶段均显着增加,而 ALT 水平没有显着变化。与 CHB 组相比,LC 和 HCC 组的 APRI 和 FIB-4 评分均显着较高 (p<0.0001),但 LC 和 HCC 之间没有差异。HCC 中 HBV DNA 水平升高,但未达到统计学显着性。因此,在缺乏先进诊断设施的环境中,这些可获取的生物标志物可以在监测和分期 HBV 诱发的肝病方面发挥重要作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB)、肝硬化 (LC) 和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 是 HBV 感染的进展阶段,代表着全球健康问题。本研究旨在比较这些阶段的生物标志物概况,以确定可用于疾病分期且具有成本效益的标志物。
设计与对象
一项回顾性横断面研究招募了 2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间在西努沙登加拉省总医院入组的 213 名慢性 HBV 感染患者,分为 CHB (n=166)、LC (n=27) 和 HCC (n=20)。
核心方法
该研究使用 Kruskal-Wallis 和事后检验进行分析。它比较了炎症、生化和病毒学生物标志物,包括中性粒细胞与淋巴细胞比率 (NLR)、血小板计数、天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST)、丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT)、AST 与血小板比率指数 (APRI)、纤维化 4 评分 (FIB-4) 和 HBV DNA 水平。
主要结果
与 CHB (p=0.0001) 和 LC (p<0.01) 组相比,HCC 组的 NLR 显着较高。血小板计数显示从 CHB 到 LC 逐渐下降 (p<0.0001),但随后从 LC 到 HCC 逐渐增加 (p<0.0001),在 CHB 和 HCC 组之间没有观察到显着差异。AST 水平在所有疾病阶段均显着增加,而 ALT 水平没有显着变化。与 CHB 组相比,LC 和 HCC 组的 APRI 和 FIB-4 评分均显着较高 (p<0.0001),但 LC 和 HCC 之间没有差异。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,某些生物标志物,例如 NLR 和 APRI,可能有助于区分疾病阶段,特别是在资源有限、无法获得先进诊断工具的环境中。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为开发可利用的生物标志物提供信息,用于在诊断资源有限的地区监测和分期乙型肝炎引起的肝病。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制。然而,鉴于结果的统计显着性和成熟生物标志物的使用,证据是强有力的。

Comparative Biomarker Profiles Across Stages of Chronic Hepatitis B

Authors: Devi Rahmadhona, Bambang Purwanto, Aryati
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB), liver cirrhosis (LC), and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) are progressive stages of HBV infection that represent a global health concern. In many regions, advanced diagnostic tools are not readily available; therefore, accessible, cost-effective assays are needed to differentiate these disease stages. This study compared inflammatory, biochemical, and virological biomarker profiles, encompassing the neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR), platelet count, aspartate transaminase (AST), alanine transaminase (ALT), AST-to-platelet ratio index (APRI), fibrosis-4 score (FIB-4), and HBV DNA levels, across HBV-related liver disease stages. A retrospective cross-sectional study enrolled 213 patients with chronic HBV infection, categorized as CHB (n=166), LC (n=27), and HCC (n=20) at The General Hospital of West Nusa Tenggara Province between January 2022 and December 2023. The Kruskal-Wallis and post hoc tests were used for the analysis. The NLR was significantly higher in the HCC group compared to both the CHB (p=0.0001) and LC (p<0.01) groups. Platelet count revealed a progressive decline from CHB to LC (p<0.0001) but exhibited a subsequent increase from LC to HCC (p<0.0001), with no significant difference observed between the CHB and HCC groups. Aspartate transaminase (AST) levels increased significantly across all disease stages, whereas ALT levels showed no significant variation. Both APRI and FIB-4 scores were significantly higher in the LC and HCC groups compared to the CHB group (p<0.0001), but did not differ between LC and HCC. HBV DNA levels were elevated in HCC but failed to reach statistical significance. Consequently, these accessible biomarkers could play an important role in monitoring and staging HBV-induced liver disease in settings lacking advanced diagnostic facilities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB), liver cirrhosis (LC), and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) are progressive stages of HBV infection that represent a global health concern. This study aimed to compare biomarker profiles across these stages to identify accessible and cost-effective markers for disease staging.
Design and population
A retrospective cross-sectional study enrolled 213 patients with chronic HBV infection, categorized as CHB (n=166), LC (n=27), and HCC (n=20) at The General Hospital of West Nusa Tenggara Province between January 2022 and December 2023.
Core methods
The study used the Kruskal-Wallis and post hoc tests for analysis. It compared inflammatory, biochemical, and virological biomarkers, including the neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR), platelet count, aspartate transaminase (AST), alanine transaminase (ALT), AST-to-platelet ratio index (APRI), fibrosis-4 score (FIB-4), and HBV DNA levels.
Main results
The NLR was significantly higher in the HCC group compared to both the CHB (p=0.0001) and LC (p<0.01) groups. Platelet count revealed a progressive decline from CHB to LC (p<0.0001) but exhibited a subsequent increase from LC to HCC (p<0.0001), with no significant difference observed between the CHB and HCC groups. AST levels increased significantly across all disease stages, whereas ALT levels showed no significant variation. Both APRI and FIB-4 scores were significantly higher in the LC and HCC groups compared to the CHB group (p<0.0001), but did not differ between LC and HCC.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that certain biomarkers, such as NLR and APRI, may be useful in differentiating disease stages, particularly in resource-limited settings where advanced diagnostic tools are not available.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results could inform the development of accessible biomarkers for monitoring and staging HBV-induced liver disease in areas with limited diagnostic resources.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias. However, the evidence is strong given the statistical significance of the results and the use of well-established biomarkers.

干扰素治疗可降低 乙型肝炎病毒 相关肝细胞癌高风险患者的肝细胞癌 发病率

Interferon therapy reduces incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with high risk of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Miao Hao, Chen-Rui Liu, Meng Zhang, Xia-Qing Zhou, Zi-Cheng Jiang, Xiao-Hong Gao, Xin Zhang, Wen-Jun Wang, Yi-Kai Wang, Xiao-Li Jia
摘要中文翻译背景我国HCC患者中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌(HCC)患者比例高达85.69%。目前的抗HBV药物包括核苷(酸)类似物(NAs)和干扰素(IFN)。目的 比较 NAs 和 IFN 治疗对 HBV 相关 HCC 高危患者 HCC 发生的影响。方法 我们纳入了 368 例 HBV 相关 HCC 高危患者,其中 120 例接受皮下注射 IFN(联合或不联合 NA),构成 IFN 组。其余 248 名患者被分配到 NAs 组,并接受单独 NAs 的 治疗 治疗。结果 对于 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者,经过 5 年 发病率 期后,IFN 组的 HCC 发病率 为 2.54%,而 NAs 组的 发病率 显着较高,为 25.74%(P 结论 与 NAs 相比,基于 IFN 的治疗显着降低了高风险患者的 HCC 风险)乙型肝炎病毒相关的肝癌。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决中国 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的高 发病率 问题,并评估干扰素 (IFN) 治疗与核苷类似物 (NA) 对高危患者 HCC 发生的影响。
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究对 368 名 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者进行,其中 120 名患者接受 IFN 治疗(联合或不联合 NA),248 名患者仅接受 NA 治疗。
核心方法
患者入组并随访 5 年 治疗 期。使用统计分析对 IFN 组和 NAs 组之间的 HCC 发病率 进行监测和比较以确定显着性。
主要结果
治疗治疗5年后,IFN组的HCC 发病率为2.54%,而NAs组的发病率显着较高,为25.74%(P < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明在高危 HBV 患者中,与单独使用 NA 相比,基于 IFN 的治疗可显着降低 HCC 风险,强调了一种新的治疗方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明,基于 IFN 的治疗可能是减少 HBV 相关 HCC 高风险患者的 HCC 发病率 的更有效选择,可能为 治疗 指南和公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制,但由于 IFN 和 NA 组之间的清晰比较以及统计学上显着的结果,证据是强有力的。

Interferon therapy reduces incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with high risk of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Miao Hao, Chen-Rui Liu, Meng Zhang, Xia-Qing Zhou, Zi-Cheng Jiang, Xiao-Hong Gao, Xin Zhang, Wen-Jun Wang, Yi-Kai Wang, Xiao-Li Jia
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The proportion of patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is as high as 85.69% among HCC patients in China. Current anti-HBV drugs include nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) and interferon (IFN). AIM To compare the impact of NAs and IFN therapy on the occurrence of HCC in patients at high risk of HBV-related HCC. METHODS We enrolled 368 patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC, and 120 received subcutaneous injections of IFN, with or without NAs, constituting the IFN group. The remaining 248 patients were assigned to the NAs group, and received treatment with NAs alone. RESULTS For patients with a high risk of HBV-related HCC, after a 5-year treatment period, HCC incidence in the IFN group was 2.54%, while the NAs group showed a significantly higher incidence of 25.74% (P CONCLUSION IFN-based therapy, compared with NAs, significantly reduced the risk of HCC in patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the high incidence of HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) in China and evaluate the impact of interferon (IFN) therapy versus nucleos(t)ide analogs (NAs) on HCC occurrence in high-risk patients.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on 368 patients with high risk of HBV-related HCC, with 120 patients receiving IFN therapy (with or without NAs) and 248 patients receiving NA therapy alone.
Core methods
Patients were enrolled and followed for a 5-year treatment period. HCC incidence was monitored and compared between the IFN group and the NAs group using statistical analysis to determine significance.
Main results
After 5 years of treatment, the HCC incidence in the IFN group was 2.54%, while the NAs group had a significantly higher incidence of 25.74% (P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that IFN-based therapy significantly reduces HCC risk compared to NAs alone in high-risk HBV patients, highlighting a novel therapeutic approach.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results suggest that IFN-based therapy could be a more effective option for reducing HCC incidence in patients at high risk of HBV-related HCC, potentially informing treatment guidelines and public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the clear comparison between IFN and NAs groups and the statistically significant results.

华支睾吸虫共感染增强 慢性乙型肝炎 患者多形核骨髓源性抑制细胞的扩增和代谢重编程

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients
作者： Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
摘要中文翻译背景已知华支睾吸虫(C. sinensis)合并感染会加速乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在两种感染流行的地区。代谢重编程已成为免疫抑制的关键驱动因素。这些代谢变化通常是由骨髓源性抑制细胞(MDSC)精心策划的,这些细胞在慢性感染期间会扩张以促进免疫耐受。中华丝虫合并感染会重新编程代谢以有利于 MDSC 相关的抑制,而这一过程与正常肝酶水平的关系仍不清楚。目的 探讨中华支原体合并感染是否会诱导肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者发生 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。方法 在这项横断面研究中,总共招募了 117 名参与者,并分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、单一感染中华支原体的参与者(n = 29)、单一感染 HBV 的参与者(n = 28)和混合感染的参与者(n = 30)。使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。进行 Spearman 相关性和中介分析来探讨炎症、代谢和免疫之间的关系。结果 HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,与HBV单一感染组相比,合并感染组的炎症细胞因子白细胞介素6(IL-6)和肿瘤坏死因子-α(TNF-α)水平较高,同时干扰素-γ(IFN-γ)水平明显较低。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。在代谢方面,共同感染组的鸟氨酸与精氨酸的比率显着较高。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。中华线虫合并感染通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高与免疫抑制相关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨华支睾吸虫合并感染是否会在肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者中诱导 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。背景强调,已知中华支原体合并感染会加速乙型肝炎患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在流行地区。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究纳入了 117 名参与者,分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、中华支原体单一感染参与者(n = 29)、HBV 单一感染参与者(n = 28)和混合感染参与者(n = 30)。
核心方法
使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。
主要结果
HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,共同感染组的炎症细胞因子:白细胞介素-6 (IL-6) 和肿瘤坏死因子-α (TNF-α) 水平较高,同时干扰素-γ (IFN-γ) 水平明显较低。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,中华线虫合并感染与通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高而产生的免疫抑制有关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。这一发现强调了共感染可能调节 HBV 患者 免疫应答 的新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于了解合并感染 HBV 患者的免疫调节,并可能指导未来基于免疫的治疗和针对合并感染情况的 监测 策略的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了其建立因果关系的能力。证据基于相关性和中介分析,可能无法完全捕捉感染与 免疫应答 之间复杂的相互作用。信心是中等的。

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients

Authors: Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Clonorchis sinensis (C. sinensis ) co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV), particularly in regions where both infections are endemic. Metabolic reprogramming has emerged as a key driver of immunosuppression. These metabolic shifts are often orchestrated by myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs), which expand during chronic infections to promote immune tolerance. sinensis co-infection reprograms metabolism to favor MDSC-linked suppression and how this process relates to normal liver enzyme levels remains unclear. AIM To investigate whether C. sinensis co-infection induces MDSC- linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. METHODS In this cross-sectional study, a total of 117 participants were enrolled and assigned to four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30). Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Spearman correlation and mediation analyses were performed to explore the relationships among inflammation, metabolism, and immunity. RESULTS HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, compared with the HBV mono-infected group, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels. Metabolically, the co-infected group had a significantly higher ornithine-to-arginine ratio. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether Clonorchis sinensis co-infection induces MDSC-linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. The background highlights that C. sinensis co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in HBV patients, particularly in endemic regions.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study enrolled 117 participants divided into four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30).
Core methods
Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels.
Main results
HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that C. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels. This finding highlights a novel mechanism by which co-infection may modulate immune responses in HBV patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the understanding of immune modulation in HBV patients with co-infections, potentially guiding future research on immune-based therapies and surveillance strategies for co-infection scenarios.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, limiting its ability to establish causality. The evidence is based on correlations and mediation analyses, which may not fully capture the complex interactions between infections and immune responses. The confidence is moderate.
