印度尼西亚巴威安岛儿童乙型肝炎血清保护状况

Seroprotection Status Against Hepatitis B among Children on Bawean Island, Indonesia
作者： Bagus Setyoboedi, Rendi Aji Prihaningtyas, Martono Tri Utomo, Arini Haq, Syania Mega Octariyandra, Sjamsul Arief
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,特别是在发展中地区。疫苗接种 显着降低感染率,但血清保护作用可能会随着时间的推移而下降,需要定期评估。
设计与对象
对来自巴威安岛各个村庄的 90 名 3 至 15 岁儿童进行了一项横断面研究。没有受试者对 HBsAg 有反应(0%)。
核心方法
收集血样,使用电化学发光免疫测定法 (ECLIA) 测定乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和乙型肝炎表面抗体 (抗 HBs) 水平。使用 SPSS 25 版进行统计分析,对分类变量使用卡方检验,对连续数据使用 Spearman 相关检验。
主要结果
The overall anti-HBs seroprotection rate among the children was 67.8 %. There was a significant difference between seroprotection status and age (p<0.001). A significant negative correlation was found between age and Anti-HBs titers (r=-0.404, p<0.001).
解释与创新
这项研究表明巴韦安岛儿童存在血清保护。抗 HBs 滴度随年龄的显着下降凸显了该偏远岛屿人群可能需要加强 疫苗接种 策略,以确保长期免疫力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以谨慎地告知偏远人群是否需要加强 疫苗接种 策略,以维持对 HBV 的长期免疫力。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横截面设计和缺乏纵向数据而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它是基于单个时间点评估。

Seroprotection Status Against Hepatitis B among Children on Bawean Island, Indonesia

Authors: Bagus Setyoboedi, Rendi Aji Prihaningtyas, Martono Tri Utomo, Arini Haq, Syania Mega Octariyandra, Sjamsul Arief
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major global public health concern, particularly in developing regions. Vaccination significantly reduces infection rates, but seroprotection may decline over time, necessitating periodic evaluation. This study evaluated the seroprotection rates against hepatitis B and the prevalence of HBV infection among children on Bawean Island, Indonesia. A cross-sectional study was conducted involving 90 children aged 3-15 years from various villages on Bawean Island. Blood samples were collected to determine hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) levels using an Electrochemiluminescence Immunoassay (ECLIA). Seroprotection was defined as anti-HBs titers ≥10 mIU/mL. Statistical analyses were performed using SPSS Version 25, utilizing the chi-square test for categorical variables and the Spearman correlation test for continuous data. None of the subjects were reactive for HBsAg (0%). The overall anti-HBs seroprotection rate among the children was 67.8 %. There was a significant difference between seroprotection status and age (p<0.001). However, no significant difference was found between seroprotection status and sex (p=0.259) or basic immunization history (p=0.627). A significant negative correlation was found between age and Anti-HBs titers (r=-0.404, p<0.001). This study demonstrates the presence of seroprotection among children on Bawean Island. The significant age-dependent decline in anti-HBs titers highlights the potential need for a booster vaccination strategy in this remote island population to ensure long-term immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major global public health concern, particularly in developing regions. Vaccination significantly reduces infection rates, but seroprotection may decline over time, necessitating periodic evaluation.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted involving 90 children aged 3-15 years from various villages on Bawean Island. None of the subjects were reactive for HBsAg (0%).
Core methods
Blood samples were collected to determine hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) levels using an Electrochemiluminescence Immunoassay (ECLIA). Statistical analyses were performed using SPSS Version 25, utilizing the chi-square test for categorical variables and the Spearman correlation test for continuous data.
Main results
The overall anti-HBs seroprotection rate among the children was 67.8 %. There was a significant difference between seroprotection status and age (p<0.001). A significant negative correlation was found between age and Anti-HBs titers (r=-0.404, p<0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
This study demonstrates the presence of seroprotection among children on Bawean Island. The significant age-dependent decline in anti-HBs titers highlights the potential need for a booster vaccination strategy in this remote island population to ensure long-term immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can cautiously inform the need for booster vaccination strategies in remote populations to maintain long-term immunity against HBV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the lack of longitudinal data. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single time-point assessment.

离散 乙型肝炎病毒 模型的混沌控制和分岔

Chaos control and bifurcations of a discrete Hepatitis B virus model

Chaos control and bifurcations of a discrete Hepatitis B virus model

Authors: Abdul Qadeer Khan, Fakhra Bibi
Original abstract (English)
In this paper, we examine the local dynamics, bifurcation sets, basic reproduction number (BRN), chaos, convergence rate and bifurcation analysis at equilibrium states of a four-dimensional discrete HBV model. More specifically, we first give the mathematical formulation of a four-dimensional discrete HBV model, and then local behavior is explored by the linear stability theory. Moreover, at equilibrium states, we have identified the bifurcation sets, and then we have studied the bifurcations at equilibrium states of the discrete HBV model by the bifurcation theory. We have studied chaos and convergence rate for the understudied HBV model. Finally, our theoretical outcomes are confirmed numerically.

乙型肝炎病毒 家族史、基线乙型肝炎表面抗原和表面抗原清除率之间的关联

Associations among family history of hepatitis B virus, baseline hepatitis B surface antigen and surface antigen clearance
作者： Jun Li, Hong-Yue Guo, Yi-Wen Zhang, Qin Du, Jing-Zhe Zhang, Jia-Yin Wang, Chao Yang, Fang Wang, Wei-Li Yin, Yan-Chao Fu

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 乙型肝炎病毒 家族史、基线乙型肝炎表面抗原水平和 慢性乙型肝炎 患者表面抗原清除率之间的关联。
设计与对象
在中国进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,重点关注接受聚乙二醇化干扰素-α 治疗 的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者。该研究纳入了有 乙型肝炎病毒 家族史和基线 HBsAg 水平记录的患者样本。
核心方法
该研究采用回顾性 队列研究 设计,分析 队列研究 患者的医疗记录。它评估了 乙型肝炎病毒 家族史、基线 HBsAg 水平以及聚乙二醇化干扰素-α 治疗 后与 HBsAg 清除相关的结果。
主要结果
研究发现,乙型肝炎病毒 家族史与 HBsAg 清除风险较低显着相关。大约 37% 的这种负相关是由基线 HBsAg 水平升高介导的。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为,有 乙型肝炎病毒 家族史的患者使用聚乙二醇化干扰素-α 实现 HBsAg 清除的可能性可能降低,可能需要较低的基线 HBsAg 水平才能达到与无家族史患者相似的结果。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于根据 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的家族史和基线 HBsAg 水平制定个性化 治疗 策略,从而有可能改善治疗结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对医疗记录的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Associations among family history of hepatitis B virus, baseline hepatitis B surface antigen and surface antigen clearance

Authors: Jun Li, Hong-Yue Guo, Yi-Wen Zhang, Qin Du, Jing-Zhe Zhang, Jia-Yin Wang, Chao Yang, Fang Wang, Wei-Li Yin, Yan-Chao Fu
Original abstract (English)

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the associations between family history of hepatitis B virus, baseline hepatitis B surface antigen levels and surface antigen clearance in patients with chronic hepatitis B.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted in China, focusing on patients with chronic hepatitis B who received pegylated interferon-α treatment. The study included a sample of patients with documented family history of hepatitis B virus and baseline HBsAg levels.
Core methods
The study utilized a retrospective cohort design, analyzing medical records of patients with chronic hepatitis B. It assessed family history of hepatitis B virus, baseline HBsAg levels, and outcomes related to HBsAg clearance following pegylated interferon-α treatment.
Main results
The study found that family history of hepatitis B virus was significantly associated with a lower hazard of HBsAg clearance. Approximately 37% of this negative association was mediated by elevated baseline HBsAg levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as suggesting that patients with a family history of hepatitis B virus may have a reduced likelihood of achieving HBsAg clearance with pegylated interferon-α, potentially requiring lower baseline HBsAg levels to achieve similar outcomes as those without family history.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the development of personalized treatment strategies for chronic hepatitis B patients based on their family history and baseline HBsAg levels, potentially improving therapeutic outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on medical records, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

对大都市中新感染乙型肝炎增加的调查:来自整个 基因组 测序的见解

Investigation of a rise in newly acquired hepatitis B in a metropolitan setting: insights from whole genome sequencing
作者： Robyn Stevenson, Yi-Horng Ler, Avram Levy, David Speers, Kathryn Glass, Benjamin Scalley, Suzanne McEvoy
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查珀斯大都市新感染乙型肝炎病例的增加情况,以了解潜在的 暴发 和 传播 动态。
设计与对象
2023年8月1日至2024年7月31日期间,在珀斯大都会进行了一项暴发调查,其中包括20个新发现的病例和另外3个待进一步分析的病例。
核心方法
人口统计、疾病发病和危险因素的流行病学分析与实验室调查相结合,包括用于基因分型和系统发育分析的完整 基因组 测序 (WGS)。
主要结果
Most cases were aged over 30 years (n = 18, 78%) and reported casual unprotected sex (n = 17, 74%); some were born overseas (n = 8, 33%) or used intravenous drugs (n = 7, 30%). Of the 20 sequenced cases, 12 were locally acquired sub-genotype A2 HBV.
解释与创新
该研究确定了三个亚基因型 A2 簇和一个亚基因型 B3 簇,强调了 WGS 在补充传统流行病学方法以更好地了解 传播 模式方面的作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,亚基因型 A2 病例的增加可能是由于 HBV 毒株多次传入珀斯,当地 传播 有限,为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
个体患者样本内的遗传多样性使相关性分析变得复杂,并且该研究的结果受到样本量小和缺乏明确的流行病学联系的限制。

Investigation of a rise in newly acquired hepatitis B in a metropolitan setting: insights from whole genome sequencing

Authors: Robyn Stevenson, Yi-Horng Ler, Avram Levy, David Speers, Kathryn Glass, Benjamin Scalley, Suzanne McEvoy
Original abstract (English)
Three sub-genotype A2 clusters and one sub-genotype B3 cluster were identified.This study is one of the first in Australia to investigate a potential HBV outbreak using WGS. Although genetic diversity within individual patient samples (within-host sequence heterogeneity) complicated relatedness analyses and limited the assessment of transmission pathways, WGS complemented epidemiological methods, which had demonstrated few links. Abstract Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is a blood-borne virus that is nationally notifiable in Australia. Despite inclusion of the HBV vaccine in the childhood National Immunisation Program, local and overseas-acquired cases occur. An increase in newly acquired HBV cases in metropolitan Perth between 1 August 2023 and 31 July 2024 prompted an outbreak investigation. Epidemiological analyses of demographics, disease onset, and risk factors were combined with laboratory investigations, including whole genome sequencing (WGS) for genotyping and phylogenetic analysis.Twenty newly acquired cases were included, with three additional cases investigated. Most cases were aged over 30 years (n = 18, 78%) and reported casual unprotected sex (n = 17, 74%); some were born overseas (n = 8, 33%) or used intravenous drugs (n = 7, 30%). Four had received at least three HBV vaccines, although timing of doses varied. Of the 20 sequenced cases, 12 were locally acquired sub-genotype A2 HBV. The increase in sub-genotype A2 cases was likely due to coincident introductions of multiple HBV strains into Perth with some limited local transmission. Acknowledgements The Master of Applied Epidemiology scholarship for RS is funded by the Boorloo Public Health Unit, Western Australia. No other funding was provided for this investigation.The authors wish to thank staff at the Boorl...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate a rise in newly acquired hepatitis B cases in metropolitan Perth to understand the potential outbreak and transmission dynamics.
Design and population
An outbreak investigation was conducted in metropolitan Perth between 1 August 2023 and 31 July 2024, including 20 newly acquired cases and three additional cases for further analysis.
Core methods
Epidemiological analyses of demographics, disease onset, and risk factors were combined with laboratory investigations, including whole genome sequencing (WGS) for genotyping and phylogenetic analysis.
Main results
Most cases were aged over 30 years (n = 18, 78%) and reported casual unprotected sex (n = 17, 74%); some were born overseas (n = 8, 33%) or used intravenous drugs (n = 7, 30%). Of the 20 sequenced cases, 12 were locally acquired sub-genotype A2 HBV.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified three sub-genotype A2 clusters and one sub-genotype B3 cluster, highlighting the role of WGS in complementing traditional epidemiological methods to better understand transmission patterns.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the increase in sub-genotype A2 cases may be due to multiple introductions of HBV strains into Perth, with limited local transmission, informing targeted public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
Genetic diversity within individual patient samples complicated relatedness analyses, and the study's results were limited by the small sample size and the lack of clear epidemiological links.

Gambaran Hasil Pemeriksaan 血源性病毒(HIV、HBV、HCV)和 Profil Leukosit pada Pasien Gagal Ginjal yang Menjalani 血液透析

Gambaran Hasil Pemeriksaan Blood Borne Virus (HIV, HBV, HCV) dan Profil Leukosit pada Pasien Gagal Ginjal yang Menjalani Hemodialisis
作者： Putri Assyifa Yusuf, Farida Noor Irfani, Nazula Rahma Shafriani
摘要中文翻译萨拉赫·萨图·玛萨拉·克塞哈坦·杨·玛西·特鲁斯·曼宁卡特·普雷瓦伦西尼亚·杜尼亚·阿达拉·潘亚基特·金贾尔·克罗尼斯(PGK)。Pasien PGK 是血液透析的危险因素,血源性病毒 (BBV) 会导致血液透析。在国际上,康迪西尿毒症帕达 PGK 达帕特贝尔凯坦登甘散文炎症克罗尼斯扬特塞明梅拉鲁伊佩鲁巴汉概况白细胞。研究了日惹 Rumah Sakit PKU Muhammadiyah 的 BBV 血液透析和 PGK 血液透析。2025年的数据将在2025年开始计算,我们将在2025年的数据回顾中回顾这一过程。Sample penelitian terdiri atas 33 pasien yang diperoleh Melalui teknik 有目的取样。Pemeriksaan BBV mencakup HIV(人类免疫缺陷病毒)、HBV(乙型肝炎病毒)、HCV(丙型肝炎病毒)menggunakan快速检测、sementara pemeriksaan profil leukosit dilakukan dengan血液分析仪5-diff。分析数据 dilakukan secara deskriptif。Sebagian besar 回应 berjenis kelamin laki-laki (54,5%) dan berada pada kelompok usia 18–59 tahun (63,6%)。Hasil pemeriksaan menunjukkan seluruh 响应非反应性 HIV 和阴性 HBV,sedangkan HCV 阳性 ditemukan pada 2 响应 (6,1%)。Rerata jumlah leukosit mencapai 10,13 × 103/μL,dengan rerata neutrofil 75,97% 和 limfosit 14,73%。请注意,HCV 可能会导致血液透析、HIV 和 HBV 感染。Profil leukosit memperlihatkan kecenderungan peningkatan neutrofil disertai penurunan limfosit yang dapat menggambarkan adanya respons inflamasi pada pasien PGK yang menjalani hemodialisis.
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
萨拉赫·萨图·玛萨拉·克塞哈坦·杨·玛西·特鲁斯·曼宁卡特·普雷瓦伦西尼亚·杜尼亚·阿达拉·潘亚基特·金贾尔·克罗尼斯(PGK)。Pasien PGK 是血液透析的危险因素,血源性病毒 (BBV) 会导致血液透析。
设计与对象
研究方法是在 33 小时内进行有目的抽样。
核心方法
Pemeriksaan BBV mencakup HIV(人类免疫缺陷病毒)、HBV(乙型肝炎病毒)、HCV(丙型肝炎病毒)menggunakan快速检测、sementara pemeriksaan profil leukosit dilakukan dengan血液分析仪5-diff。分析数据 dilakukan secara deskriptif。
主要结果
Hasil pemeriksaan menunjukkan seluruh 响应非反应性 HIV 和阴性 HBV,sedangkan HCV 阳性 ditemukan pada 2 响应 (6,1%)。Rerata jumlah leukosit mencapai 10,13 × 103/μL,dengan rerata neutrofil 75,97% 和 limfosit 14,73%。
解释与创新
根据上述结果,HCV仍存在于部分接受透析的PGK患者中,而HIV和HBV未被检测到。白细胞谱显示中性粒细胞增加和淋巴细胞减少的趋势,这可能反映了接受透析的PGK患者存在炎症反应。
科研与公卫意义
本研究的结果可以提供关于接受透析的PGK患者中BBV,特别是HCV的患病率信息。这有助于提高对该人群BBV感染的监测和预防水平。
局限与证据强度
在我的记忆中进行研究,以验证数据的正确性和正确性。请注意,乙肝病毒感染的方法。

Gambaran Hasil Pemeriksaan Blood Borne Virus (HIV, HBV, HCV) dan Profil Leukosit pada Pasien Gagal Ginjal yang Menjalani Hemodialisis

Authors: Putri Assyifa Yusuf, Farida Noor Irfani, Nazula Rahma Shafriani
Original abstract (English)
Salah satu masalah kesehatan yang masih terus meningkat prevalensinya di seluruh dunia adalah penyakit ginjal kronis (PGK). Pasien PGK yang menjalani hemodialisis memiliki risiko terpapar Blood Borne Virus (BBV) karena adanya prosedur yang melibatkan paparan darah secara berulang. Selain itu, kondisi uremia pada PGK dapat berkaitan dengan proses inflamasi kronis yang tercermin melalui perubahan profil leukosit. Studi ini berguna untuk mengkarakterisasi profil leukosit dan hasil tes BBV pada pasien PGK yang menjalani hemodialisis di Rumah Sakit PKU Muhammadiyah di Yogyakarta. Sesuai dengan data dari tahun 2025 rekam medis, studi ini mengenakan metode deskriptif dengan pendekatan retrospektif. Sampel penelitian terdiri atas 33 pasien yang diperoleh melalui teknik purposive sampling. Pemeriksaan BBV mencakup HIV (Human Immunodeficiency Virus), HBV (Hepatitis B Virus), dan HCV (Hepatitis C Virus) menggunakan rapid test, sementara pemeriksaan profil leukosit dilakukan dengan hematology analyzer 5-diff. Analisis data dilakukan secara deskriptif. Sebagian besar responden berjenis kelamin laki-laki (54,5%) dan berada pada kelompok usia 18–59 tahun (63,6%). Hasil pemeriksaan menunjukkan seluruh responden nonreaktif terhadap HIV dan negatif HBV, sedangkan HCV positif ditemukan pada 2 responden (6,1%). Rerata jumlah leukosit mencapai 10,13 × 103/μL, dengan rerata neutrofil 75,97% dan limfosit 14,73%. Berdasarkan hasil tersebut, HCV masih ditemukan pada sebagian kecil pasien hemodialisis, sementara HIV dan HBV tidak ditemukan. Profil leukosit memperlihatkan kecenderungan peningkatan neutrofil disertai penurunan limfosit yang dapat menggambarkan adanya respons inflamasi pada pasien PGK yang menjalani hemodialisis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Salah satu masalah kesehatan yang masih terus meningkat prevalensinya di seluruh dunia adalah penyakit ginjal kronis (PGK). Pasien PGK yang menjalani hemodialisis memiliki risiko terpapar Blood Borne Virus (BBV) karena adanya prosedur yang melibatkan paparan darah secara berulang.
Design and population
Studi ini mengenakan metode deskriptif dengan pendekatan retrospektif dan sampel penelitian terdiri atas 33 pasien yang diperoleh melalui teknik purposive sampling.
Core methods
Pemeriksaan BBV mencakup HIV (Human Immunodeficiency Virus), HBV (Hepatitis B Virus), dan HCV (Hepatitis C Virus) menggunakan rapid test, sementara pemeriksaan profil leukosit dilakukan dengan hematology analyzer 5-diff. Analisis data dilakukan secara deskriptif.
Main results
Hasil pemeriksaan menunjukkan seluruh responden nonreaktif terhadap HIV dan negatif HBV, sedangkan HCV positif ditemukan pada 2 responden (6,1%). Rerata jumlah leukosit mencapai 10,13 × 103/μL, dengan rerata neutrofil 75,97% dan limfosit 14,73%.
Interpretation and novelty
Berdasarkan hasil tersebut, HCV masih ditemukan pada sebagian kecil pasien hemodialisis, sementara HIV dan HBV tidak ditemukan. Profil leukosit memperlihatkan kecenderungan peningkatan neutrofil disertai penurunan limfosit yang dapat menggambarkan adanya respons inflamasi pada pasien PGK yang menjalani hemodialisis.
Scientific and public-health significance
Hasil penelitian ini dapat memberikan gambaran tentang prevalensi BBV, terutama HCV, pada pasien PGK yang menjalani hemodialisis. Hal ini dapat membantu dalam meningkatkan pemantauan dan pencegahan infeksi BBV pada pasien ini.
Limitations and evidence strength
Studi ini memiliki beberapa batasan, termasuk ukuran sampel yang kecil dan pendekatan retrospektif yang mungkin mempengaruhi validitas data. Selain itu, metode pemeriksaan yang digunakan mungkin tidak dapat mendeteksi infeksi HBV pada tahap awal.

功能性治愈的免疫学基础和治疗策略研究进展

Advances in the immunological basis and therapeutic strategies for functional cure of chronic hepatitis B
作者： Fan-E Cheng, Shi-Hao Zheng, Wen-Ying Qi, Xin-Yu Zhao, Chun-Feng Mei, Xiao-Bin Zao, Yong-An Ye

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索 慢性乙型肝炎 背后的免疫学机制,并确定实现 功能性治愈 的治疗策略。背景强调,持续的病毒储存宿主和持续的抗原驱动的免疫功能障碍是慢性乙型肝炎的功能性治愈的主要限制。
设计与对象
本综述文章概述了 慢性乙型肝炎 的免疫学基础和治疗策略。它概述了免疫功能障碍的机制及其对 治疗 方法的影响,但没有描述具体的研究人群或设计。
核心方法
该综述综合了人类临床证据,并讨论了生物标志物引导的分层和合理的治疗排序以平衡免疫介导的肝损伤疗效的潜力。
主要结果
该综述确定了 乙型肝炎病毒 特异性 T 细胞耗竭、B 细胞功能障碍、肝脏免疫耐受、先天免疫失调以及代谢和肠肝轴重塑等关键机制作为实现 功能性治愈 的限制。它还概述了新兴的治疗策略,包括聚乙二醇干扰素、治疗性 疫苗接种、免疫检查点调节和先天免疫激活。
解释与创新
作者解释说,了解这些机制可以为制定持久 HBsAg 消失的分阶段策略提供信息。新颖之处在于将这些免疫机制与 功能性治愈 的治疗方法联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来研究和治疗策略的设计提供信息,旨在通过解决免疫功能障碍和病毒 储存宿主 来实现 慢性乙型肝炎 的 功能性治愈。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项综述,不提供原始临床数据,这限制了证据的强度。研究结果的可信度中等,因为它们是基于现有文献而不是新的实验结果。

Advances in the immunological basis and therapeutic strategies for functional cure of chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Fan-E Cheng, Shi-Hao Zheng, Wen-Ying Qi, Xin-Yu Zhao, Chun-Feng Mei, Xiao-Bin Zao, Yong-An Ye

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the immunological mechanisms underlying chronic hepatitis B and identify therapeutic strategies for achieving a functional cure. The background highlights that persistent viral reservoirs and sustained antigen-driven immune dysfunction are major constraints in the functional cure of chronic hepatitis B.
Design and population
This review article provides an overview of the immunological basis and therapeutic strategies for the functional cure of chronic hepatitis B. It outlines the mechanisms of immune dysfunction and their implications for treatment approaches, with no specific study population or design described.
Core methods
The review synthesizes human clinical evidence and discusses the potential of biomarker-guided stratification and rational sequencing of therapies to balance efficacy against immune-mediated liver injury.
Main results
The review identifies key mechanisms such as hepatitis B virus-specific T-cell exhaustion, B-cell dysfunction, hepatic immune tolerance, innate immune dysregulation, and metabolic and gut-liver axis remodeling as constraints to achieving a functional cure. It also outlines emerging therapeutic strategies including pegylated interferon, therapeutic vaccination, immune checkpoint modulation, and innate immune activation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that understanding these mechanisms can inform the development of a staged strategy for durable HBsAg loss. The novelty lies in linking these immunological mechanisms to therapeutic approaches for functional cure.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the design of future studies and therapeutic strategies aimed at achieving a functional cure for chronic hepatitis B by addressing immune dysfunction and viral reservoirs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a review and does not present original clinical data, which limits the strength of the evidence. The confidence in the findings is moderate as they are based on existing literature rather than new experimental results.

接受替诺福韦保留抗逆转录病毒治疗的艾滋病毒感染者的乙型肝炎再激活和感染

Hepatitis B Reactivation and Infection in People with HIV on Tenofovir-Sparing Antiretroviral Therapy
作者： G. Haser, N. Lisha, H. Kim, G. Burkholder, E. Cachay, H. Crane, J. Delaney, O. Falade-Nwulia, J. Fleming, M. Kitahata
摘要中文翻译随着越来越多的 HIV 感染者 (PWH) 转向保留替诺福韦 (TXF) 的抗逆转录病毒治疗 (ART),乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活 (HBVr) 和新感染 (HBVi) 的风险令人担忧。我们对艾滋病研究中心综合临床系统网络 队列研究 的 5,776 名无活动性 HBV 感染且在 2014 年 5 月 1 日后使用 ≥1 种保留 TXF 的 ART 方案的 PWH 进行了一项回顾性研究,并检查了定义为新 HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性或 HBV 病毒血症的 HBVi/r 发作。我们计算了每 10,000 人年在保留 TXF 的 ART 总时间内的 HBVi/r 率。我们在中位 5.3 年的随访中发现了 17 例 HBVi/r 病例。HBVi 率为每 10,000 人年 3.9 例,HBVr 率为每 10,000 人年 6.0 例,对于进入 队列研究 时 HBV 表面抗体 (HBsAb) 阴性的患者以及未接受 ART 且无抗 HBV 活性的患者,风险可能较高。肝损伤(定义为丙氨酸转氨酶实验室值≥100 U/L)仅在 1 例 HBVi 病例中发生在 3 个月内。在美国,保留 TXF 的 ART 中出现 HBVi/r 的情况很少见。HBVi/r 发生在一些既往 HBsAb 阳性的患者中,但未观察到明显的肝脏耀斑。当开始保留 TXF 的 ART 时,提供者应检查完整的 HBV 血清学,根据需要提供初始或额外的 疫苗接种,并监测潜在的 HBVi/r。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着越来越多的 HIV 感染者 (PWH) 转向保留替诺福韦 (TXF) 的抗逆转录病毒治疗 (ART),乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 再激活 (HBVr) 和新感染 (HBVi) 的风险令人担忧。
设计与对象
我们对艾滋病研究中心综合临床系统网络 队列研究 的 5,776 名无活动性 HBV 感染且在 2014 年 5 月 1 日后使用 ≥1 种保留 TXF 的 ART 方案的 PWH 进行了一项回顾性研究,并检查了定义为新 HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性或 HBV 病毒血症的 HBVi/r 发作。
核心方法
我们计算了每 10,000 人年在保留 TXF 的 ART 总时间内的 HBVi/r 率。
主要结果
我们在中位 5.3 年的随访中发现了 17 例 HBVi/r 病例。

HBVi 率为每 10,000 人年 3.9 例,HBVr 率为每 10,000 人年 6.0 例,对于进入 队列研究 时 HBV 表面抗体 (HBsAb) 阴性的患者以及未接受 ART 且无抗 HBV 活性的患者,风险可能较高。
科研与公卫意义
在美国,保留 TXF ART 的 HBVi/r 情况很少见。队列研究,但提供者应检查完整的 HBV 血清学,根据需要提供初始或额外的 疫苗接种,并监测潜在的 HBVi/r。

Hepatitis B Reactivation and Infection in People with HIV on Tenofovir-Sparing Antiretroviral Therapy

Authors: G. Haser, N. Lisha, H. Kim, G. Burkholder, E. Cachay, H. Crane, J. Delaney, O. Falade-Nwulia, J. Fleming, M. Kitahata
Original abstract (English)
As more people with HIV (PWH) transition to tenofovir (TXF)-sparing antiretroviral therapy (ART), the risk of hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation (HBVr) and new infection (HBVi) is a concern. We conducted a retrospective study of 5,776 PWH in the Centers for AIDS Research Network of Integrated Clinical Systems cohort without active HBV infection with ≥1 TXF-sparing ART regimen prescribed after 5/1/2014 and examined episodes of HBVi/r defined as new HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) positivity or HBV viremia. We calculated rate of HBVi/r over total time on TXF-sparing ART per 10,000 person-years. We identified 17 cases of HBVi/r over a median 5.3 years of follow up. The rate of HBVi was 3.9 per 10,000 person-years, and rate of HBVr was 6.0 per 10,000 person-years, with possible higher risk for those who were HBV surface antibody (HBsAb) negative at cohort entry and among those not on ART with no anti-HBV activity. Liver injury, defined as alanine aminotransferase lab value ≥ 100 U/L, occurred within 3 months in only one HBVi case. HBVi/r on TXF-sparing ART was rare in this U.S. HBVi/r occurred in some with prior HBsAb positivity, but significant hepatic flares were not observed. When starting TXF-sparing ART, providers should check complete HBV serologies, offer initial or additional vaccination as needed, and monitor for potential HBVi/r.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As more people with HIV (PWH) transition to tenofovir (TXF)-sparing antiretroviral therapy (ART), the risk of hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation (HBVr) and new infection (HBVi) is a concern.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective study of 5,776 PWH in the Centers for AIDS Research Network of Integrated Clinical Systems cohort without active HBV infection with ≥1 TXF-sparing ART regimen prescribed after 5/1/2014 and examined episodes of HBVi/r defined as new HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) positivity or HBV viremia.
Core methods
We calculated rate of HBVi/r over total time on TXF-sparing ART per 10,000 person-years.
Main results
We identified 17 cases of HBVi/r over a median 5.3 years of follow up.
Interpretation and novelty
The rate of HBVi was 3.9 per 10,000 person-years, and rate of HBVr was 6.0 per 10,000 person-years, with possible higher risk for those who were HBV surface antibody (HBsAb) negative at cohort entry and among those not on ART with no anti-HBV activity.
Scientific and public-health significance
HBVi/r on TXF-sparing ART was rare in this U.S. cohort, but providers should check complete HBV serologies, offer initial or additional vaccination as needed, and monitor for potential HBVi/r.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for this study.

埃及亚历山大私立医院医护人员的针刺伤特征和暴露后行为

Characteristics of needlestick injuries and post-exposure practices among healthcare workers in private hospitals in Alexandria, Egypt
作者： Marwa El-Zoghby, Abdelfattah Hammouda, Eman Abdel Hamid Omran, Engy Aboelsaad, M. Elzoghby
摘要中文翻译摘要 针刺伤 (NSI) 是医护人员 (HCW) 的主要职业危害,可能会接触血源性病原体并产生相关的健康后果。使用 2023 年至 2024 年期间在埃及亚历山大的四家私立医院经历过 NSI 的 318 名医护人员的记录进行了一项基于记录的回顾性研究,所有医院均采用标准化的 NSI 报告系统。数据包括人口和职业特征、受伤情况、安全实践、乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 状态、感染预防和控制 (IPC) 培训以及暴露后管理。大多数 NSI 发生在女性医护人员 (61.6%) 中,护士是受影响最大的职业群体 (63.5%)。65.1% 的 NSI 涉及注射器,而患者突然移动是最常报告的促成因素 (26.1%)。在记录有 IPC 培训状态的记录中,79.8% 表示之前没有接受过培训,而 36.2% 的记录中缺少培训状态。总体而言,16.0% 的医护人员未接种乙型肝炎疫苗。30.8% 的记录中未记录暴露后行为,只有 29.6% 的医护人员接受了有记录的丙型肝炎病毒、乙型肝炎和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 暴露后检测。这些发现凸显了 IPC 培训、乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 和暴露后管理方面的差距,并强调了标准化报告、及时的暴露后护理和改进职业安全实践的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
针刺伤 (NSI) 对医护人员 (HCW) 构成严重的职业危害,可能会接触血源性病原体并造成相关的健康后果。本研究旨在描述埃及亚历山大私立医院的 NSI 和暴露后做法。
设计与对象
使用 2023 年至 2024 年期间在埃及亚历山大的四家私立医院经历过 NSI 的 318 名医护人员的记录进行了一项基于记录的回顾性研究,所有医院均采用标准化的 NSI 报告系统。数据包括人口和职业特征、受伤情况、安全实践、乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 状态、感染预防和控制 (IPC) 培训以及暴露后管理。
核心方法
该研究利用现有记录收集 NSI 数据,包括人口和职业特征、受伤情况、安全实践、乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 状态、IPC 培训和暴露后管理。所提供的证据中没有详细说明具体的实验室或统计程序。
主要结果
大多数 NSI 发生在女性医护人员 (61.6%) 中,护士是受影响最大的职业群体 (63.5%)。65.1% 的 NSI 涉及注射器,而患者突然移动是最常报告的促成因素 (26.1%)。
解释与创新
研究结果强调需要改进职业安全实践,并强调对暴露于 乙型肝炎病毒 的医护人员进行标准化报告和及时暴露后护理的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为制定有针对性的干预措施提供了依据,以改善埃及亚历山大私立医院医护人员的 乙型肝炎病毒 预防和暴露后管理实践。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性记录,这可能会引入偏差并限制报告实践的准确性。由于依赖现有数据且缺乏详细的统计分析,证据强度中等。

Characteristics of needlestick injuries and post-exposure practices among healthcare workers in private hospitals in Alexandria, Egypt

Authors: Marwa El-Zoghby, Abdelfattah Hammouda, Eman Abdel Hamid Omran, Engy Aboelsaad, M. Elzoghby
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Needlestick injuries (NSIs) are a major occupational hazard among healthcare workers (HCWs), with potential exposure to blood-borne pathogens and associated health consequences. A retrospective record-based study was conducted using 318 records of HCWs who experienced NSIs during 2023–2024 in four private hospitals in Alexandria, Egypt, all with standardized NSI reporting systems. Data included demographic and occupational characteristics, injury circumstances, safety practices, hepatitis B vaccination status, infection prevention and control (IPC) training, and post-exposure management. Most NSIs occurred among female HCWs (61.6%), and nurses represented the largest affected occupational group (63.5%). Syringes were involved in 65.1% of NSIs, while sudden patient movement was the most frequently reported contributing factor (26.1%). Among records with documented IPC training status, 79.8% indicated no prior training, while training status was missing in 36.2% of records. Overall, 16.0% of HCWs were documented as unvaccinated against hepatitis B. Post-exposure actions were not documented in 30.8% of records, and only 29.6% of HCWs underwent documented post-exposure testing for hepatitis C virus, hepatitis B, and human immunodeficiency virus (HIV). These findings highlight gaps in IPC training, hepatitis B vaccination, and post-exposure management and underscore the need for standardized reporting, timely post-exposure care, and improved occupational safety practices.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Needlestick injuries (NSIs) pose a significant occupational hazard for healthcare workers (HCWs), with potential exposure to blood-borne pathogens and associated health consequences. This study aimed to characterize NSIs and post-exposure practices in private hospitals in Alexandria, Egypt.
Design and population
A retrospective record-based study was conducted using 318 records of HCWs who experienced NSIs during 2023–2024 in four private hospitals in Alexandria, Egypt, all with standardized NSI reporting systems. Data included demographic and occupational characteristics, injury circumstances, safety practices, hepatitis B vaccination status, infection prevention and control (IPC) training, and post-exposure management.
Core methods
The study utilized existing records to collect data on NSIs, including demographic and occupational characteristics, injury circumstances, safety practices, hepatitis B vaccination status, IPC training, and post-exposure management. No specific laboratory or statistical procedures were detailed in the supplied evidence.
Main results
Most NSIs occurred among female HCWs (61.6%), and nurses represented the largest affected occupational group (63.5%). Syringes were involved in 65.1% of NSIs, while sudden patient movement was the most frequently reported contributing factor (26.1%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the need for improved occupational safety practices and emphasize the importance of standardized reporting and timely post-exposure care for HCWs exposed to hepatitis B virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform the development of targeted interventions to improve hepatitis B virus prevention and post-exposure management practices among healthcare workers in private hospitals in Alexandria, Egypt.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective records, which may introduce bias and limit the accuracy of reported practices. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on existing data and the absence of detailed statistical analyses.

乙型和丁型肝炎:基于干扰素的治疗 - 用于临床试验或标准护理 - 回到未来?

Hepatitis B and D: Interferon-Based Therapy-For Clinical Trials or Standard of Care-Back to the Future?
作者： Zillah Cargill, Harry L A Janssen, Tarik Asselah, Kosh Agarwal
摘要中文翻译过去三十年来,干扰素 (IFN) 在慢性病毒性乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的治疗中发挥了重要作用。核苷/核苷酸类似物(NUC)在抑制乙型肝炎病毒方面的广泛应用(其副作用较小),加上最近获得的用于丁型肝炎 治疗 的 bulevirtide 许可,意味着其临床使用进一步下降。聚乙二醇化干扰素α (peg-IFNα) 治疗 效果欠佳,耐受性差,仅在有限数量的患者中实现了乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的持久清除。尽管如此,与单独使用 NUC 的患者相比,其在乙型肝炎中的使用显着增加了实现乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 消失的患者数量。有证据表明,peg-IFNα 还与慢性丁型肝炎的长期临床结果的整体改善相关,即使对于许多丁型肝炎病毒载量在停药后反弹的人来说也是如此。最近的临床试验重点关注 peg-IFNα 与新型 抗病毒药物 疗法的联合治疗,以实现乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的有限治愈。本文重点介绍最新的研究进展,探讨 peg-IFNα 治疗 是否仍可能在乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎 治疗 中发挥重要作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
过去三十年来,干扰素 (IFN) 在慢性病毒性乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的治疗中发挥了重要作用。
证据基础与方法
核苷/核苷酸类似物(NUC)在抑制乙型肝炎病毒方面的广泛应用(其副作用较小),加上最近获得的用于丁型肝炎 治疗 的 bulevirtide 许可,意味着其临床使用进一步下降。
共识与结论
聚乙二醇化干扰素α (peg-IFNα) 治疗 效果欠佳,耐受性差,仅在有限数量的患者中实现了乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎的持久清除。
争议与缺口
尽管如此,与单独使用 NUC 的患者相比,其在乙型肝炎中的使用显着增加了实现乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 消失的患者数量。
科研与实践启示

Hepatitis B and D: Interferon-Based Therapy-For Clinical Trials or Standard of Care-Back to the Future?

Authors: Zillah Cargill, Harry L A Janssen, Tarik Asselah, Kosh Agarwal
Original abstract (English)
Interferon (IFN) has historically played a major role in chronic viral hepatitis B and D management over the last three decades. Widening utilisation of nucleoside/nucleotide analogues (NUCs) for hepatitis B viral suppression, which have a better side-effect profile, alongside the recent licensing of bulevirtide for treatment of hepatitis D, means that its clinical use has further declined. Pegylated-interferon alpha (peg-IFNα) treatment is suboptimal with poor tolerability and achieves durable clearance of hepatitis B and D in only a limited number of patients. Nevertheless, its use in hepatitis B has significantly increased the number of patients achieving hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss compared to those on NUCs alone. Evidence has shown that peg-IFNα is also associated with overall improved long-term clinical outcomes in chronic hepatitis D, even for the many whose hepatitis D viral load rebounds after discontinuation. Clinical trials have recently focused on peg-IFNα in combination with novel antiviral therapies in the goal for finite cure in hepatitis B and D. This article focuses on recent research advances, examining if peg-IFNα treatment may still play an important role in hepatitis B and D treatment.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Interferon (IFN) has historically played a major role in chronic viral hepatitis B and D management over the last three decades.
Evidence base and method
Widening utilisation of nucleoside/nucleotide analogues (NUCs) for hepatitis B viral suppression, which have a better side-effect profile, alongside the recent licensing of bulevirtide for treatment of hepatitis D, means that its clinical use has further declined.
Consensus and conclusions
Pegylated-interferon alpha (peg-IFNα) treatment is suboptimal with poor tolerability and achieves durable clearance of hepatitis B and D in only a limited number of patients.
Controversies and gaps
Nevertheless, its use in hepatitis B has significantly increased the number of patients achieving hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss compared to those on NUCs alone.
Research and practice implications
Evidence has shown that peg-IFNα is also associated with overall improved long-term clinical outcomes in chronic hepatitis D, even for the many whose hepatitis D viral load rebounds after discontinuation.

美国的 慢性乙型肝炎:当前形势、未满足的需求和未来方向。

Chronic Hepatitis B in the United States: Current Landscape, Unmet Needs and Future Directions.
作者： Chari Cohen, James Philip Esteban, Shravani Bobde, Robert J Wong
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染是美国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,与及时 诊断、治疗、监测和疾病管理相关的大量需求未得到满足。这篇叙述性综述探讨了美国慢性乙型肝炎的情况,包括患者护理方面的差距、不断发展的治疗前景、血清学生物标志物在改善乙型肝炎患者 (PLWHB) 患者的治疗结果和经历方面的临床效用。在美国,乙型肝炎的诊断和治疗仍然不足,目前的努力不足以实现世界卫生组织相关的 2030 年消除目标。美国肝病研究协会和美国传染病学会的最新指南代表了北美的观点,标志着向扩大 治疗 资格标准和共同决策的转变。核苷(酸)类似物 (NA) 是美国目前的护理标准,但单独使用 NA 很少能实现 功能性治愈。针对 功能性治愈 的新型有限疗法,例如反义寡核苷酸、小干扰 RNA、免疫调节剂以及组合或序贯疗法,正在研究中。由于慢性乙型肝炎的不可预测性和复杂性,定量乙型肝炎表面抗原等生物标志物可为 治疗 决策提供预后价值,并有助于指导个体化慢性乙型肝炎管理。PLWHB 经历心理影响和健康相关问题,包括对 传播 和 乙型肝炎病毒 相关并发症的恐惧。改善 CHB 管理的机会包括增加筛查和与护理的联系、开发更多针对 功能性治愈 的 治疗、采用血清学生物标志物来告知 治疗 决策以及优先考虑 PLWHB 的生活经历和偏好。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染是美国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,与及时 诊断、治疗、监测和疾病管理相关的大量需求未得到满足。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述探讨了美国慢性乙型肝炎的情况,包括患者护理方面的差距、不断发展的治疗前景、血清学生物标志物在改善乙型肝炎患者 (PLWHB) 患者的治疗结果和经历方面的临床效用。

在美国,乙型肝炎的诊断和治疗仍然不足,目前的努力不足以实现世界卫生组织相关的 2030 年消除目标。

改善 CHB 管理的机会包括增加筛查和与护理的联系、开发更多针对 功能性治愈 的 治疗、采用血清学生物标志物来告知 治疗 决策以及优先考虑 PLWHB 的生活经历和偏好。

Chronic Hepatitis B in the United States: Current Landscape, Unmet Needs and Future Directions.

Authors: Chari Cohen, James Philip Esteban, Shravani Bobde, Robert J Wong
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection represents a major public health challenge in the US, with substantial unmet needs related to timely diagnosis, treatment, monitoring and disease management. This narrative review explores the CHB landscape in the US, including gaps in patient care, the evolving therapeutic landscape, the clinical utility of serological biomarkers to improve outcomes and the experiences of people living with hepatitis B (PLWHB). Hepatitis B remains underdiagnosed and undertreated in the US, and current efforts are insufficient to meet the associated World Health Organization 2030 elimination targets. Latest guidelines from the American Association for the Study of Liver Diseases and the Infectious Diseases Society of America, representing a North American perspective, signify a shift towards expanded treatment eligibility criteria and shared decision-making. Nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) are the current standard of care in the US, but functional cure is rarely achieved with NAs alone. Novel finite therapies targeting functional cure, such as antisense oligonucleotides, small interfering RNAs, immune modulators and combination or sequential regimens, are under investigation. Because of the unpredictable, complex nature of CHB, biomarkers such as quantitative hepatitis B surface antigen offer prognostic value for treatment decisions and could help guide individualised CHB management. PLWHB experience psychological impacts and health-related concerns, including fear of transmission and hepatitis B virus-related complications. Opportunities for improvement of CHB management include increased screening and linkage to care, development of more treatments targeting functional cure, adoption of serological biomarkers to inform treatment decisions and prioritising the lived experiences and preferences of PLWHB.

Evidence base and method

Controversies and gaps

蛋白质糖基化在 HBV 感染中的双重作用:促进病毒 发病机制 和塑造 宿主 防御

The dual role of protein glycosylation in HBV infection: facilitating viral pathogenesis and shaping host defense
作者： Jia-Jun Yang, Zhao-Yang Sun, Hong-Mei Li, Yu-Hui Zhao
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎是全球最流行的慢性病毒感染,对全球公共卫生构成严重威胁。蛋白质糖基化是一种关键的翻译后修饰,调节多种生理过程。乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染与病毒和 宿主 蛋白的糖基化改变有关,这会影响病毒进入、免疫逃避和疾病进展,而 HBV 感染会重塑 宿主 糖基化。考虑到糖基化模式对病理扰动的敏感性,检查 HBV 如何利用 宿主 糖基化机制来促进病毒持久性和 发病机制 尤为重要。本综述围绕双重角色框架进行组织:一方面涵盖糖基化的原病毒功能(病毒进入、包膜蛋白折叠、加工、组装和分泌、持久性、免疫逃逸和疾病进展),另一方面涵盖相同途径的 宿主 保护功能(糖基化依赖性限制因子、聚糖依赖性 ER 质量控制和 ERAD、细胞应激反应和免疫调节)。这项探索提供了对 HBV 感染背后的聚糖依赖性机制及其对开发新的诊断和治疗策略的影响的见解。

范围与问题

证据基础与方法
蛋白质糖基化是一种关键的翻译后修饰,调节多种生理过程。

The dual role of protein glycosylation in HBV infection: facilitating viral pathogenesis and shaping host defense

Authors: Jia-Jun Yang, Zhao-Yang Sun, Hong-Mei Li, Yu-Hui Zhao
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B is the most prevalent chronic viral infection worldwide, posing a serious threat to global public health. Protein glycosylation, a critical post-translational modification, regulates diverse physiological processes. Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is associated with altered glycosylation of both viral and host proteins, which influences viral entry, immune evasion and disease progression, while HBV infection remodels host glycosylation. Given the sensitivity of glycosylation patterns to pathological perturbations, it is particularly relevant to examine how HBV exploits host glycosylation machinery to facilitate viral persistence and pathogenesis. This review is organised around a dual-role framework: one arm covers the proviral functions of glycosylation (viral entry, envelope protein folding, processing, assembly and secretion, persistence, immune escape and disease progression), the other the host-protective functions of the same pathways (glycosylation-dependent restriction factors, glycan-dependent ER quality control and ERAD, cellular stress responses and immune regulation). This exploration provides insights into the glycan-dependent mechanisms underlying HBV infection and their implications for developing novel diagnostic and therapeutic strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Hepatitis B is the most prevalent chronic viral infection worldwide, posing a serious threat to global public health.
Evidence base and method
Protein glycosylation, a critical post-translational modification, regulates diverse physiological processes.
Consensus and conclusions
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is associated with altered glycosylation of both viral and host proteins, which influences viral entry, immune evasion and disease progression, while HBV infection remodels host glycosylation.
Controversies and gaps
Given the sensitivity of glycosylation patterns to pathological perturbations, it is particularly relevant to examine how HBV exploits host glycosylation machinery to facilitate viral persistence and pathogenesis.
Research and practice implications
This review is organised around a dual-role framework: one arm covers the proviral functions of glycosylation (viral entry, envelope protein folding, processing, assembly and secretion, persistence, immune escape and disease progression), the other the host-protective functions of the same pathways (glycosylation-dependent restriction factors, glycan-dependent ER quality control and ERAD, cellular stress responses and immune regulation).

乙型肝炎慢性:进展、并发症和治疗策略 — 文献综述 慢性乙型肝炎:疾病进展、并发症和治疗策略 — 文献综述

Hepatite B crônica: progressão da doença, complicações e estratégias terapêuticas — uma revisão narrativa da literatura Chronic Hepatitis B: disease progression, complications, and therapeutic strategies — a literature review
作者： Marcos Afonso dos Santos Miranda, Luiz Henrique Freitas Faller, Gustavo Luiz Farias Alves, Alcione de Oliveira dos Santos
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 是一个重要的公共卫生问题,因为它有可能发展为肝纤维化、肝硬化和肝细胞癌。疾病进展受病毒、免疫、临床、代谢和 宿主 相关因素以及与 诊断、治疗 和随访相关的条件的影响。本研究旨在分析 慢性乙型肝炎 临床进展、主要相关因素、并发症和治疗策略的现有证据。这是一篇基于 PubMed/MEDLINE、LILACS 和 SciELO 检索的叙述性文献综述,考虑了 2015 年 1 月至 2026 年 7 月葡萄牙语、英语和西班牙语的出版物。优先考虑涉及感染自然史、疾病进展相关因素、并发症和 治疗 的科学研究、临床指南以及技术和规范文件。文献表明,慢性乙型肝炎 的进展是病毒复制、免疫应答 和 宿主 特征之间相互作用的结果,也可能受到合并感染、饮酒、代谢紊乱和影响获得医疗保健的条件的影响。抗病毒药物 疗法在抑制病毒复制和降低疾病进展风险方面发挥着核心作用,而纵向随访对于识别并发症和分层风险仍然至关重要。总之,慢性乙型肝炎的管理需要采取综合方法,包括预防、早期诊断、个体化评估、及时治疗和持续随访。

本研究旨在分析 慢性乙型肝炎 临床进展、主要相关因素、并发症和治疗策略的现有证据。

Hepatite B crônica: progressão da doença, complicações e estratégias terapêuticas — uma revisão narrativa da literatura Chronic Hepatitis B: disease progression, complications, and therapeutic strategies — a literature review

Authors: Marcos Afonso dos Santos Miranda, Luiz Henrique Freitas Faller, Gustavo Luiz Farias Alves, Alcione de Oliveira dos Santos
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B is an important public health problem due to its potential to progress to liver fibrosis, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Disease progression is influenced by viral, immunological, clínical, metabolic, and host-related factors, as well as by conditions associated with diagnosis, treatment, and follow-up. This study aimed to analyze the available evidence on the clinical progression of chronic hepatitis B, its main associated factors, complications, and therapeutic strategies. This is a narrative literature review based on searches conducted in PubMed/MEDLINE, LILACS, and SciELO, considering publications from January 2015 to July 2026 in Portuguese, English, and Spanish. Scientific studies, clinical guidelines, and technical and normative documents addressing the natural history of infection, factors associated with disease progression, complications, and treatment were prioritized. The literature indicates that the progression of chronic hepatitis B results from the interaction between viral replication, immune response, and host characteristics, and may also be influenced by coinfections, alcohol consumption, metabolic disorders, and conditions affecting access to healthcare. Antiviral therapy plays a central role in suppressing viral replication and reducing the risk of disease progression, whereas longitudinal follow-up remains essential for identifying complications and stratifying risk. In conclusion, the management of chronic hepatitis B requires an integrated approach involving prevention, early diagnosis, individualized assessment, timely treatment, and continuous follow-up.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study aimed to analyze the available evidence on the clinical progression of chronic hepatitis B, its main associated factors, complications, and therapeutic strategies.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative literature review based on searches conducted in PubMed/MEDLINE, LILACS, and SciELO, considering publications from January 2015 to July 2026 in Portuguese, English, and Spanish.
Consensus and conclusions
The literature indicates that the progression of chronic hepatitis B results from the interaction between viral replication, immune response, and host characteristics, and may also be influenced by coinfections, alcohol consumption, metabolic disorders, and conditions affecting access to healthcare.
Controversies and gaps
Antiviral therapy plays a central role in suppressing viral replication and reducing the risk of disease progression, whereas longitudinal follow-up remains essential for identifying complications and stratifying risk.
Research and practice implications
In conclusion, the management of chronic hepatitis B requires an integrated approach involving prevention, early diagnosis, individualized assessment, timely treatment, and continuous follow-up.

HIV-乙型肝炎-疟疾界面的三重负担:病毒-寄生虫相互作用、流行病学复杂性以及撒哈拉以南非洲综合 监测 和社区预防的案例

Triple burden at the HIV–hepatitis B–malaria interface: viral–parasite interactions, epidemiological complexity and the case for integrated surveillance and community-based prevention in sub-Saharan Africa
作者： Matthew Chibunna Igwe, Grace Aghaji Nwankwo, Oluchi Jacinta Ossai, Ogbonna Alphonsus Ogbuabor
摘要中文翻译人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和疟疾在撒哈拉以南非洲地区有重叠,但它们的传播方式、持续时间以及项目衡量成功的方式有所不同。这篇批判性的叙述性评论审查了每个界面的生物相互作用的证据、它们的重叠造成的诊断和流行病学陷阱,以及综合 监测 和社区预防的案例。HIV 相关的免疫抑制持续增加疟疾的发生频率和严重程度,而急性疟疾会暂时升高血浆 HIV RNA。HIV 加速了慢性 HBV 的自然病程。由于 HIV 治疗 和预防正在转向治疗方案和对 HBV 无活性的长效产品,因此在任何治疗方案改变之前,记录乙型肝炎表面抗原结果变得至关重要。抗逆转录病毒药物和抗疟药物也以临床相关方式相互作用。乙肝病毒和疟原虫之间的直接相互作用是合理的,通过疟疾期间干扰素介导的乙肝病毒抑制,但这种相互作用主要依赖于动物模型和小型、不一致的人体研究,并且不存在对三重感染的可靠估计。疟疾期间的发烧、贫血和肝脏检查异常可能会掩盖并发的慢性​​病毒感染,而常规设施数据可能会产生明显的合并感染模式,这些模式反映的是检测强度而不是生物学。借鉴世界卫生组织关于综合疾病 监测 和应对的指导,我们描述了一种检测-验证-行动方法,该方法将社区信号、确认性测试和机密纵向记录联系起来,同时每种感染都有自己的指标和审查周期。整合应该基于共享交付,而不是假设的共享 发病机制,并且需要前瞻性的 队列研究 和实施试验来证明它是否可以改善结果和公平性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染、基因型和疾病。该综述的重点是生物相互作用、流行病学复杂性以及撒哈拉以南非洲地区综合 监测 和社区预防的案例,特别是在 HIV-HBV-疟疾界面。
证据基础与方法
这篇批判性的叙述性评论审查了每个界面的生物相互作用的证据、它们的重叠造成的诊断和流行病学陷阱,以及综合 监测 和社区预防的案例。该综述基于对现有文献的综合分析,没有报告具体的研究计数或荟萃分析方法。
共识与结论
HIV 相关的免疫抑制持续增加疟疾的发生频率和严重程度,而急性疟疾会暂时升高血浆 HIV RNA。HIV 还会加速慢性 HBV 的自然病程。抗逆转录病毒药物和抗疟药物以临床相关方式相互作用。
争议与缺口
乙型肝炎病毒和疟原虫之间的直接相互作用似乎是合理的,但它主要依赖于动物模型和小型且不一致的人体研究。不存在对三重感染的可靠估计。疟疾期间的发烧、贫血和肝脏检查异常可能会掩盖并发的慢性​​病毒感染,而常规设施数据可能会产生明显的合并感染模式,这些模式反映的是检测强度而不是生物学。
科研与实践启示
整合应该基于共享交付,而不是假设的共享 发病机制,并且需要前瞻性的 队列研究 和实施试验来证明它是否可以改善结果和公平性。借鉴世界卫生组织关于综合疾病 监测 和应对的指导,描述了一种检测-验证-行动方法,该方法将社区信号、确认性测试和机密纵向记录联系起来,而每种感染都有自己的指标和审查周期。

Triple burden at the HIV–hepatitis B–malaria interface: viral–parasite interactions, epidemiological complexity and the case for integrated surveillance and community-based prevention in sub-Saharan Africa

Authors: Matthew Chibunna Igwe, Grace Aghaji Nwankwo, Oluchi Jacinta Ossai, Ogbonna Alphonsus Ogbuabor
Original abstract (English)
Human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV) and malaria overlap across sub-Saharan Africa, yet they differ in how they spread, how long they last and how programmes measure success. This critical narrative review examines the evidence for biological interaction at each interface, the diagnostic and epidemiological traps that their overlap creates, and the case for integrated surveillance and community prevention. HIV-associated immunosuppression consistently increases the frequency and severity of malaria, and acute malaria transiently raises plasma HIV RNA. HIV accelerates the natural history of chronic HBV. Because HIV treatment and prevention are shifting towards regimens and long-acting products with no activity against HBV, a documented hepatitis B surface antigen result has become essential before any regimen change. Antiretroviral and antimalarial drugs also interact in clinically relevant ways. A direct interaction between HBV and Plasmodium is plausible, through interferon-mediated suppression of HBV during malaria, but it rests mainly on animal models and small, inconsistent human studies, and no reliable estimate of triple infection exists. Fever, anaemia and abnormal liver tests during malaria can mask a concurrent chronic viral infection, and routine facility data can produce apparent coinfection patterns that reflect testing intensity rather than biology. Drawing on the World Health Organization’s guidance on integrated disease surveillance and response, we describe a detect–verify–act approach that links community signals, confirmatory testing and confidential longitudinal records, while each infection keeps its own indicators and review cycle. Integration should rest on shared delivery, not on assumed shared pathogenesis, and prospective cohorts and implementation trials are needed to show whether it improves outcomes and equity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, genotypes and disease. The review focuses on biological interactions, epidemiological complexity, and the case for integrated surveillance and community-based prevention in sub-Saharan Africa, particularly at the HIV–HBV–malaria interface.
Evidence base and method
This critical narrative review examines the evidence for biological interaction at each interface, the diagnostic and epidemiological traps that their overlap creates, and the case for integrated surveillance and community prevention. The review is based on a comprehensive analysis of existing literature and does not report specific study counts or meta-analysis methods.
Consensus and conclusions
HIV-associated immunosuppression consistently increases the frequency and severity of malaria, and acute malaria transiently raises plasma HIV RNA. HIV also accelerates the natural history of chronic HBV. Antiretroviral and antimalarial drugs interact in clinically relevant ways.
Controversies and gaps
A direct interaction between HBV and Plasmodium is plausible, but it rests mainly on animal models and small, inconsistent human studies. No reliable estimate of triple infection exists. Fever, anaemia and abnormal liver tests during malaria can mask a concurrent chronic viral infection, and routine facility data can produce apparent coinfection patterns that reflect testing intensity rather than biology.
Research and practice implications
Integration should rest on shared delivery, not on assumed shared pathogenesis, and prospective cohorts and implementation trials are needed to show whether it improves outcomes and equity. Drawing on the World Health Organization’s guidance on integrated disease surveillance and response, a detect–verify–act approach is described that links community signals, confirmatory testing and confidential longitudinal records, while each infection keeps its own indicators and review cycle.

核苷(酸)类似物停药策略以实现 慢性乙型肝炎 的 功能性治愈?系统评价和荟萃分析。

Nucleos(t)ide analogue discontinuation strategy to achieve the functional cure for chronic hepatitis B? A systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Mariantonietta Pisaturo, Pierantonio Grimaldi, Lorenzo Onorato, Chiara Cacace, Nicola Coppola
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
在连续核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗期间,功能性治愈 发生率较低,暂停治疗已表现出有趣的非 治疗 反应。
证据基础与方法
进行了一项系统回顾和荟萃分析,以调查稳定的 HBsAg 阳性病毒抑制患者对停药的反应,包括 HBsAg 清除、临床缓解、病毒学复发、丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 发作以及重新引入治疗的必要性。
共识与结论
A total of 28 papers (4495 patients) were included in the analysis, with low risk of bias: HBsAg clearance rate at 24, 48 and 96 weeks were of 3%, 8% and 11% (95% IC 1-8%, 4-15%, 7-15%) respectively, with low to moderate heterogeneity (I2=58.4%%, 66.5%, 47.3%); according to the HBeAg status at NA introduction, in those HBeAg negative the HBsAg clearance rates were 3%, 8% and 10% at 24, 48 and 96 weeks, respectively, while in those HBeAg positive 2.3 and 3.4% at 24 and 48 weeks, respectively. Overall a higher HBsAg clearance rate (39% at 96 weeks, 95% CI=10-78%, I2=83.3%) was described in patients with HBsAg <100 UI/ml at discontinuation.
争议与缺口
这些结果显示,除此时 HBsAg 滴度较低的患者外,NA 停药后 HBsAg 清除率总体较低。因此,在单个病例中,必须平衡 NA 的停药,以应对病毒学和临床复发的风险。
科研与实践启示
由于病毒学和临床复发率在 24、48 和 96 周时重新引入 治疗 分别为 21%、12% 和 17%。

Nucleos(t)ide analogue discontinuation strategy to achieve the functional cure for chronic hepatitis B? A systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Mariantonietta Pisaturo, Pierantonio Grimaldi, Lorenzo Onorato, Chiara Cacace, Nicola Coppola
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Functional cure rate is low during continuous nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy, and therapy suspension has demonstrated interesting off-treatment responses. METHODS: A systematic review and meta-analysis to investigate how stable, HBsAg positive-virally suppressed patients would respond to drug discontinuation in terms of HBsAg clearance, clinical remission, virological relapses, alanine-aminotransferases (ALT) flares, and necessity of therapy reintroduction were performed. Included studies were prospective, experimental and observational studies, including data on outcomes after NA discontinuation in HBV DNA negative patients with positive HBsAg, involving at least 20 patients receiving entecavir or tenofovir, published from 2008 to February 2026, written in English language. RESULTS: A total of 28 papers (4495 patients) were included in the analysis, with low risk of bias: HBsAg clearance rate at 24, 48 and 96 weeks were of 3%, 8% and 11% (95% IC 1-8%, 4-15%, 7-15%) respectively, with low to moderate heterogeneity (I2=58.4%%, 66.5%, 47.3%); according to the HBeAg status at NA introduction, in those HBeAg negative the HBsAg clearance rates were 3%, 8% and 10% at 24, 48 and 96 weeks, respectively, while in those HBeAg positive 2.3 and 3.4% at 24 and 48 weeks, respectively. Overall a higher HBsAg clearance rate (39% at 96 weeks, 95% CI=10-78%, I2=83.3%) was described in patients with HBsAg <100 UI/ml at discontinuation. Treatment reintroduction due to virological and clinical relapse rates were 21%, 12%, and 17% at 24, 48 and 96 weeks, respectively. CONCLUSIONS: These results showed an overall low rate of HBsAg clearance after NA discontinuation, except for the patients with low HBsAg titers at this time. Thus, NA discontinuation must be balanced in the single case for the risk of virological and clinical relapses.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Functional cure rate is low during continuous nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy, and therapy suspension has demonstrated interesting off-treatment responses.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and meta-analysis to investigate how stable, HBsAg positive-virally suppressed patients would respond to drug discontinuation in terms of HBsAg clearance, clinical remission, virological relapses, alanine-aminotransferases (ALT) flares, and necessity of therapy reintroduction were performed.
Consensus and conclusions
A total of 28 papers (4495 patients) were included in the analysis, with low risk of bias: HBsAg clearance rate at 24, 48 and 96 weeks were of 3%, 8% and 11% (95% IC 1-8%, 4-15%, 7-15%) respectively, with low to moderate heterogeneity (I2=58.4%%, 66.5%, 47.3%); according to the HBeAg status at NA introduction, in those HBeAg negative the HBsAg clearance rates were 3%, 8% and 10% at 24, 48 and 96 weeks, respectively, while in those HBeAg positive 2.3 and 3.4% at 24 and 48 weeks, respectively. Overall a higher HBsAg clearance rate (39% at 96 weeks, 95% CI=10-78%, I2=83.3%) was described in patients with HBsAg <100 UI/ml at discontinuation.
Controversies and gaps
These results showed an overall low rate of HBsAg clearance after NA discontinuation, except for the patients with low HBsAg titers at this time. Thus, NA discontinuation must be balanced in the single case for the risk of virological and clinical relapses.
Research and practice implications
Treatment reintroduction due to virological and clinical relapse rates were 21%, 12%, and 17% at 24, 48 and 96 weeks, respectively.

乙型肝炎病毒和乙型肝炎病毒/丁型肝炎病毒检测:意大利当前状况与展望

Testing for hepatitis C virus and hepatitis B virus/hepatitis D virus: Current scenarios and perspectives in Italy
作者： Francesca Colapietro, Maurizia Rossana Brunetto, Nicola Coppola, Stefano Fagiuoli, Pietro Lampertico
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people.
证据基础与方法
本专家意见审查总结了意大利三级中心积极从事 HCV、HBV 和 HDV 医院检测的经验,并确定了改进检测和与护理联系的实际途径。
共识与结论
在意大利,丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 筛查受益于专门的政府资助,而 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 检测则得到科学协会建议的支持,但并未受益于专门的国家公共卫生计划。
争议与缺口
世界卫生组织的目标是通过 疫苗接种 和筛查计划,到 2030 年将新发肝炎感染率减少 90%,死亡人数减少 65%。
科研与实践启示
本文提出了通过以下方式提高意大利病毒性肝炎管理有效性的建议:(1)加强医院单位之间的协调,整合全科医生和医院之间的信息系统;(2)实施集中报告制度和结构化转诊网络,确保检测结果呈阳性的患者及时得到适当的治疗;(3) 提供所需资源,以增加公共成瘾服务 治疗(Servizi per le Dipendenze)和监狱的检测;(4) 促进对高危人群,包括弱势群体和老年人群的筛查;(5) 开展全国病毒性肝炎宣传活动。

Testing for hepatitis C virus and hepatitis B virus/hepatitis D virus: Current scenarios and perspectives in Italy

Authors: Francesca Colapietro, Maurizia Rossana Brunetto, Nicola Coppola, Stefano Fagiuoli, Pietro Lampertico
Original abstract (English)
Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people. The World Health Organization aims to reduce new hepatitis infections by 90% and deaths by 65% by 2030 through vaccination and screening programs. In Italy, screening for hepatitis C virus (HCV) benefits from dedicated government funding, whereas testing for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) is supported by recommendations from scientific societies but does not benefit from dedicated national public health programmes. This expert-opinion review summarises the experience of tertiary-level Italian centres actively engaged in hospital-based testing for HCV, HBV, and HDV and identifies practical pathways to improve testing and linkage to care. This article presents proposals to improve the effectiveness of viral hepatitis management in Italy by: (1) Strengthening coordination between hospital units and integrating information systems between general practitioners and hospitals; (2) Implementing a centralised reporting system and a structured referral network to ensure that patients with positive test results receive appropriate therapies in a timely manner; (3) Providing the resources needed to increase testing in public addiction treatment services (Servizi per le Dipendenze) and prisons; (4) Promoting screening in high-risk populations, including vulnerable and older populations; and (5) Implementing a national awareness campaign on viral hepatitis. These measures could help Italy make concrete progress towards eliminating HCV infection and controlling HBV/HDV infection within the next decade.

Liver disease accounts for approximately 4% of all deaths worldwide, and viral hepatitis affects more than 300 million people.
Evidence base and method
This expert-opinion review summarises the experience of tertiary-level Italian centres actively engaged in hospital-based testing for HCV, HBV, and HDV and identifies practical pathways to improve testing and linkage to care.
Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
The World Health Organization aims to reduce new hepatitis infections by 90% and deaths by 65% by 2030 through vaccination and screening programs.

慢性乙型肝炎 中代谢功能障碍相关的脂肪肝和 抗病毒药物 反应:系统评价和荟萃分析

Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver and antiviral response in chronic hepatitis B: A systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Jia Liu, Peng Zhang, Ke-Zhen Hu, Zhi-Hui Zhao, Yong-Ning Xin, Yan-Zhen Bi
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述旨在评估 MAFLD 对 抗病毒药物 治疗 反应和 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者长期临床结果的影响。核心问题是 MAFLD 是否影响 CHB 患者对 抗病毒药物 治疗的病毒学、生化和临床反应。
证据基础与方法
本次系统评价和荟萃分析遵循 PRISMA 指南。证据基础包括来自 PubMed、Cochrane 图书馆、EMBASE 和 Web of Science 的截至 2025 年 11 月 30 日发表的研究。基于 抗病毒药物 方案和 MAFLD 诊断方法进行亚组分析,并进行留一敏感性分析以评估研究结果的稳健性。
共识与结论
MAFLD 与较低的病毒学和生化反应率、第 48 周时乙型肝炎表面抗原血清清除的几率较高(特别是在接受核苷(酸)类似物治疗的患者中)以及尽管接受 抗病毒药物 治疗但发生肝细胞癌(HCC)的几率增加有关。然而,在任何时间点均未观察到 MAFLD 与乙型肝炎 e 抗原血清清除之间存在显着关联。
争议与缺口
亚组分析表明,抗病毒药物 方案和 MAFLD 诊断方法部分解释了总体结果的异质性,特别是 乙型肝炎病毒 DNA 清除率和 HCC 方面。证据没有报告 MAFLD 与这些结果相关的具体机制,也没有报告 MAFLD 对其他临床结果(如肝纤维化进展)的影响。
科研与实践启示
研究结果支持将代谢风险评估纳入慢性乙型肝炎的管理,并强调需要对 MAFLD 和代谢功能障碍相关的脂肪性肝病进行标准化定义。

Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver and antiviral response in chronic hepatitis B: A systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Jia Liu, Peng Zhang, Ke-Zhen Hu, Zhi-Hui Zhao, Yong-Ning Xin, Yan-Zhen Bi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Metabolic dysfunction-associated fatty liver disease (MAFLD) frequently coexists with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). However, its association with antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with CHB remains unclear. AIM To systematically evaluate the impact of MAFLD on antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with CHB. METHODS This systematic review and meta-analysis was conducted in accordance with the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses guidelines. PubMed, the Cochrane Library, EMBASE, and Web of Science were searched for studies published up to November 30, 2025. Meta-analyses were performed to assess associations between MAFLD and antiviral treatment responses or clinical outcomes. Subgroup analyses were conducted according to antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach, and leave-one-out sensitivity analyses were performed to evaluate robustness. RESULTS Thirty-two studies were included, of which 20 evaluated antiviral treatment efficacy and 13 assessed clinical outcomes in patients with CHB and concomitant MAFLD (one study reported both treatment responses and clinical outcomes). MAFLD was associated with a higher rate of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at 48 weeks [odds ratio (OR) = 1.41, 95%CI: 1.28-1.55], although this association was attenuated at 96 weeks (OR = 1.44, 95%CI: 0.96-2.16). No significant association was observed between MAFLD and hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance at any time-point. MAFLD was consistently associated with lower rates of alanine aminotransferase normalization and hepatitis B virus DNA clearance throughout the treatment duration. MAFLD was associated with a significantly increased risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) despite antiviral therapy (OR = 1.50, 95%CI: 1.01-2.24). Subgroup analyses according to the antiviral regimen revealed distinct patterns, partly explaining the heterogeneity in the overall analyses. Subgroup analyses suggested that the antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach partly explained the heterogeneity, particularly for hepatitis B virus DNA clearance and HCC. CONCLUSION MAFLD was associated with lower virological and biochemical response rates, higher odds of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at week 48, particularly among patients receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, and higher odds of HCC in patients with CHB, but not with hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance. These findings support metabolic risk assessment in CHB management and highlight the need for standardized MAFLD/metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease definitions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review aims to evaluate the impact of MAFLD on antiviral treatment responses and long-term clinical outcomes in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The central question is whether MAFLD influences virological, biochemical, and clinical responses to antiviral therapy in CHB.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis followed the PRISMA guidelines. The evidence base included studies from PubMed, the Cochrane Library, EMBASE, and Web of Science, published up to November 30, 2025. Subgroup analyses were conducted based on antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach, and leave-one-out sensitivity analyses were performed to assess the robustness of findings.
Consensus and conclusions
MAFLD was associated with lower virological and biochemical response rates, higher odds of hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance at week 48, particularly among patients receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, and increased odds of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) despite antiviral therapy. However, no significant association was observed between MAFLD and hepatitis B e antigen seroclearance at any time-point.
Controversies and gaps
Subgroup analyses suggested that antiviral regimen and MAFLD diagnostic approach partly explained the heterogeneity in the overall findings, particularly for hepatitis B virus DNA clearance and HCC. The evidence does not report on the specific mechanisms linking MAFLD to these outcomes or the impact of MAFLD on other clinical outcomes such as liver fibrosis progression.
Research and practice implications
The findings support the integration of metabolic risk assessment in the management of CHB and highlight the need for standardized definitions of MAFLD and metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease.

人工智能驱动的乙型肝炎相关肝细胞癌的风险评估、早期检测、诊断特征和预后

Artificial intelligence-driven risk estimation, early detection, diagnostic characterisation and prognosis in hepatitis b-related hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Pranjal Kashiv, Manish Ramesh Balwani, Khushboo Saxena, Priyanka Tolani, Amit Pasari, Vivek B Kute
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要前因,但早期检测所依赖的 监测 框架在其两个承载点上受到限制:每两年一次的超声检查对早期肿瘤的适度敏感性,以及将非线性、随时间演变的过程压缩为一小组附加加权协变量的风险评分的有限区分度。人工智能 (AI) 已作为一种纠正措施而被提出,但该术语的应用很宽松,并且支持证据来自异质人群和经常混淆的临床任务。这篇叙述性综述评估了 HBV-HCC 途径中四个独立任务的 AI:慢性乙型肝炎 中的风险估计、监测 中的早期检测、已确定病变的诊断特征以及 诊断 后的预后。我们说明什么算作人工智能,什么不算作人工智能,描述搜索策略和选择原则,并根据其证据是乙型肝炎病毒特异性、混合病因学、丙型肝炎病毒衍生、来自一般肝癌人群还是仅限于复发和预后对每项引用的研究进行分类。将基于常规临床变量构建的机器学习模型与单次验证 队列研究 中的既定分数进行比较,他们可以更准确地进行区分。卷积网络改进了超声病变检测,血清学和无细胞DNA甲基化模型已应用于甲胎蛋白以外的早期检测,多组学整合已用于分子亚分类。这些发现基于主要是回顾性和单中心的文献,其中外部验证是例外,校准报告不一致,可解释性参差不齐,荟萃分析人员反复判断基础研究存在高偏倚风险。特别从乙型肝炎 队列研究 中提取的证据仍然是已发表作品的少数,并且没有研究表明人工智能引导的策略可以改善 诊断 的阶段或生存率。因此,前瞻性多中心验证、透明校准、遵守 TRIPOD+AI 和 PROBAST+AI 以及有意纳入非洲和南亚人群是先决条件,而不是改进。

Artificial intelligence-driven risk estimation, early detection, diagnostic characterisation and prognosis in hepatitis b-related hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Pranjal Kashiv, Manish Ramesh Balwani, Khushboo Saxena, Priyanka Tolani, Amit Pasari, Vivek B Kute
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains the leading global antecedent of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), yet the surveillance framework on which early detection depends is constrained at both of its load-bearing points: The modest sensitivity of biannual ultrasonography for early-stage tumours, and the bounded discrimination of risk scores that compress a nonlinear, time-evolving process into a small set of additively weighted covariates. Artificial intelligence (AI) has been advanced as a corrective, but the term is applied loosely and the supporting evidence is drawn from heterogeneous populations and from clinical tasks that are frequently conflated. This narrative review appraises AI across four separable tasks in the HBV-HCC pathway: Risk estimation in chronic hepatitis B, early detection during surveillance, diagnostic characterisation of established lesions, and prognosis after diagnosis. We state what is and is not counted as AI, describe the search strategy and selection principles, and categorise every cited study according to whether its evidence is HBV-specific, mixed-aetiology, hepatitis C virus-derived, drawn from general HCC populations, or confined to recurrence and prognosis. Where machine-learning models built on routine clinical variables have been compared with established scores within a single validation cohort, they have discriminated more accurately. Convolutional networks have improved ultrasonographic lesion detection, serological and cell-free DNA methylation models have been applied to early-stage detection beyond alpha-fetoprotein, and multi-omics integration has been used for molecular subclassification. These findings rest on a literature that is predominantly retrospective and single-centre, in which external validation is the exception, calibration is reported inconsistently, explainability is uneven, and meta-analysts repeatedly judge the underlying studies to be at high risk of bias. Evidence drawn specifically from hepatitis B cohorts remains a minority of the published work, and no study has demonstrated that an AI-guided strategy improves stage at diagnosis or survival. Prospective multicentre validation, transparent calibration, adherence to TRIPOD+AI and PROBAST+AI, and deliberate inclusion of African and South Asian populations are therefore prerequisites rather than refinements.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains the leading global antecedent of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), yet the surveillance framework on which early detection depends is constrained at both of its load-bearing points: The modest sensitivity of biannual ultrasonography for early-stage tumours, and the bounded discrimination of risk scores that compress a nonlinear, time-evolving process into a small set of additively weighted covariates.

Consensus and conclusions
Where machine-learning models built on routine clinical variables have been compared with established scores within a single validation cohort, they have discriminated more accurately. These findings rest on a literature that is predominantly retrospective and single-centre, in which external validation is the exception, calibration is reported inconsistently, explainability is uneven, and meta-analysts repeatedly judge the underlying studies to be at high risk of bias.

Prospective multicentre validation, transparent calibration, adherence to TRIPOD+AI and PROBAST+AI, and deliberate inclusion of African and South Asian populations are therefore prerequisites rather than refinements.

印度乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的 血清流行率 及其趋势:系统评价和荟萃分析

Seroprevalence of hepatitis B and hepatitis C and its trends in India: a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Vivekanandan Shanmugam, John W. Ward, Lindsey Hiebert, Lavanya Ayyasamy, Murali Krishnan Nambirajan, Vaishnavi Girija, Gomathi Pounraj, Jayalakshmi Masilamani, Vimal K, Priyanga Thirunavukkarasu
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这项系统评价和荟萃分析旨在根据现有证据综合印度的 HBV 和 HCV 血清流行率,并确定关键亚人群之间的差异。
证据基础与方法
A systematic search was conducted on PubMed, Embase, and Scopus for studies published from January 1999 to September 2024 reporting HBV and/or HCV seroprevalence in India. Following PRISMA guidelines, 87 studies (61 on HBV, 69 on HCV) were included. Given substantial heterogeneity across studies, pooled seroprevalence was estimated using a random-effects model.
共识与结论
HBV (HBsAg + )(n = 693,879 名参与者)的汇总 血清流行率 为 3.0% [95% CI 2.0–4.0%],抗 HCV 抗体(n = 1,079,706 名参与者)的 血清流行率 为 4.0% [95% CI: 2.0–7.0%]。HBV 患病率 的范围从献血者中的约 1.0% 到社区环境中的较高水平 (7.0%) 和高危人群 (4.0%)。从地理位置上看,HBV 患病率 在印度北部 (3.0%) 和西部 (3.0%) 最高。对于 HCV,患病率 在高危人群中显着较高 (11.0%)。
争议与缺口
不同研究之间观察到相当大的异质性(I 2 > 99%),反映了研究人群、地理区域和研究环境的差异。这些发现表明印度人群中病毒性肝炎的 流行病学 存在相当大的异质性。
科研与实践启示
扩大基于社区的筛查、分散分子检测和改善与护理的联系对于加强印度国家病毒性肝炎控制计划 (NVHCP) 和实现世界卫生组织 2030 年病毒性肝炎消除目标至关重要。

Seroprevalence of hepatitis B and hepatitis C and its trends in India: a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Vivekanandan Shanmugam, John W. Ward, Lindsey Hiebert, Lavanya Ayyasamy, Murali Krishnan Nambirajan, Vaishnavi Girija, Gomathi Pounraj, Jayalakshmi Masilamani, Vimal K, Priyanga Thirunavukkarasu
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B (HBV) and Hepatitis C (HCV) are major public health challenges in India. Yet, the existing prevalence studies differ significantly regarding population characteristics, geographic location, and study design, complicating the interpretation of the overall disease burden. This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to synthesize HBV and HCV seroprevalence in India from available evidence and identify variations across key subpopulations. A systematic search was conducted on PubMed, Embase, and Scopus for studies published from January 1999 to September 2024 reporting HBV and/or HCV seroprevalence in India. Following PRISMA guidelines, 87 studies (61 on HBV, 69 on HCV) were included. Given substantial heterogeneity across studies, pooled seroprevalence was estimated using a random-effects model. Subgroup analyses were conducted by publication year, study setting, population, and geographical region to explore variability. Eighty-seven studies reporting HBV or HCV seroprevalence in India were included. Considerable heterogeneity was observed across studies ( I 2 > 99%), reflecting differences in study populations, geographic regions, and study settings. The pooled seroprevalence was 3.0% [95% CI 2.0–4.0%] for HBV (HBsAg + ) ( n = 693,879 participants) and 4.0% [95% CI: 2.0–7.0%] for anti-HCV antibody ( n = 1,079,706 participants). However, substantial variation was observed across study populations. HBV prevalence ranged from approximately 1.0% among blood donors to higher levels in community-based settings (7.0%) and high-risk populations (4.0%). Geographically, HBV prevalence was highest in Northern (3.0%) and Western (3.0%) India. For HCV, prevalence was markedly higher among high-risk populations (11.0%). Regional analysis showed the highest HCV seroprevalence in Eastern India (10.0%) and Northern India (6.0%). Molecular confirmatory tests such as reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR), HBV DNA, or HCV RNA detection were reported in 19 of the 87 included studies (21.8%); chemiluminescent microparticle immunoassay (CMIA) was classified as a serological immunoassay rather than a molecular method. These findings indicate considerable heterogeneity in the epidemiology of viral hepatitis across populations in India. This study highlights the persistent burden of HBV and HCV in India. Scaling up community-based screening, decentralizing molecular testing, and improving linkage-to-care are essential to strengthen India’s National Viral Hepatitis Control Programme (NVHCP) and to achieve the WHO 2030 Viral Hepatitis elimination targets. Systematic review registration: PROSPERO CRD42024576147
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to synthesize HBV and HCV seroprevalence in India from available evidence and identify variations across key subpopulations.
Evidence base and method
A systematic search was conducted on PubMed, Embase, and Scopus for studies published from January 1999 to September 2024 reporting HBV and/or HCV seroprevalence in India. Following PRISMA guidelines, 87 studies (61 on HBV, 69 on HCV) were included. Given substantial heterogeneity across studies, pooled seroprevalence was estimated using a random-effects model.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled seroprevalence was 3.0% [95% CI 2.0–4.0%] for HBV (HBsAg + ) ( n = 693,879 participants) and 4.0% [95% CI: 2.0–7.0%] for anti-HCV antibody ( n = 1,079,706 participants). HBV prevalence ranged from approximately 1.0% among blood donors to higher levels in community-based settings (7.0%) and high-risk populations (4.0%). Geographically, HBV prevalence was highest in Northern (3.0%) and Western (3.0%) India. For HCV, prevalence was markedly higher among high-risk populations (11.0%).
Controversies and gaps
Considerable heterogeneity was observed across studies ( I 2 > 99%), reflecting differences in study populations, geographic regions, and study settings. These findings indicate considerable heterogeneity in the epidemiology of viral hepatitis across populations in India.
Research and practice implications
Scaling up community-based screening, decentralizing molecular testing, and improving linkage-to-care are essential to strengthen India’s National Viral Hepatitis Control Programme (NVHCP) and to achieve the WHO 2030 Viral Hepatitis elimination targets.
